# Creating a Jujutsu Sorcerer

![Three Jujutsu Sorcerers](https://i.ibb.co/q7HcB6N/3-way.png)


### Quick Build
You can make a jujutsu sorcerer quickly by following these suggestions. First, put your highest ability score in Dexterity, followed by Charisma. Second, choose the Sorcerer Student, Sorcerer Family, Curse User, or the New Shadow Style Student background. Third, choose the Cursed Spirit, Cursed Vessel, Death Painting, or Human race.

## Jujutsu Sorcerer Class Features
As a Jujutsu Sorcerer you gain the following class features.

### Hit Points
Hit Dice: 1d8 per Jujutsu Sorcerer level
Hit Points at 1st Level: 8 + Constitution modifier
Hit Points at Higher Levels: 1d8 (or 5) + Constitution modifier per Jujutsu Sorcerer level after 1st

### Proficiencies
Armor: Light Armor, Medium Armor, Shields
Weapons: Simple Weapons, Martial Weapons
Tools: Choose one type of artisan's tools or one musical Instrument
Saving Throws: Strength or Dexterity and Charisma
Skills: Choose two from Acrobatics, Arcana, Athletics, History, Perception, Religion, Intimidation, and Stealth

### Equipment
You start with the following equipment, in addition to the equipment granted by your background:

- (a) Dungeoneer's pack or (b) Explorer's pack
- (a) 1 Handgun and 20 rounds or (b) 1 Katana
- (a) 1 Simple Weapon or (b) 1 Martial Weapon
If you are using starting wealth, you have 5d4x10 gp in funds.


| Level | Proficiency Bonus | Features | Cursed Energy | Martial Arts |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | +2 |Cursed Technique, Cursed Energy, Unarmored Movement, Unarmored Defense | 2 + cha |  - |
| 2 | +2 |Martial Arts, Cursed Energy Enhancement, Cursed Martial Arts | 4 + cha | 1d4 |
| 3 | +2 |Jujutsu Schools, Sorcerer Training, Cursed Technique feature | 6 + cha | 1d4 |
| 4 | +2 | Ability Score Improvement | 8 + cha | 1d6 |
| 5 | +3 | Extra Attack, Binding Vow | 10 + cha |  1d6 |
| 6 | +3 |Curse-Empowered Strikes, Cursed Technique feature | 12 + cha | 1d6 |
| 7 | +3 |Advanced Studies, Advanced Sorcerer Training, Hollow Wicker Basket | 14 + cha | 1d6 |
| 8 | +3 | Ability Score Improvement | 16 + cha | 1d6 |
| 9 | +4 | Cursed Energy Recovery | 18 + cha | 1d8 |
| 10 | +4 | Cursed Technique feature | 20 + cha | 1d8 |
| 11 | +4 | Advanced Energy Usage, Efficiency Fundamentals | 22 + cha | 1d8 |
| 12 | +4 | Ability Score Improvement  | 24 + cha | 1d8 |
| 13 | +5 |High Output Sorcery, Exorcise | 26 + cha | 1d10 |
| 14 | +5 |School Graduation | 28 + cha  | 1d10 |
| 15 | +5 |Ability Score Improvement | 30 + cha  | 1d10 |
| 16 | +5 | Ability Score Improvement | 32 + cha | 1d10 |
| 17 | +6 | Near The Pinnacle, Extra Attack(2) | 34 + cha | 1d12 |
| 18 | +6 | Ability Score Improvement | 36 + cha  | 1d12 |
| 19 | +6 | Ability Score Improvement  | 38 + cha | 1d12 |
| 20 | +6 | Cursed Technique feature | 40 + cha  | 1d12 |


## Cursed Technique
Starting at 1st Level, you were born with a **cursed technique**. Cursed techniques are unique abilities congenitally engraved into the user's body, specifically in the brain's right prefrontal cortex. Most jujutsu sorcerers are born with an innate technique that manifests around the age of five or six, continually carving its formula into the user over time. You must choose one innate technique, all detailed under Cursed Techniques. Your choice grants you features at 1st, 3rd, 6th, 10th, and 20th Levels.

### Cursed Techniques
Every innate technique grants you a cursed technique with many different uses.

*Techniques typically require somatic and verbal components to cast, represented as S or V followed by a number. For S, the number represents the number of free hands necessary; for example, something with S-1 requires one hand, and V represents the number of free mouths required. When a technique's action cost is reduced to a free action or lower, you may reduce its S by 1, and at the DM's discretion, you may lower a technique's V by 1 at the cost of increasing S by 2. If a technique requires an integral component (such as speech, touch, or another essential medium), its S or V  cannot be reduced below 1. Some techniques require you to hold a sword or object, and these are included in the S-2 or S-1 requirement without impeding on it.
*If you possess more than one Innate Technique, only one can be used at a time. If you are actively using one of your cursed techniques, such as a summoned shikigami, a duration-based ability (e.g., Limitless' one-minute Infinity or Hakuna Laana's one-minute dance), or any other ongoing effect tied to that technique, activating a different cursed technique will immediately end all effects of the currently active technique. Some passive effects of a technique will remain, such as absorbed spirits from the Cursed Spirit Manipulation technique. Work with your DM to determine which effects would be dispelled and which effects would remain passively. Imbuing a cursed technique into a Domain Expansion does not count towards using more than one innate technique.

### Cursed Energy
Your training has allowed you to control the cursed energy within yourself. cha is your cursed energy modifier for your cursed energy fueled features. A number of cursed energy points represent your access to this energy. You have a number of cursed energy points equal to twice your Jujutsu sorcerer Level + your cha modifier, as shown in the cursed energy column of the Jujutsu sorcerer table. You can spend these points to fuel various Jujutsu sorcerer class features, including your cursed techniques, which are the features you gain from your innate technique.

When you spend cursed energy, it becomes unavailable until you finish a long rest; at the end, you regain all spent cursed energy.

Some cursed energy applications require their target to make a saving throw to resist the application's effect. The saving throw DC is calculated as follows:

**Cursed Energy save DC = 8 + proficiency bonus + your cha modifier.**

**Cursed Energy attack roll = proficiency bonus + your cha modifier.**

Your own cursed energy deals half as much damage to yourself.

## Alternate Feature: Sorcerer Path
Starting at 1st Level, some sorcerers are not lucky and are born without an innate technique. However, this hasn't stopped them from pursuing their ambitions as sorcerers, and so they adapted. You must choose a sorcerer path listed below in the Sorcerer Paths section. Your choice grants you features at 1st, 3rd, 6th, 10th, and 20th Levels.


## Unarmored Defense
Beginning at 1st Level, while wearing no armor and not wielding a shield, your AC equals 10 + your dex or str modifier + your cha modifier.

## Martial Arts
At 2nd Level, your martial arts practice gives you mastery of combat styles that use unarmed strikes. You gain the following benefits while you aren't wearing armor or wielding a shield:

- You can use dex instead of str for your unarmed strikes' attack and damage rolls.
- You can roll a d4 in place of the normal damage of your unarmed strike. The damage die increases as you gain Jujutsu Sorcerer Levels, as shown in the Martial Arts column of the Jujutsu Sorcerer table.
- When you use the Attack action with melee weapon attack on your turn, you can make one melee weapon attack as a Bonus Action.
- You gain access to Sorcerer Weapons. You may use your martial arts damage die instead of the sorcerer weapon damage die if you choose to.

## Cursed Energy Enhancement
At 2nd Level, you began to focus your cursed energy to manipulate it better. You learn the following enhancements at the Novice tier as shown below:

### Cursed Strike
Before you make an unarmed strike, you may spend up to your proficiency bonus in cursed energy to empower your next strike. This enhancement lasts until you hit a creature with your unarmed strike or until a minute passes. You deal additional force damage equal to 1d6 per point spent on a hit. You can also use this in response to rolling a natural 20 on an attack roll with an unarmed strike or landing a black flash instead of beforehand. You can only use this enhancement again after it has ended. 

You can use this feature a number of times per round equal to your proficiency bonus.

### Cursed Armor
On your or another creature's turn, and for an amount of cursed energy up to your cha modifier (no action required), you can surround yourself with cursed energy for one minute. You gain temporary hit points equal to the amount of cursed energy spent times 5 for the duration. You can only benefit from this feature again after 1 minute or having your temporary hit points reduced to 0. You cannot use this enhancement if you cannot take actions due to a condition or effect. You may clear your cursed armor as a free action if you are not directly participating in combat (as determined by your dm).

### Cursed Weapon Enhancement
As a bonus action, you can spend cursed energy up to your proficiency bonus to add to a melee, ranged or improvised weapon you are wielding for 1 minute. Whenever you hit a target with the weapon, it takes extra force damage equal to the amount spent. You can only use this enhancement again after 1 minute has passed. Other creatures cannot use your Cursed Weapon Enhancement, and must use their own. The duration remains while you are not wielding the weapon, in case you are going to wield it again. As a free action you may clear your Cursed Weapon Enhancement on a weapon you are wielding.

When using Cursed Weapon Enhancement on a ranged weapon, its projectiles deal extra force damage equal to the amount spent.

In addition, if you do this 25 times or more to the same weapon, it becomes a Semi-Cursed Tool as long as it's not already a cursed tool. An imbuement only counts towards this total if it lasts the entire duration of Cursed Weapon Enhancement.

### Higher Tiers
As you progress as a Sorcerer, your Cursed Energy Enhancement skill increases. There are 2 tiers above Novice, Advanced, and Expert, with the below effect for each enhancement you have at that tier.

* *Advanced Cursed Strikes.* The dice of your cursed strikes increases to a d8.
* *Advanced Cursed Armor.* The temporary hit points gained per cursed energy spent increases to 10.
* *Advanced Cursed Weapon Enhancement.* The amount of force damage added increases to twice the amount spent.

* *Expert Cursed Strikes.* The dice of your cursed strikes increases to a d10.
* *Expert Cursed Armor.* The temporary hit points gained per cursed energy spent increases to 15.
* *Expert Cursed Weapon Enhancement.* The amount of force damage added increases to three times the amount spent. In addition, you can now use Cursed Weapon Enhancement as a free action.

At 10th level all of your enhancements have their tier increased by one, from novice to advanced, or advanced to expert.

## Cursed Martial Arts
At 2nd Level, your Jujutsu training has taught you advanced martial techniques. You can now do any of the following Cursed Martial Arts:

### Cursed Blast of Blows
Immediately after taking the Attack action on your turn, you can spend 2 cursed energy to perform two unarmed strikes as a bonus action.

### Cursed Patient Defense
You can spend 2 cursed energy to take the Dodge action as a bonus action on your turn.

### Cursed Wind Step
You can spend 2 cursed energy to take the Disengage or Dash action as a bonus action on your turn, and your jump distance doubles until the end of your turn.

## Jujutsu Schools
Starting at 3rd Level, you have begun to learn from a jujutsu school or teacher how to improve your cursed energy. Choose between Osaka Jujutsu High, Kyoto Jujutsu High, Ainu Jujutsu High, and Sendai Jujutsu High.

### Osaka Jujutsu High

> **You came from a more liberal school, where you decided not to focus on the basics but on improving your Innate Technique.**



You learn three enhancements to apply to one of your Lapse, Extension, Maximum, and Reversal techniques. A technique cannot have multiple enhancements, nor can one enhancement be applied to multiple techniques.

- **Power Enhancement.** *You can now spend additional cursed energy up to your proficiency bonus on the technique to add an additional damage die per additional cursed energy spent to the total damage. For example, if you have a proficiency bonus of 5 and apply this to a feature that deals 5d10 damage, it would now deal 10d10, or if you applied this to a feature that in one attack deals 7d10 and 7d12 you would distribute the dice increase between the two sets as you please. It could now do 7d10 and 12d12, 9d10 and 10d12, 12d10 and 7d12, or any other combination that totals to an increase in dice equaling your proficiency bonus.*

- **Cursed Energy Economy.** *The cursed energy cost of the technique is now reduced by half your proficiency bonus (rounded up, to a minimum of 1 cursed energy).*

- **Distance Enhancement.** *'You can spend up to your proficiency bonus in additional cursed energy on the technique to add 10 ft. to the range for each additional cursed energy spent unless it's touch range. If the technique affects an area, it instead increases the size of it by 5 ft. per cursed energy spent. If it is a line, you pick to make it longer or wider.'*

When an enhancement is applied to a Lapse technique, it will also apply to any further Technique Improvements related directly to it. You may reallocate your enhancements after a short or long rest.


### Kyoto Jujutsu High

> **You have come from a very conservative school, where you learned to ignore your individuality as a jujutsu sorcerer and instead focus on mastering the basic Jujutsu arts better than any other.**



Your **Cursed Martial Arts** techniques each now only cost 1 cursed energy to perform, and you may increase one **Cursed Energy Enhancement** by a tier. In addition, one of your cursed martial arts is more refined than the average sorcerer's:

- **Cursed Blast of Blows.** *When you use this feature, you can now make three unarmed strikes instead of two.*

- **Cursed Patient Defense.** *You gain advantage on con and str saving throws when you use the Dodge action granted by this feature, as long as you benefit from the Dodge.*

- **Cursed Wind Step.** *When you take the Dash action granted by this feature, you instead gain extra movement equal to twice your speed. Additionally, when you take the Disengage action granted by this feature, you gain extra movement equal to your speed for the current turn.*

You gain one additional refined martial art at 6th and another at 10th Level.


### Ainu Jujutsu High

> **You have come from a school up north. This school values barrier techniques over all others and teaches you to use them well.**



You learn three enhancements that you can apply to your barrier techniques. You can apply these enhancements to basic barriers, curtains, simple domains, etc. This barrier can not be a domain barrier. Each barrier technique cannot have multiple enhancements, nor can one enhancement be applied to multiple barriers.

- **Health Enhancement.** *You can now spend additional cursed energy up to your proficiency bonus on the technique to add an additional 5 hit points to the barrier per cursed energy spent.*

- **Cursed Energy Economy.** *The cursed energy cost of the barrier is now reduced by your proficiency bonus (to a minimum of 1 cursed energy).*

- **Distance Enhancement.** *You can spend up to your proficiency bonus in additional cursed energy on the barrier to add 10 ft. to the radius for each additional cursed energy spent.*

You may reallocate your enhancements after a short or long rest.


### Sendai Jujutsu High

> **You have come from a school that values defensive techniques. As a result, you are naturally more evasive and durable than students from other schools.**


Your **Cursed Martial Arts** techniques now only cost 1 cursed energy, and you may increase one **Cursed Energy Enhancement** by a tier. In addition, you gain one of the following benefits: 

- **Durable Body.** You gain a damage reduction equal to your con modifier. 

- **Cursed Patient Defense.** *When you use the Dodge action granted by this feature, you gain advantage on cha saving throws that aren't self-inflicted, as long as you benefit from the Dodge. Additionally, you keep the benefits from this Dodge action even if your speed drops to 0, however you still must be able to otherwise move to gain its benefits.*

- **Cursed Fortitude.** You have advantage on ability checks made to resist being grappled or knocked prone as a result of a Grapple or Shove.

You gain one additional benefit at 6th and another at 10th Level.


## Alternate Feature: Straightforward Improvement
*Replaces Jujutsu School's Osaka Benefits*

At 3rd Level, if you have a **Sorcerer Path** rather than a **Cursed Technique** alongside selecting **Osaka Jujutsu High** as your Jujutsu School, your focus has been on improving your traits.

You gain the following benefits dependent on your **Sorcerer Path**:

- **Taijutsu Master.** *Your unarmed strikes gain a bonus to their attack and damage rolls. This bonus is half of your proficiency bonus, rounded down.*

- **Cursed Energy Manipulator.** *Once again, you may increase the tier of one of your Cursed Energy Enhancements. (From Novice to Advanced, or Advanced to Expert).*

- **Cursed Corpse Expert.** *You now add half your proficiency bonus (rounded down) in additional modifications for every Cursed Corpse you make.*

- **Positive Energy Master.** *You can take the Stabilize action as a free or bonus action once per turn. Lastly, whenever you stabilize a creature, you can make them regain 1 hit point. This does not work on  Cursed Spirits.*

- **Weapon Master.** *You can choose a second option from the **Fighting Style** class feature.*

- **New Shadow Style Master.** *When reducing the radius of your **New Shadow Style: Simple Domain** to only take up your own space, you increase the effects of **Technique Dampening** by half your proficiency bonus, rounded down. Additionally the refinement cap is 130 instead of 125.*

- **Shikigami Master.** *The distance you can summon your shikigami is now 120ft.*

- **Barrier Master.** *The amount of cursed energy you can spend into a barrier further increases by half of your cha modifier, rounded down.*

## Sorcerer Training
At 3rd Level, you have been focusing on improving yourself as a sorcerer, trying to focus on your strength. You will gain one of the following feats that you meet the requirements of (you may ignore one prerequisite when picking a body feat): 

### Body Feats
**Athlete History**, **50 Meters In 3 Seconds!**, or **Resilient**

### Taijutsu Feats
**Taijutsu Sorcery**, **Slam Master**, or **Acrobatic Fighter**

### Barrier Technique Feats
**Basic Barrier**

### Cursed Energy Feats
**Immense Cursed Energy**

### Cursed Energy Manipulation Feats
**Cursed Energy Manipulator**, or **Advanced Energy Usage**

### Cursed Creations Feats
**Shikigami Summon**

## Ability Score Improvement
When you reach 4th Level, and again at 8th, 12th, 15th, 16th, 18th, and 19th Level, you can increase one ability score of your choice by 2 or two ability scores of your choice by 1. As usual, using this feature, you can't increase an ability score above 20.
You may also ignore this increase to gain a **Cursed Feat** that you meet the prerequisite of.

## Extra Attack
Starting at 5th Level, you can attack twice, instead of once, when you take the Attack action during your turn. The number of attacks increases to three when you reach 17th Level.

## Binding Vow
Starting at 5th Level, you can create a binding vow with a willing creature or yourself, entering into a strict contract formed by Jujutsu. At any point, you can create one binding vow and choose one of the following vows listed in the Binding Vows. During a combat encounter, once per round, you can make a Binding Vow (no action required). You can have a maximum number of binding vows equal to 1 + your int modifier, a minimum of 2 at a time. During a Short or Long rest, you can end a number of Binding Vows equal to your int modifier (minimum of 1).


> **Design Note:** Because binding vows are free in player creativity, many custom vows can be inherently unbalanced without the DM knowing it. Because of this, we have created a list of binding vow rules so the DM knows what to look out for and so the players know what to build their vows more fairly. See the Binding Vow Rules.

## Curse-Empowered Strikes
At 6th Level, you have begun focusing more cursed energy into your strikes or weapon. Your attacks with weapons or unarmed strikes, your choice when you gain this feature, now count as magical for the purposes of overcoming resistances and immunities to damage from nonmagical attacks. Depending on your cha modifier, you may add an extra force damage die to the total damage of your choice.

| Jujutsu Density | Damage Dice |
| --- | --- |
| If you have a +1 in cha | d4 |
| If you have a +2 in cha | d6 |
| If you have a +3 in cha | d8 |
| If you have a +4 in cha | d10 |
| If you have a +5 in cha | d12 |

At 11th Level, the attacks with the option you haven't chosen at 6th Level are now considered magical and will deal the additional die.

## Advanced Studies
Starting at 7th Level, your talents have been refined by the teachings of your Jujutsu High. You receive one of the following depending on which school you chose:

### Osaka Jujutsu High

> **You learn three more enhancements to apply to one of your Lapse, Extension, Maximum, and Reversal techniques. A technique cannot have multiple enhancements, nor can one enhancement be applied to multiple techniques. You are able to pair one enhancement from this feature with one from the 3rd-level Osaka school feature. These enhancements are as follows:**


- **Increased Damage Output.** Its total damage dice number will increase by your cha modifier. For example, if you have a Cha modifier of 5 and apply this to a feature that deals 5d10 damage, it would now deal 10d10, or if you applied this to a feature that in one attack deals 7d10 and 7d12 you would distribute the dice increase between the two sets as you please. It could now do 7d10 and 12d12, 9d10 and 10d12, 12d10 and 7d12, or any other combination that totals to an increase in dice equaling your Cha modifier.
- **Increased Reach.** Its range will increase by 30ft unless it's touch range. If the technique affects an area, the size instead increases by 15ft. If it is a line, you may choose to make it longer or wider.
- **Optimized Technique.** The cursed energy cost of the technique is now reduced by half your proficiency bonus (rounded down, to a minimum of 1 cursed energy).
When a Lapse technique has one of these enhancements added to it, the benefits will also apply to any further Technique Improvements related directly to it. You may change the placement of these enhancements whenever you gain an ability score increase in this class or when you reach Level 20 in this class.


### Kyoto Jujutsu High

> **You have been improving your cursed energy usage, striving to master the basics of all aspects of Jujutsu and become an exemplary sorcerer. You may increase one **Cursed Energy Enhancement** by a tier and get the following **stronger enhancements**: **


- **Stronger Cursed Strikes.** Creatures of your choice that are not the initial target, within 5 feet of the impact, take a number of d12 of the same damage type equal to half of the cursed energy spent (rounded down). Objects take twice the damage from this effect.
- **Aggressive Cursed Armor.** While your armor is active, your melee weapon attacks will deal an additional amount of damage equal to your Proficiency bonus.
- **Precise Cursed Weapon.** Half the cursed energy spent (rounded down) will add to the weapon's attack rolls.



### Ainu Jujutsu High

> **You have a favorite barrier, which you have enhanced to its maximum potential. This barrier can not be a domain barrier. This barrier has become your **specialized barrier**. One of your barriers will gain all of the following benefits:**


- **Increased Durability.** Its hit points will increase by an amount equal to 10 times your cha modifier.
- **Increased Radius.** Its radius will increase by 30ft unless it's touch range.
- **Optimized Usage.** Its cursed energy cost will be halved (rounded up, minimum 1).
You may change your **specialized barrier** whenever you gain an ability score increase in this class or when you reach Level 20 in this class.


### Sendai Jujutsu High

> **You have been improving your cursed energy usage, striving to master the basics of all aspects of Jujutsu and become an exemplary sorcerer. You may increase one **Cursed Energy Enhancement** by a tier and the following **stronger enhancements**:**


- **Defensive Cursed Strikes.** As a reaction when you take damage, you may spend cursed energy as if you used cursed strike. You then reduce the damage you take by how much your cursed strike would've dealt. This does not count towards your limited use of cursed strike per round.
- **Durable Cursed Armor.** Your cursed armor now takes half as much damage from non-magical sources, and grants you damage reduction equal to your cha modifier.
- **Cursed Weapon Deflection.** As a reaction to taking damage from a weapon attack roll, whilst holding a melee weapon, you may spend cursed energy as if you used **Cursed Weapon Enhancement**, to deflect the attack. You reduce the damage of the attack by the base damage of your weapon + your **Cursed Weapon Enhancement** damage. This does not activate **Cursed Weapon Enhancement** on the weapon, and may be used on a weapon that already has **Cursed Weapon Enhancement** active.


Instead of picking one of the above options, you may pick an additional feat from Advanced Sorcerer Training.

## Advanced Sorcerer Training
At 7th Level, you have furthered your focus on improving the strengths you have already been developing.

You gain one of the following feats that you meet the requirements of but excluding their Level requirements, if any:

### Body Feats
**Athlete History**, **Colossal Physique**, **50 Meters In 3 Seconds!**, **Untraceable Speed**, **Strong Body**, **Human Wall** or **Resilient**.

### Taijutsu Feats
**Slam Master**, **Acrobatic Fighter**, or **Cursed Martial Artist**

### Taijutsu & Weapon Feats
**Taijutsu Sorcery**

### Weapon Feats
**Cursed Weapon Wielder**

### Barrier Technique Feats
**Basic Barrier**, **Barrier Expert**, or **New Shadow Style: Simple Domain**

### Cursed Energy Feats
**Immense Cursed Energy**, or **Overflowing Cursed Energy**

### Cursed Energy Manipulation Feats
**Cursed Energy Manipulator**, **Advanced Energy Usage**,  **Cursed Energy Slash**, **Cursed Energy Ray** or **Cursed Energy Explosion**

### Cursed Creations Feats
**Shikigami Summon** or **Cursed Blacksmith**

### Binding Vow Feats
**Vows Master**

## Hollow Wicker Basket

At 7th level, you learn Hollow Wicker Basket, a technique that predates New Shadow Style's Simple Domain and was most popular during the Golden Age of Sorcery, the Heian Era.

As an action, bonus action, or reaction (S-2, V-1) when you are affected by a Domain Expansion's sure-hit effect or see a Domain Expansion open, you can spend 5 Cursed Energy to surround yourself with a barrier that occupies only your space for 1 minute. Within the barrier, the domain's sure-hit feature and sure-hit effect are negated, and one creature of your choice inside the barrier is unaffected.

The barrier has hit points equal to 50 times half your proficiency bonus, rounded down. At the start of your turn inside a domain, it takes 3d12 force damage per 100 refinement the domain has. If the barrier disappears or breaks, anyone it protected, including you, is immediately struck by the domain's sure-hit effect if they are still inside one.

The barrier has no effect on cursed techniques or other creatures entering it; it only negates sure-hit features and effects.

When you activate this feature, or as a free action while it is active, you may choose to maintain the barrier continuously. This requires concentration, but the barrier no longer takes damage at the start of your turn. You must have two free hands, which remain occupied while concentrating, and you must continuously chant. You cannot activate anything that requires you to speak while concentrating unless you have another way to speak, such as a second mouth. If you fail to meet either requirement, concentration ends and the barrier is immediately affected by the sure-hit effect.
## Cursed Energy Recovery
At 9th Level, due to extended periods of use, your cursed energy constantly recovers itself from the adrenaline of battle. While in combat, you regain 1 cursed energy at the beginning of your turn, up to your maximum.
The combat must be one where your life is at risk.

## Advanced Energy Usage
At 11th Level, you have learned some more basic principles any sorcerer should apply, whether in battle or investigation. You will gain two of the following Cursed Energy Uses:


 |+ style="text-align: left;" |



### Cursed Energy Tracker
You have increased your skill in Tracking remains of cursed energy as well as active cursed energy.

*You have learned how to track down remains of cursed energy. You may make a wis (perception) check, adding your cha modifier, to see if there are any remains of cursed energy in the place you're at, and if you succeed you know exactly who the energy is from. In case you do not know who the person is, then you can only track down where the cursed energy trail leads you to. The DC is equal to the cursed energy DC of the creature you're trying to track. Tracking residual cursed energy may not always lead all the way to the person, with your DM determining how the residual trail goes. Usage of a cursed technique or cursed energy fueled feature will often leave much more residuals than normal.
*You have learned to read the flow of other's cursed energy. You know when any creature you can see spends cursed energy and how much they spend when doing so. Additionally, if you possess proficiency in wis (Perception), as a free action you can focus on a creature for 1 minute, seeing the sparks of cursed energy that always happen before cursed technique usage. For that minute when that creature forces you to make a save using their cursed technique or makes a cursed energy attack roll against you using their cursed technique they must roll cha(Stealth) against your wis (Perception). If you win you get advantage on the saving throw, or the attack roll is made with disadvantage. You can focus on a number of creatures with this feature at once equal to half of your proficiency bonus, rounded down.

### Cursed Energy Concealer
You have become more skilled at hiding and retaining your cursed energy passively.

*You have become adept at hiding your presence and Cursed Energy. You may now take the Hide action as a bonus action. Anytime you take the Hide action, you can attempt to conceal your cursed energy by making a dex (stealth) check. For the next 10 minutes, wherever you pass through you will not leave your cursed energy tracks behind carelessly, so any person that tries to track or sense your remains will need to be successful on a contested check against what you rolled + your cha modifier, if they have any ability or feature that would allow them to track your energy, and if not they can't track you at all.
*While both you and your Cursed Energy are hidden, the benefit of being an Unseen Attacker also extends to your cursed energy saving throws, forcing any creatures affected to roll their saving throw with disadvantage.

### Negative Flow
Understanding that the core of cursed energy is negative emotions you've now grown to synthesize your own cursed energy when distressed. Whenever you are under the effects of the conditions frightened, berserk, bloodlusted, are under half your maximum hit points, missing a limb lost in this encounter; once per round you can use your free action at the beginning of your turn to gain an amount of temporary cursed energy equal to half of your Cha modifier (maximum of 3) at the start of your turn which cannot go above 1/4th of your cursed energy maximum. The DM can determine that an encounter is not suitable for Negative Flow to be able to be used.

### Focused Rage
Deepening your knowledge on the creation of cursed energy you can now strengthen your resolve to focus your negative thoughts into more power. During your turn, while using Negative Flow and being under any of the circumstances listed in the feature, you can begin concentrating as a bonus action to increase the temporary cursed energy gained at the start of your turn to your full Cha modifier (maximum of 5 cursed energy). While concentrating on this feature, you no longer need to use a free action to gain the cursed energy from Negative Flow. When the circumstances listed end you can no longer concentrate on this feature. (This feature can be concentrated on even while under the bloodlust or berserk conditions)

### Curse Enhanced Senses
Cursed energy can increase your physical capabilities, but can also finely tune your senses. You've learned how to enhance them to a higher degree than normal.

As a bonus action for 4 cursed energy, you can finely hone your five senses, allowing you to add half of your cha modifier to wis (Perception) checks for one minute. If you have the Cursed Energy Reinforcement feat, this will instead be your full cha modifier. While this feature is active, you must use a bonus action at the beginning of your turns to keep it active. Outside of combat, this feature will last one hour instead. You may end this feature at will. You can only use this feature again once it has ended. 

### Object Infusion
You've become more adept at imbuing cursed energy not just into special tools, but mundane objects for crafty use.

*The amount of cursed energy spent on infusing the objects is based on the size of the object itself. You are able to infuse a nonmagical tiny object that fits into your hand for almost no cursed energy as a free action. After you infuse 10 of these tiny objects per long rest, it will cost 1 cursed energy. For example, infusing 9 pebbles costs nothing, infusing 10 will then cost 1 cursed energy, infusing 15 will cost none after the initial 1 spent at 10, and infusing 20 will cost another 1 for a total of 2 spent. For nonmagical objects above tiny, the action cost and energy cost are as follows: 


| Size | Action Cost | Cursed Energy Cost |

| Small | Bonus Action | 2 Cursed Energy |

| Medium | Action | 4 Cursed Energy |

| Large | Full Turn Action | 8 Cursed Energy           |

| Huge | 2 Full Turn Actions | 16 Cursed Energy    |

| Gargantuan | 4 Full Turn Actions | 32 Cursed Energy         |

*An object stays imbued for 1 minute. For the duration, it counts as an improvised weapon and has double the hit points it had before it was infused. If you want to throw a tiny object you have imbued, the normal range is 10 feet times your str modifier or dex modifier, and the long range is double the normal range. Additionally, you can expend a lot of cursed energy to cause the object to become volatile. When imbuing an object, you can instead spend double the normal amount of cursed energy to make the object reactive. If used on a tiny object, it costs 1 cursed energy per object. An object imbued in this way will explode when disturbed, like if it is moved or attacked (determined by the dm). Tiny, small, and medium objects heavily obscure an area equal to their size when they explode for 1 round. Large, Huge, and Gargantuan objects deal damage when they explode. With the damage being 4d6 force, 8d6 force, and 16d6 force for their respective size, with a radius equal to 5ft centered on the object or greater determined by the DM. The saving throw type and DC are determined by the DM as well. Only nonmagical objects can be infused in this way.
*You can more acutely use random items infused with cursed energy to attack. When you use Cursed Weapon Enhancement on an improvised weapon, it will use your martial arts die rather than a d4.

### Cursed Energy Flotation
Cursed energy can be used for a lot of purposes, especially when finely infused into an object, allowing it to float. As a free action, you may touch a non-magical tiny object and imbue it with cursed energy, causing it to float. After you infuse 10 of these tiny objects per long rest, it will cost 1 cursed energy. For example, infusing 9 pebbles costs nothing, infusing 10 will then cost 1 cursed energy, infusing 15 will cost none after the initial 1 spent at 10, and infusing 20 will cost another 1 for a total of 2 spent. For nonmagical objects above tiny, the action cost and energy cost are as follows: 


| Size | Action Cost | Cursed Energy Cost |

| Small | Bonus Action | 2 Cursed Energy |

| Medium | Action | 4 Cursed Energy |

| Large | Full Turn Action | 8 Cursed Energy           |

| Huge | 2 Full Turn Actions | 16 Cursed Energy    |

| Gargantuan | 4 Full Turn Actions | 32 Cursed Energy         |

An object stays floating for a number of minutes equal to your cha modifier or until you end its imbuement (no action required). An object that's floating is weightless, and stays in place unless pushed, dragged, or otherwise moved.

### Cursed Energy Battery
You can accumulate a part of your cursed energy in an object that will become a battery.  As an action, you may store Cursed Energy in your battery, spending Cursed Energy up to your cha modifier + your proficiency bonus, and half of the energy spent is then stored. The amount stored cannot be reduced, nor can it be temporary, and the amount spent on storage will always be equal to half of the amount stored. Attempting to store more energy in an object than it can hold will result in it exploding, dealing magical force damage in a radius around the object that is equal to its size. Creatures within that radius must make a Con saving throw, taking half damage on a success. You cannot spend the stored energy for any of your personal features, however, this energy may be used in or by external things, such as a Cursed Corpse or Puppet, or a Barrier you have made.


| Battery Size | Explosion Damage | Maximum Amount of Cursed Energy Imbued |

| Tiny | 1d6 | Â½ of your Cursed Energy Maximum |

| Small | 4d6 | Equal to your Cursed Energy Maximum |

| Medium | 8d6 | Double of your Cursed Energy Maximum |

| Large | 12d6 | Triple of your Cursed Energy Maximum |

| Huge | 16d6 | Quadruple of your Cursed Energy Maximum |

| Gargantuan | 20d6 | Quintuple of your Cursed Energy Maximum |


Other creatures cannot contribute to a battery that does not belong to them.


## Efficiency Fundamentals
At 11th level, you have gained the fundamentals of efficiency with utilizing cursed energy. 

You gain 2 selections to reduce the cursed energy cost of. For each selection, choose a feature that costs cursed energy that you possess. Each selection has its curse energy cost reduced by half of your proficiency bonus (rounded down), to a minimum of 1 cursed energy. If you choose to reduce the cost of a **Domain Expansion**, it is only reduced by half of the amount it would normally be reduced. You may change these selections upon leveling up.

When you select a feature to reduce the cost of, you pick one specific cost, rather the entire feature. For example, you cannot choose to reduce the entire Cursed Energy Enhancement feature, but can reduce your Cursed Strikes with one selection.

## Exorcise
At 13th Level, you've learned to eliminate weaker cursed spirits without spending effort or energy. As an Action, you can automatically destroy a cursed spirit within 30 ft. of you that you can see that has a CR equal to or lower than half of your proficiency bonus (rounded down).

## High Output Sorcery
Starting at 13th level you've begun to learn some of the most explosive sorcery techniques, utilizing extreme levels of output. You learn one of the following abilities: Chanting, Maximum Output, or Explosive Blow.

### Chanting

> **By not choosing to omit any part of your technique's activation requirements, going through the chants and the dances, you can draw out power beyond your limit.**



You may choose one of your Innate Technique features and recite one of your chants as an Action *(V-1)*. You must then maintain concentration on Chanting as if concentrating on a spell until the beginning of your next turn. After successfully maintaining concentration, the next time you use that Innate Technique Feature that has a recitation within two turns of completing chanting, it will gain two of the following benefits of your choice:

*Your technique will ignore resistances and treat immunities as resistances.

*You may double the original Range, Width, or Radius of your Technique.

*You may triple the original Range, Width, or Radius of your Technique. (You must have doubled the range already through Chanting in order to apply this bonus).

*If the Innate Technique feature requires a saving throw, the target(s) makes their save at disadvantage. You also raise the DC by your proficiency bonus for that saving throw.

*If your Innate Technique feature has you make an attack roll, you gain advantage on that attack roll and may add your proficiency bonus to that roll an extra time.

*The cost of your Innate Technique feature is halved.

*Your Innate Technique will ignore all forms of cover.

*Your Innate Technique's Action cost will be lowered by 1 (Ex. Full Turn Action -> Action -> Bonus Action -> Free Action). If the feature is a Reaction and its Action Cost is reduced you will gain an extra Reaction specifically for that feature. (You must be Complex Chanting in order to apply this bonus)

*The duration of your Innate Technique feature is doubled.

*The duration of your Innate Technique feature is tripled. (You must have doubled the duration already through Chanting in order to apply this bonus).

*If an Innate Technique feature inflicts damage by causing a target to make a save, when taking the Resistant feature into consideration, the DC used for the save will be the total DC instead of the base DC. (You must be Complex Chanting in order to apply this bonus).

*The cost of your Innate Technique feature is reduced to Â¼. (You must be Complex Chanting in order to apply this bonus)(You must have halved the cost already through Chanting in order to apply this bonus. This reduction does not stack with the Â½ reduction, but replaces it).

*If your Innate Technique feature would force your opponent to make a saving throw, they roll 3 dice instead of one and take the lowest result for the roll. (You must be Complex Chanting in order to apply this bonus).

*If your Innate Technique feature would have you make an Attack roll, you may roll 3 dice instead of 1 and take the highest result for the roll. (You must be Complex Chanting in order to apply this bonus).

Every subsequent turn you spend your Action chanting the technique it will gain another two bonuses of your choice. The chanted technique may only have a number of bonuses applied to it equal to 1+ your int or wis mod. In addition, the chanted Innate Technique feature will have its final damage boosted by 20% per round you spent chanting. (After all the dice have been rolled, multiply the total damage(s) by 1.2). This damage bonus is capped at 100%. (2x Damage).

Rather than using an action, you can perform a more complex variation of Chanting that combines both complex hand signs and delicate recitations as a Full Turn Action *(S-2, V-1)*. When Chanting in this manner you may add four bonuses from the above list to the chanted technique, and every subsequent turn you spend a Full Turn Action chanting the technique in this way it will gain four more bonuses of your choice. Additionally, the chanted Innate Technique feature will have its final damage boosted by 50% per round you spent Complex Chanting. This damage bonus is capped at 100%. (2x Damage).

If your Innate Technique feature that benefits from Chanting allows you to make more than one attack roll or forces a target to make more than one save or has multiple ranges, you may only choose one of those components at a time to apply the benefit to. (Ex: You could choose to apply a saving throw disadvantage to the first saving throw of the technique, but in order to apply one to the second saving throw of the technique you would have to add that bonus to that part as well). You cannot perform Maximum Output on an Innate Technique feature benefiting from Chanting and vice versa. You cannot apply the benefits of Chanting towards a Domain Expansion. You cannot ready an action for chanting.

When using **Cursed Concentration**, you do not gain any of its benefits when concentrating on this feature specifically.


### Maximum Output

> **You have learned how to increase the power of your technique by outputting the most cursed energy you can, for a high power move. **



As a bonus action, you must spend Cursed Energy equal to the original cost of one of your lapse, reversal, extension, or maximum techniques twice. The first time you pay this cost, any reductions that apply to the technique are used; the second time, none are applied. Doing this will amplify its cursed energy output, making it gain two of the following benefits the next time it's cast before the end of your next turn:

*The technique's range and area of effect are increased by half its original range, rounded to the highest 5. For example, a technique with 10ft. of range would become a 15ft. range technique. This range increase does not work with touch-based techniques.
*The technique's damage dice is increased by half of its original dice or damage bonus if the technique is a flat damage rounded up. For example, a technique with 10d10 die of damage would add 5d10 die of damage to the total damage die.
*The technique's cursed energy attack roll bonus to hit is increased by half of your proficiency bonus, rounded up.
*The technique's DC is increased by half of your proficiency bonus rounded up.
*The technique's duration is increased by half of its original duration. For example, a technique that lasts a minute would last 1 minute and 5 rounds.
You cannot choose the same benefit twice.

Maximum Output lasts on a technique (once it has been casted) for a number of rounds equal to half of your cha modifier, rounded up.


### Explosive Blow

> **You've reached a level of bodily reinforcement far above the average sorcerer, able to pack your fists with unreal amounts of cursed energy. **



As a bonus action, you can concentrate on channeling a very condensed portion of your cursed energy into either your limbs or a weapon for 6 cursed energy. For 1 minute after you perform this bonus action, the next unarmed strike or attack with a weapon you land adds half of your attacks' damage dice (rounded down) as extra damage onto the damage roll for your attack.

For 12 cursed energy instead of 6 cursed energy, the melee attack that you use with this feature will add your attacks' damage dice to the damage roll instead of only half. Additional damage dice from explosive blow are not doubled on a critical hit.

On a successful melee attack that benefits from Explosive Blow, the creature must succeed a str saving throw, or be knocked back a number of feet equal to either your attacks' ability modifier or cha modifier times 10 in the direction of your choice. If the creature collides with an unsecured object or structure, then the creature and the unsecured object or structure receive 1d10 bludgeoning damage per every 10 feet the creature traveled before colliding with the surface (to a maximum of 10d10). If it was another creature, they take damage equal to half of your attack's damage roll instead.


## School Graduation
Starting at 14th Level, you have finally graduated from your Jujutsu School, now becoming a full-on Jujutsu Sorcerer. You gain one of the following depending on which school you have chosen:

### Osaka Jujutsu High
You have graduated from Osaka Jujutsu High and are becoming the best with your technique. You gain a unique technique exclusive to your Cursed Technique or Sorcerer Paths.

### Kyoto Jujutsu High
You have graduated from Kyoto Jujutsu High, mastering what a Sorcerer should be. You gain one cursed feat you meet the prerequisites of from your experiences. Lastly, you also gain access to the following feature: 

- **Accursed Adrenaline.** Your combat training has honed yourself in diligence to ramp up. At the beginning of your turn, if you're at or below half your hitpoints and in a combat against an enemy whose CR is equal to or above your level, you may enter a state ramping up your body with adrenaline and cursed energy. For one minute, you may make an additional attack, dash, disengage, dodge, or object interaction during your turn. Either your str or dex score increases by 2, as does its maximum. Lastly, once per turn, you may reduce the cost of Cursed Strikes by half your cha Modifier (rounded down, minimum of 1) or increase the damage of an active Cursed Weapon Enhancement by half of your Charisma modifier (rounded down, minimum of 1). 

You may use this feature once per long rest. At the end of this period, you need to make a DC 20 con saving throw, gaining 1 level of exhaustion on a failure.

### Ainu Jujutsu High
You have graduated from Ainu Jujutsu High, mastering the usage of barriers. When you use any features that use barriers, you may choose to reduce their action cost by 1(full turn action->action->bonus action->free action). If this would reduce a reaction's action cost, you gain 1 additional reaction only for using said reaction. This buff cannot reduce the action cost of a domain.

### Sendai Jujutsu High
You have graduated from Sendai Jujutsu High, mastering the essence of being a defensive sorcerer. You gain one cursed feat you meet the prerequisites of from your experiences. You also gain access to the following feature:

- **Martyr's Stance.** Your focus on defense has lead to you developing an emergency last resort: the Martyr's Stance. At the beginning of your turn, if you're at or below half your hitpoints in a combat against an enemy whose CR is equal to or above your level, you can decide to enter a stance optimizing your defensive capabilities while sacrificing stamina and offense. For one minute, your speed is halved, while you can add your con modifier again when calculating your damage reduction from Sendai's durable body feature. In addition, your con score increases by 2, as well as its maximum. Lastly, you gain another +2 bonus to your Armor Class. You can only do this once per long rest. At the end of this period, you need to make a DC 20 con saving throw, gaining 1 level of exhaustion on a failure.

## Near The Pinnacle
At 17th Level, your strength grows by the moment; you'll soon reach the pinnacle of a normal sorcerer. You gain a +4. You may distribute this +4 in your scores. For example, you can put 2 in str and 2 in dex. 

Also, you must choose one of the following peaks to reach:

**Peak of Might.** Your str score maximum increases to 24. The increase is lost if your str maximum is or would become equal to or higher than this. Additionally, you may shove or grapple the creature whenever you hit a critical with a melee weapon attack that uses str.

**Peak of Speed.** Your dex score maximum increases to 24.  The increase is lost if your dex maximum is or would become equal to or higher than this. Additionally, you can take the dash action as a bonus action. If you already could do that, you may do it as a free action instead.

**Peak of Endurance.** Your con score maximum increases to 24. The increase is lost if your con maximum is or would become equal to or higher than this. Additionally, your maximum hit points increase by your Jujutsu sorcerer level added twice.

**Peak of Intellect.** Your int score maximum increases to 24. The increase is lost if your int maximum is or would become equal to or higher than this. Additionally, you can now ignore one prerequisite before taking a **cursed feat**. You cannot ignore prerequisites requiring you to have another cursed feat, be a certain race, possess a specific innate technique, possess an innate technique in general, or be a certain class to obtain them.

**Peak of Wisdom.** Your wis score maximum increases to 24. The increase is lost if your wis maximum is or would become equal to or higher than this. Additionally, you gain one **cursed feat** that you meet the prerequisites of, proficiency in one skill or tool of your choice, and expertise in one skill.

**Peak of Sorcery.** Your cha score maximum increases to 24. The increase is lost if your cha maximum is or would become equal to or higher than this. Additionally, your Cursed Energy maximum increases by 20.


## Cursed Techniques
<details markdown="1"><summary>Ten Shadows Technique</summary>
`45a51bc7771c1075fc6365941f88c3ff.jpg`
The various hand signs for the numerous shikigami granted by the ten shadows technique, source [2]
The Ten Shadows Technique can summon up to ten powerful shikigami using shadows as an intermediary. 

#### Shikigami Summoning

(REWORK COMING SOON)
**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 1st level, you have the prized technique of the Zenin clan, the ten shadows earned to summon shikigami from the shadows. As an action for 1 cursed energy you may summon up to 2 Divine Dogs (S-2) anywhere within 5 feet of you. Any shikigami summoned as part of this technique last until they are reduced to 0 hit points, or you take a short or long rest. They regain all lost hit points at the end of a long rest.

Divine Dogs count as 1 shikigami for summoning and controlling purposes.

To control and command your shikigami, use the Summon Commanding Rules and you may command and summon a number of summons equal to half of your Charisma modifier rounded up, to a maximum of 3.

Additionally, the 10 Shadows Technique comes with a trial, in order to gain other shikigami, you must subjugate the Shikigami.

Trial. As you level, you will gain access to your other shikigami, and you must face them in combat alone to gain control over them. As a full round action(which is completed at the beginning of your next turn), you may summon any shikigami that are on the table below within 5 feet of you. If you haven't battled against the untamed shikigami and won, it will be under the GM's control, act on their own turn and be hostile to you. If you have battled against the untamed shikigami alone (does not include using your tamed ten shadows shikigami) and reduce it to 0 hit points, you will now be able to summon the shikigami under your control, tamed. The distance which you can summon an untamed shikigami cannot be increased.

You can choose any creatures within 15 feet of you or the untamed shikigami to become part of the trial until either they or the untamed shikigami are dead. If you are reduced to 0 hit points or are killed while any creature other than the untamed shikigami is within the trial, you will enter a state of stasis until the ritual is properly complete, when your death will be completed. If the ritual is interrupted before it is properly concluded, such as an outsider killing the untamed shikigami before all other creatures in the ritual have been killed, then you will instead begin rolling death saves when the ritual concludes, if applicable. Additionally, if you are reduced to 0 hit points, the untamed shikigami becomes hostile to all other creatures within the trial.

| Cursed Energy | Shikigami | Level Requirement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 2 cursed energy | Gama (S-2) | Level 1 |
| 4 cursed energy | Nue (S-2) | Level 3 |
| 3 cursed energy | Rabbit Escape (S-2) | Level 5 |
| 5 cursed energy | Great Serpent (S-1) | Level 6 |
| 6 cursed energy | Round Deer (S-2) | Level 8 |
| 7 cursed energy | Max Elephant (S-2) | Level 10 |
| 8 cursed energy | Mourn Tiger (S-2) | Level 12 |
| 9 cursed energy | Piercing Ox (S-2) | Level 14 |
| 10 cursed energy | Eight-Handled Sword Divergent Sila Divine General Mahoraga (S-2, V-1) | Level 17 |
Additionally, should any one of your tamed shikigami die, they can no longer be summoned ever again. Should this happen, they are now eligible for Totality.


#### Summoning Techniques

When you defeat an untamed shikigami in battle, you gain a new special form to summon it.

You can summon the shikigami as an action.
The distance you can summon your shikigami is 10 feet.
##### Rabbit Escape

You may summon Rabbit Escape for 3 cursed energy as a reaction to an attack made against you and add your Charisma modifier to your AC against that attack. If this causes the attack to miss, you reappear in a space up to a distance equal to your speed from your original location. This movement does not generate opportunity attacks and after the attack hits or misses, Rabbit Escape is immediately desummoned however summoning Rabbit Escape in this way does not count toward your summon limit.
##### Round Deer

You may summon Round Deer as a reaction to being afflicted by a cursed technique or a cursed energy fueled feature, causing it to appear within 5 feet of you, and using its Neutralize Energy action(if applicable). Doing so uses Round Deer's action for the round.
To use the other special summoning techniques, you must choose a Ten Shadows Feat.

Totality. Additionally, if one of your summons is killed, you can use a bonus action to grant one of your summons its Totality form. You cannot use totality on a Shikigami that is already summoned and you cannot have more than 2 shikigami in a totality, which is the living shikigami and the deceased shikigami. When you make a totality, the creature gains the strengths of the deceased shikigami. They will gain the following changes:

Their Strength, Dexterity, Constitution and Charisma scores become those of the creature who has the higher score. Then they will increase by the modifier of the creature with the smaller scores.(Ex: Nue has 14 Strength while Divine Dog has 17, their totality would have 19 Strength.)
When you combine two shikigami of the same size, the totalities size increases by 1
The cost of the totality will be equal to both shikigami used. (Ex: Combining Great Serpent with Nue will cost 9 Cursed Energy before reductions.)
Their AC will become that of the creature with the higher AC.
When making a totality, the base shikigami gains the skill and saving throw proficiency's of the shikigami used in the totality.
They receive the amount of hit die of both creatures combined, with the size of the hit die being determined as normal.
Their speeds are added together.(Ex: Two creatures with 30 base speed would have 60 base speed in totality form)
They will receive all features, traits, actions, bonus actions, reactions, legendary actions, skill, saving throw proficiencies, damage resistances, damage immunities, condition immunities, and senses from both creatures.
Their size will become the size of the bigger creature.
Their CR will become that of the creature who has a higher CR. Then it will increase by half the CR of the creature with the smaller CR rounded up.(Ex: Nue's CR is 4, and Great Serpent's CR is 5, their totality's CR would be 7)
After calculating the ability scores, the difference in the ability modifier from the original user of a damaging feature and the totality is added to the amount of dice. This value can not increase the damage of a feature to over double its original value in dice. You apply this before Stronger shadows. For save attack damage, the stat corresponding to the save is used. (Ex: Mourn Tiger has a +7 in Dexterity and Nue has a +3. Mourn Tiger’s Dexterity Modifier increases by +1, meaning you add 1 additional dice to the Totality's attack to the damage dice that use Dexterity as the ability modifier, changing Mourn Tiger’s bite attack from a 2d10+7 into a 3d10+8.) This increase only applies to direct attack rolls and saving throws.(excluding Natural Weight) This value is halved(rounded down to a minimum of 1) for attacks that can be used through a multi-attacking feature.(Ex: Nue’s Wings and Talon)
You can not make a Totality using Rabbit Escape or Eight-Handled Sword Divergent Sila Divine General Mahoraga. You only apply Stronger Shadows to the shikigami after they are in the totality form, so the calculations above are made using their stats without Stronger Shadows. When summoning a totality, you may choose to instead summon the base shikigami being the living shikigami before being made into a totality.

Stronger Shadows. Your shikigami grow alongside you as you become stronger. Your Shikigami can gain Stronger Shadows counters in the following ways:

Shikigami Reserve: Your tamed shikigami gain a number of Stronger Shadows counters equal to 1/20th of your cursed energy maximum (Rounded Down.) These counters are applied passively.

Shikigami Output: When you summon a tamed shikigami, you may spend additional cursed energy up to half of your Charisma modifier (rounded down), to apply stronger shadows counters to the shikigami, for every 2 cursed energy spent the shikigami will gain a Stronger Shadows counter. Stronger Shadows counters applied this way will last for a number of hours equal to your Charisma modifier, and while applied to a shikigami, you cannot apply more Stronger Shadows counters to the shikigami in this way, unless you remove the Stronger Counters applied this way (no action required).

At 11th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier. At 17th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier plus half of your Charisma modifier (rounded down). At 20th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier added twice.

No matter from which source, a shikigami can only possess a maximum of 5 stronger shadows counters at any time, with the exception of Divine Dog, which can have 10. If it is the base for a totality, then the extra 5 counters that can be spent on it only apply to the Dog's features from the totality.

For each stronger shadows counter applied, the shikigami will gain the following benefits:

Overall Increase. They gain a +1 bonus to their Strength, Dexterity, Constitution and Charisma scores. Their AC is not increased by ability scores increasing.
Vitality Increase. They gain an additional hit die.
Speed Increase. They gain 5 additional feet of speed.
Attack Power Increase. Their attack rolls damage die increases by one die.
Power Increase. Their dice for features that force creatures to make a saving throw increases one die.
Defense Increase. Their Armor Class increases by 1. This does not apply to Eight-Handled Sword Divergent Sila Divine General Mahoraga.

#### Shadow Manipulation


**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 3rd level, you've learned to make use of your ability to manipulate shadows, allowing you to form an extra dimensional space to hide your things and slightly manipulate the shadow of other creatures. You gain the following features:

Shadow Storage: You may store objects in an extra-dimensional space within your shadow. As a Bonus Action or Action, you may spend 1 curse energy to store or withdraw an item you are holding, an unattended object, or a Shikigami from this extradimension space. You may store any number of your shikigami. Stored items count against your carrying capacity.

As a Bonus Action for 2 curse energy, you may force a creature standing on darkness, that you can see within 30 feet of you, yo make a Dexterity (Acrobatics) check against your Curse Energy DC. They make this check with Disadvantage if they are completely covered in darkness or if they are inside a veil. On a failure, they are trapped inside your Shadow Storage and begin suffocating. At the end of their turns, trapped creatures may make a Strength or Dexterity saving throw (their choice) at the end of their turns, freeing themselves on a success and appearing in an unoccupied space within 5ft of you of their choice. You may have up to your Proficiency Bonus in Medium Sized creatures trapped at a time. For each size category above medium, they take up an additional creature spot.

Shadow Ridge. Once per round, as a reaction for 1 Cursed Energy against a melee attack roll, you can force a creature within 10ft of you to make said attack with disadvantage as their legs fall into their own shadow momentarily.


#### The Well's Unknown Abyss


**Extension Technique**

At 6th level, you can combine two of your shikigami into a single fusion, sacrificing their individual strength for a versatile but weaker form. As an action, you can spend 6 Cursed Energy to fuse a base shikigami with an enhancer shikigami, granting the fused shikigami the shikigami Alteration feature of the enhancer. The fusion ends if it drops to 0 hit points or if you dismiss it as a bonus action. When fused, the shikigami follows these rules:

Designate one shikigami as the base and another as the enhancer.
The fused shikigami inherits all traits of both shikigami and gains the Shikigami Alteration feature from the enhancer.
It gains no immunities, resistances, or vulnerabilities from either shikigami.
Its hit points are half of the base shikigami's hit points.
Its speed is half of base Shikigami's speeds rounded down in increments of 5 feet.
Its ability scores are half of the base shikigami’s ability scores.
The fused shikigami's size matches the base shikigami.
The fused shikigami's AC matches the base shikigami.
The fused shikigami gains one condition immunity of your choice from either shikigami.
The fusion's CR equals the base shikigami’s CR plus half of the enhancer’s CR.
The fused shikigami's size can be reduced, and its quantity increased. For every size reduction, the number of shikigami increases by two, up to a limit of times equal to half the summoner’s proficiency bonus (rounded up). For example, if the base shikigami is Large, the summoner can reduce its size to Medium and summon 3 fusion shikigami, or further reduce it to Small to summon 5 fusion shikigami. The multiple shikigami created this way will only count as 1 shikigami for the purposes of summoning, controlling, commanding and any reactions it has.
When the size is reduced and quantity is increased, the CR, STR and CON stats, as well as the hit points of the base shikigami and cursed energy are split evenly among the summoned shikigami. This split applies only to the fusion and does not affect the original shikigami’s individual stats when unfused.
If the fused shikigami is split into multiple shikigami, the damage of all damage dealing features will be evenly split between each fusion shikigami. The dice tier of these damage dealing features is also reduced by each step the base shikigami's size is reduced.
This ability cannot be used to fuse Rabbit Escape or Eight-Handled Sword Divergent Sila Divine General Mahoraga. If the fused shikigami is destroyed, you may resummon the original shikigami individually as long as the base shikigami was not destroyed. You may only have one fusion. You only apply Stronger Shadows to the shikigami after they are in the fused form, so the calculations above are made using their stats without Stronger Shadows. Shikigami Gigantification cannot be used with The Well's Unknown Abyss in any step. For the purposes of summoning and controlling, a Wells Unknown Abyss fusion counts as one shikigami.




#### Shikigami Alteration Feature

| Spirit | Shikigami Alteration |  |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Divine Dogs | Gives Pack Tactics feature |  |
| Gama | Gives the Support Carry, Tongue, Swallow and Tongue Grab features |  |
| Great Serpent | Gives Shadow Burrow feature, 30 ft. burrow speed and 30 ft. swim speed |  |
| Nue | Gives 80 ft fly speed |  |
| Round Deer | Grants the Reverse Cursed Technique feature, the fusion will use its scores and CR to determine its RCT pool and cursed energy pool |  |
| Piercing Ox | It will grant the Battering Ram feature and the Gore attack, the fusion will use its scores to determine the attack and damage bonuses. |  |
| Mourn Tiger | It will grant the Sneak Attack feature |  |
| Max Elephant | Increases hit dice by 20 |  |

#### Shadow Manipulation Expansion


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, you got better at manipulating shadows, becoming able to even manipulate to the shadows of others and even use shadows in esoteric ways never before seen, you can use the following techniques:

Shadow Merge. You get better at manipulating shadows, becoming able to connect to the shadows of others and even summoning your creatures from behind them. As a free action or during a short or long rest, you can connect to the shadow of a creature within 120ft of you. As a bonus action for 3 cursed energy, you may teleport behind this creature's shadow from up to 120 feet away. Also, if you have any of your shikigami summoned you can spend 3 additional cursed energy to make one of them be teleported alongside you.

As a bonus action for 3 cursed energy, you can sink into your or another creature whose shadow you're connected to. While inside the shadow, you are considered invisible and under total cover until you take any type of action, which makes you leave the shadow within 5 feet of the creature. You are also removed from the shadow if the creature is knocked unconscious. You can stay under a creature's shadow for up to your Charisma modifier (minimum of 1) hours.

In case you utilize this feature on a hostile creature, the creature makes a Wisdom (Perception) check against your cursed energy DC. On a success they know you are behind them, on a failure they will be surprised. If you were completely hidden before teleporting to said creature's shadow, it makes the Wisdom (Perception) check with disadvantage.

You can connect to the shadows of a number of creatures up to your proficiency bonus. Whenever you try to connect to a new shadow after reaching the maximum, you will choose which shadow to lose your connection to.

Shadow Summon. You've learnt how to impede your opponents through their own shadows. When you spend cursed energy to summon your Shikigami, you can spend an extra 6 Cursed Energy to summon your Shikigami through the shadow of the creature you're in the shadow of. When summoned this way, you can command your Shikigami to take one action it is capable of. If it is an attack roll, the one action has advantage. And if it forces a saving throw, the target has disadvantage.

Additionally, you can now summon your Shikigami as a bonus action.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Incomplete Shadows


**Extension Technique**

At 14th level, you have learned how to utilize the special property of your shikigami, their form of shadow. You have derived a special summoning technique to keep their form incomplete. When you summon a ten shadows shikigami, you can summon them in an unstable form, causing their maximum hit points to be halved and any damage it deals to be halved(rounded down) while in this form. In addition, while in this form their speed is reduced to 10ft, and their size can be increased by up to two sizes. While in their incomplete form, the action cost to command them is increased by 1. This form lasts a number of rounds equal to your Charisma modifier, or until you choose to end it at any time (no action required). While in this unstable form any hit points lost are only lost until the unstable form ends, and if the shikigami dies in this unstable form, you retain the ability to summon it again. When incomplete shadows ends you may choose to immediately summon the shikigami in its normal form at the same location for no cursed energy cost. This cannot be used on Eight-Handled Sword Divergent Sila Divine General Mahoraga.


#### Shikigami Gigantification


**Technique Improvement**

Finally at 20th level, you have developed a technique that allows you to enhance the size of your shikigami. As part of summoning a shikigami created by your cursed technique and using Shikigami Output, for every 5 cursed energy spent, you may increase the shikigami's size by one, to a maximum of Gargantuan. If they are already Gargantuan, you can only increase their size once, making their width and height doubles. When a shikigami is increased this way, they gain the following:

Their hit die increase by two per size increase.
Their attack roll damage dice increase by one per size increase.
Their saving throw damage dice if any increase by two per size increase.
Their Constitution score increases by two per size increase (to a maximum of 30).
You cannot use this extension technique on Eight-Handled Sword Divergent Sila Divine General Mahoraga or Totalities that have more than two shikigami. This lasts as long as the Stronger Shadows counters from Shikigami Output last, or until you end it at will (no action required).


#### Light Manipulation


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

With great mastery over Reversed Cursed Technique, you have found the reversal of your technique. You have learnt how to channel light rather than shadow, illuminating your or another creature's shadow. As a bonus action for 4 cursed energy, you may target a creature's shadow who you can see, causing it to erupt in blinding flash of light. The creature must make a Constitution saving throw. On a success, nothing happens. On a failure, the creature is blinded until the end of your turn.

Summon Dispelling. You can also manipulate the light in such a way as to interfere with the summons of others. As a Bonus Action, you can invert the shadow of a summon within 120 feet which you are connected to, for a number of cursed energy equal to half of the summons's CR or level(rounded down) You may force its summoner to make a Charisma saving throw. On a success, nothing happens. On a failure, the Summon becomes incapacitated and cannot move until the end of your next turn. If the summoner fails the saving throw by 5 or more, the summon is instead de-summoned, and cannot be summoned again for a number of rounds equal to your Charisma modifier.

Light Ridge. As a reaction to a melee attack roll for 2 cursed energy, if the attacker is within 10 feet of you, you may force them to make a Constitution Saving throw, on a success, nothing happens. On a failure, the attack misses, and the attacker is blinded until the end of the turn. A creature is immune to this feature if they do not rely on sight, such as with blindsight.

Using Light Manipulation causes your shadow storage to become unstable. One random object, if any, currently inside your shadow storage will be ejected out, ending up within 5 feet of the shadow on which Light Manipulation was used.

At 10th level, you can now use Light Manipulation to target an entire area of shadow, inverting it into a blinding flash of Light. As a bonus action for 6 cursed energy, you may cause a creature's Shadow that you are connected to and can see, or your own shadow, to flash in large radius. All creatures within a 20 feet radius must make a constitution saving throw. On a success, nothing happens, on a failure becoming blinded for one minute. A creature may repeat the saving throw at the beginning of their turns, ending the effects on a success.

Using Light manipulation in this way causes all items within your Shadow Storage to be forced out, ending up within 5 feet of the shadow on which Light Manipulation was used.

You cannot be blinded by Light Manipulation.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Shrine</summary>
`e16d6a2515b2fb05a84db0cc3128c594.jpg`
The strongest sorcerer in history using dismantles, source [3]
This cursed technique unleashes invisible slashes that carve through foes and conjures powerful flames. 

#### Dismantle

Lapse Technique (S-1)
Starting at 1st level, you can create basic, flying slashes. As an action or replacing an attack on your Attack action, you can spend an amount of cursed energy up to half of your Charisma modifier (rounded up, minimum 1) to replace one of those attacks with a ranged cursed energy attack roll against a creature within 100 feet of you. On a hit, the target takes 1d8 slashing damage per cursed energy spent.

You make this cursed energy attack roll with advantage if the target can't see invisible objects. You can replace multiple attacks with Dismantle, but you must spend appropriate cursed energy for each attack. This cannot replace attacks from features such as Cursed Blast of Blows.

Finally, when you make a dismantle attack, you may choose to make three ranged cursed energy attack rolls or only one. If you use dismantle this way, each attack will deal a third of the dice of your normal dismantle(rounded down) slashing damage.

At 5th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier when using Dismantle. At 11th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier + half of your Charisma modifier(rounded down). At 17th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier added twice. At 20th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier added thrice.


#### Grid Slash


**Extension Technique**

At 3rd level, you have elaborated a new way to use your slashes in a wide area. As part of using Dismantle, you can spend additional cursed energy up to half your Charisma modifier (rounded up) to force all creatures in a 10-times-the-amount-spent-foot-long, 30-foot-wide and 30-foot-tall line starting from you to make a Dexterity saving throw by turning it into into a Grid. On a failure, they take your Dismantle's damage, or half as much on a success.

At 5th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier when using Grid Slash. At 11th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier + half of your Charisma modifier(rounded down). At 17th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier added twice. At 20th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier added thrice.


#### Cleave

Lapse Technique (S-1)
At 6th level, you have developed a new type of slash that adapts its power depending on the target. As an action, or replacing an attack as part of the Attack action if you target a creature you are grappling or an object, you may make a melee weapon attack roll against a creature.

On a hit, before determining the damage of this attack, you will learn how much cursed energy you need to spend for the average damage of your cleave to reduce the target to 0 hit points from their current hit points, taking into account temporary hit points, damage reductions and thresholds, resistances and immunities, and any other feature which would directly affect the outcome. You will be told the maximum amount if you cannot reduce them to 0 hit points from their current amount by this calculation.

After learning this amount, you may spend up to your Charisma modifier added twice in cursed energy. The target then takes 1d12 slashing damage per cursed energy spent and must make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, they are bleeding. On a success, they receive no additional conditions.

At 11th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier added twice + half of your Charisma modifier (rounded up). At 17th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier added thrice. At 20th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier added four times.


#### Spiderweb


**Extension Technique**

At 10th level, you have developed an extension of your technique to shatter the battlefield. As part of using Cleave on the ground, you can spend an amount of cursed energy up to your Charisma modifier added twice to turn the ground in a 10 times the amount spent foot radius circle into difficult terrain.

All creatures within range except you must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they are knocked prone. On a success, nothing happens.
