Whenever you witness a miracle happening, a Purple Marking appears right under one of your eyes, making you gain a Miracle Mark. You can have up to 6 Miracle Marks under your eyes. Those miracles are listed down below:

| Miracle | Description |
| --- | --- |
| Lucky! | Hitting a jackpot is exciting for everyone, not just people with gambling addictions! Whenever you or an ally you can see or hear rolls the maximum amount they could on a dice roll, you gain 1 Miracle Mark. If it is a damage roll, it is necessary that all of the rolls are the maximum amount. |
| Equal Numbers! | Equal numbers means good luck, time to celebrate! Every time you or an ally you can see or hear rolls two equal numbers, such as 11, 22, 33, 44 and so on, you gain 1 Miracle Mark. |
| Treasure! | Finding free stuff is always great! Every time you or an ally you can see finds a new item, weapon or armor of at least Rare rarity you gain 1 Miracle Mark. This only counts towards cursed tools and magical items. |
| Hardship Overcoming! | Overcoming hardships is something rare to see in such a vile world we live in, so seeing it is truly a miracle. Whenever you or an ally you can see or hear succeed in a saving throw or ability check, or hit an attack roll where you had disadvantage on you gain 1 Miracle Mark. |
Whenever you are hit by an attack or fail a saving throw that would reduce you to 0 hit points or lower (even if it would kill you outright), you can spend 1 Miracle Mark and 2 cursed energy to instead take ¼ as much damage from the damage source. If it would reduce you to 0 hit points or lower, you're instead reduced to 1 hit point.

While you have Miracle Markings you roll death saving throws with advantage.

Additionally, At 11th level, you gain 2 Miracle Marks for every miracle you witness. This is increased to 3 Miracle Marks at 17th level.

You cannot gain Miracle Marks from the effects of your own cursed technique, except when you naturally fulfill a listed miracle condition as described above.


#### Calamity


#### Reversal Technique

After mastering reverse cursed technique, you found out your technique reversal, learning about Calamity Marks. Whenever you witness a calamity happening, a Yellow Marking appears right above one of your eyes, making you gain a Calamity Mark. You can have up to 6 Calamity Marks above your eyes. Those calamities are listed down below:

| Calamity | Description |
| --- | --- |
| Unlucky. | Failing makes no one but the person who failed sad. Whenever you or a hostile creature you can see or hear rolls the minimum amount they could on a dice roll, you gain 1 Calamity Mark. |
| Opposite Numbers. | Nobody likes opposite numbers, it just looks ugly. Every time you or a hostile creature you can see or hear rolls two different numbers with the first one being odd and the other being even, such as 12, 14, 16, 18 and so on, you gain 1 Calamity Mark. |
| Trash. | Finding free stuff is cool, but only if it’s valuable. Every time you or a hostile creature you can see finds a new item, weapon or armor of at max Uncommon rarity you gain 1 Calamity Mark. This only counts towards cursed tools and magical items. |
| Failing with Ease. | Failing is common in this world, but failing so easily is impressive. Whenever you or a hostile creature you can see or hear fails in a saving throw or ability check, or misses an attack roll where they had advantage on, you gain 1 Calamity Mark. |
As a reaction to a hostile creature succeeding a roll, you can spend 2 Calamity Marks and 4 cursed energy to roll a d10. After rolling it, subtract it from the roll. If they fail because of your die and it reaches a 1 or lower (counting the natural die with no modifiers), it is considered as if they have rolled a nat 1 for all purposes but for features that avoid nat 1’s.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Ice Formation</summary>
`bcf421c5b37fd62a9c8a2eb78300d997.jpg`
An Ice Formation user from the Heian Era utilizing frost calm, Source [31]
This Innate technique gives its user the ability to create and manipulate ice. It can range from small to large quantities, depending on the desired utilization of the technique. 

#### Created Ice

Whenever you utilize your technique, you create varying quantities of ice. The ice occupies the same place it was used at, which means that if you use a 10ft. cone, it will cover a 10ft. cone of ice and will be 10ft. in height.

Any ice created by you has an amount of hit points equal to your Charisma modifier (minimum of 1) times your Jujutsu Sorcerer level. Its AC is equal to your cursed energy DC. Its size is the same as the size of the creature caught in it, with its size increasing by one for each creature affected by it. It is vulnerable to fire and bludgeoning damage, is immune psychic and poison, and if it receives cold damage it regains an equal amount of hit points instead.

Creatures reduce your ice maximum hit points by ¼ of their maximum when they break out of it.

All ice created by your technique will last until you end it at will (no action required), it is reduced to 0 hit points, or you receive the poisoned, paralyzed, stunned, petrified or unconscious conditions.


#### Ice Formation

Lapse Technique (S-1)
Starting at 1st level, your innate technique has allowed you to mess with the temperature around you, creating extreme levels of cold that you can utilize to partially freeze others. There are a few ways that you can utilize this:

Ice Needle. As an action for up to your Charisma modifier in cursed energy, you can make a needle of ice to launch at an enemy. To use this you make a ranged cursed energy attack roll against a creature within 50 feet of you. On a hit, you deal 1d6 cold damage per energy spent.

This damage increases to 1d8 per energy spent at 5th level, to 1d10 at 11th level, and to 1d12 at 17th level.

Additionally, if you spend the maximum amount on this technique the needle increases in size. When used this way the damage tier is increased by 1 and changed from cold to piercing, along with the range being doubled. Additionally, the needle can pierce through enemies if you roll 5 over the armor class of the targeted creature. If you pierce through the needle will continue for the rest of its range. If it would hit another creature it uses the original attack roll and deals half the original damage.

Ice Grab. As an action for up to your Charisma modifier in cursed energy, you can make a cursed energy melee attack roll as you attempt to freeze an enemy. On a hit, you deal 1d6 cold damage per cursed energy spent and the target must make a Strength saving throw. On a failure, they are Partially Frozen for 1 minute. The limb frozen is random (determined by DM). On a success, they are not Partially Frozen. Creatures can repeat the saving throw to bust the ice off their limb and end the Partially Frozen condition at the start of their turns.

This damage increases to 1d8 per energy spent at 5th level, to 1d10 at 11th level, and to 1d12 at 17th level.


#### Ice Fall

Extension Technique (S-1)
At 3rd level, you have created an extension technique that serves as a great execution method. As an action for 6 cursed energy, you crouch down to create ice on the ground. You telepathically manipulate the ice created, launching one for every creature of your choice within 30 feet of you. Make a ranged cursed energy attack roll. On a hit, they take 6d10 cold damage and must make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, they become restrained and are also Partially Frozen. On a success, they receive no additional effects. If they fail by 5 or more, they are Frozen instead.

The creature can make a Strength saving throw at the beginning of its turns, ending the conditions early on a success.

Hitting or missing, giant spikes of ice will fall from the sky on top of the targeted creatures, forcing them to make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take 6d8 piercing damage. On a success, they take half as much damage.

This technique's damage increases to 9d10 + 9d8 at 11th level, to 12d10 + 12d8 at 17th level and to 15d10 + 15d8 at 20th level.


#### Frost Calm

Extension Technique (S-1, V-1)
At 6th level, you have learned how to unleash thick layers of ice through mist by breathing it through your hand. As an action for 8 cursed energy, you can send a cloud of freezing mist in a 60-foot cone. Creatures in the said cone must make a Strength saving throw. On a failure, they take 8d12 cold damage and are both restrained and Partially Frozen by the ice. If they fail by 5 or more, they are Frozen. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not restrained or Partially Frozen.

The creature may remake the Strength saving throw at the beginning of its turns, breaking out of the ice on a success.

This technique's damage is increased to 11d12 at 11th level, 14d12 at 17th level, and to 17d12 at 20th level.


#### Ice Moon

Extension Technique (S-1)
At 10th level, you have developed a new extension that boosts your technique. As an action for 6 cursed energy, you can create a large ball of ice that floats behind you. This ball lasts for 1 minute as long as you maintain concentration on it. While it is active you gain the following benefits:

The range of your technique is doubled.
Your techniques deal 2 extra damage dice.
The cursed energy cost of your techniques is reduced by 1, to a minimum of 1.

#### Tokyo School Feature: Cold Environment


**Technique Improvement**

At 14th level, an accurate description of being stuck within your ice is feeling like you’re freezing in a cold mountain with death approaching slowly but painfully. Whenever a creature is within at least X feet (5 times your Charisma modifier, minimum of 1) of ice created by your innate technique, they may suffer the possible side effects of a cold environment described down below:

Extreme Cold. They receive the effects of extreme cold with the DC now being your cursed energy DC, and with the effects happening at the end of each round. After they've left the area of your ice, they can remake the Constitution save at the beginning of their turns to end the condition.

Freezing Winter. If a creature has been affected by the effects of extreme cold for 3 rounds in a row due to your technique, they must make an additional Constitution saving throw when they make the next save for extreme cold. On a failure, they are Chilled. On a success, they are unaffected. They can remake the save at the beginning of their turns to end the condition.

A creature can only receive these effects from one ice at once, even if there's more than one ice within range.


#### Freezing Hell


**Extension Technique**

Finally at 20th level, when you are fighting it feels like an ice age. Cold damage caused by you ignores resistances but not immunities. Also, your ice isn’t vulnerable to fire damage anymore.

Additionally, whenever a creature breaks out of your ice they take piercing damage equal to your Charisma modifier depending on their size.


#### Ice Size

| Size | Prerequisite |
| --- | --- |
| Tiny | Your Charisma modifier. |
| Small | Your Charisma modifier added twice. |
| Medium | Your Charisma modifier added thrice. |
| Large | Your Charisma modifier added four times. |
| Huge | Your Charisma modifier added five times. |
| Gargantuan | Your Charisma modifier added six times. |
The damage caused by breaking your ice cannot be increased by any methods.


#### Heat Dissociation


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat (S-1)**

Because of your mastery over Reverse Cursed Technique, you have learned your cursed technique reversal. You can now enhance the heat in the air, making creatures boil alive. As an action for 4 cursed energy, you can force a number of targets up to your Charisma modifier within 30 ft of you to make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, they take 8d10 fire damage and receive Second Degree burns. On a success, they take half as much damage and do not receive any burns.

If they fail by 5 or more, they receive Third Degree burns.

This technique’s damage increases to 14d10 fire damage at 17th level and 22d10 fire damage at 20th level.

This technique deals twice as much damage to objects and structures.


#### Pain Killer

`Arata_using_his_technique_on_Yuji_%28Anime%29.gif`
A Pain Killer user stopping someone's injuries, Source [32]
This technique allows you to stop injuries from getting worse, however you do not heal people. 

#### Pain Killer

Lapse Technique (S-1)

Your cursed technique allows you to suppress the harmful effects of certain physical conditions without removing the condition itself.

When you gain this technique, choose one of the following conditions: Bleeding, Burned, Chilled, Frozen, Poisoned, or Shocked. You can use Pain Killer to suppress the chosen condition.

As a bonus action, you can expend 1 Cursed Energy and touch one creature within your reach. If the target is suffering from a condition you can suppress, you suppress that condition for 1 minute. While the condition is suppressed, the target doesn't suffer any damage or other harmful effects caused by that condition.
If a condition isn't removed from the target. When the duration ends, the condition resumes as normal.

Pain Killer can't suppress damage caused by a creature's environment, such as damage from standing in fire or acid.

You learn to suppress an additional Physical Condition at 3rd, 5th, 8th, 12th, and 16th level.

In addition, you gain proficiency in the Medicine skill. If you are already proficient in Medicine, you instead gain expertise in it.


#### Pain-Resistance


**Technique Improvement**

Your cursed energy dulls the body's response to pain, allowing a creature to maintain focus despite suffering injury.

While a creature is affected by Pain Killer, it has advantage on Constitution saving throws it makes to maintain concentration. .


#### Neural Numbing


**Technique Improvement**


Your cursed technique has developed enough precision to affect the mind, allowing you to numb specific portions of a creature's brain and suppress abnormal mental responses.

You can now choose from the following Mental Conditions when selecting a condition you can suppress with Pain Killer: Berserk, Charmed, Confused, Frightened, and Hallucinating.

You learn to suppress one Mental Condition of your choice at 6th level. You learn to suppress an additional Mental Condition at 10th, 14th, 18th, and 20th level.

When you use Pain Killer to suppress a Mental Condition, your cursed energy temporarily numbs the portion of the target's brain responsible for the affected mental response. The condition itself isn't removed, and the target is still affected by the underlying cause of the condition.

#### Advanced Numbing


**Technique Improvement**


Your control over your cursed technique has become precise enough to suppress multiple conditions simultaneously and stabilize creatures on the brink of death.

When you use Pain Killer, you can choose to suppress up to two conditions affecting the target, provided you can suppress each of the chosen conditions. The conditions are suppressed for the normal duration of Pain Killer.

In addition, when you use Pain Killer on a creature that has 0 hit points, that creature is automatically stabilized. The next time the creature would gain a failed death saving throw as a result of taking damage, it doesn't gain that failed death saving throw.

You can use Pain Killer on the creature again to grant it this protection again.

This protection doesn't prevent the creature from dying if it takes damage equal to or greater than its hit point maximum, as normal.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Overclock


**Technique Improvement**

Tokyo School Technique — 14th Level

You can flood another creature's body with cursed energy, temporarily suppressing its natural limitations and forcing it to operate beyond its normal capabilities.

As a bonus action, you can expend 3 Cursed Energy and touch one willing creature within your reach, other than yourself. For 1 minute, the target gains the following benefits:

It gains a +2 bonus to Armor Class.

It has advantage on Dexterity saving throws.

It can take one additional action on each of its turns. That action can be used only to take the Attack (one weapon attack or unarmed strike only), Dash, Disengage, Hide, or Use an Object action.

It can take one additional reaction each round.

This effect requires concentration, as if concentrating on a spell.

When the effect ends, the target's body is overwhelmed by the strain. Until the end of its next turn, its speed is halved, it can't take reactions, and it has disadvantage on attack rolls and saving throws.


#### Pain Nullifier


**Technique Improvement**

Your mastery of Pain Killer allows you to spread its effects across multiple creatures simultaneously.

When you use Pain Killer, you can target any number of creatures within your reach instead of one. Each target must meet the requirements of Pain Killer. You expend 1 Cursed Energy, regardless of the number of creatures targeted.

#### Pain Amplify


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat (S-1)**

Your mastery over Reverse Cursed Technique has granted you your cursed technique reversal. You are now able to amplify the pain of others instead of nullifying it. As a reaction for up to your Charisma modifier cursed energy, you can amplify the damage a creature takes from an attack. You must be able to touch this creature. For every 1 cursed energy you spend on this technique, the target will take an extra damage die. So if a creature gets hit for 8d6 damage and you spend 4 cursed energy, they will take an extra 4d6 damage.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Vengeful Cursed Spirit</summary>
`6igm5od9aqx71.jpg`
Yuta Okkotsu standing with the queen of curses Rika, source [33]
The Vengeful Cursed Spirit technique involves the use of immense amounts of cursed energy while being aided by the spirit of a loved one. 

Removed

</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Blazing Courage</summary>
`ogi-zenin-by-jujutsukaisenfan112-df8makr-fullview.jpg`
Ogi Zenin in a battle stance, source [34]
The blazing courage innate technique allows its user to ignite his weapon in flames or even replace their broken parts with flames. 

#### Blazing Courage

Lapse Technique (S-1)
Starting at 1st level, your technique allows yourself to ignite your weapon in flames, increasing your fire power. You can spend up to your Charisma modifier (minimum of 1) in cursed energy as a bonus action while holding a melee weapon, igniting said weapon in flames for 1 minute. For the duration, the weapon deals an additional 1d6 fire damage on a hit per energy spent, and you emit bright light in 5 feet and dim light in 10 feet.

You can deactivate this at will (no action required).

If you're from Tokyo Jujutsu High or have Maximum Output, you cannot increase the damage of this feature using any of its enhancements or through Maximum Output. Additionally, when using Improved Cursed Energy Output on this feature, you may not pick the benefit that increases its damage.

If you use your Lapse technique while Cursed Weapon Enhancement is active, its damage type is fire instead of force damage.

At 5th level, you now deal 1d8 fire damage per energy spent, this increases to 1d10 at 11th level, and to 1d12 at 17th level.


#### Soulfire Stance


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, your technique comes from your heart, no obstacles shall stand in the way of your blazing flames. If your weapon is suffering any penalties from being broken in some kind, when you activate blazing courage those penalties are removed until the technique ends.

Also, while Blazing Courage is active, you may spend 5 Cursed Energy as an Action to emit an aura of flames within a 10ft radius centered on you that lasts for 1 minute. While the aura persists you have resistance to cold and fire damage, and any creature that enters the ring of flames or starts its turn on the radius must make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, they take your Blazing courage damage dice in fire damage. On a success, they take half as much damage. If they fail by 5 or more, they receive First Degree burns. A creature can only be subject to the saving throw once per round. The user must spend 2 Cursed Energy at the beginning of each of their turns to maintain this technique or it ends immediately.


#### Extensive Flames


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, when you use your full output your flames extend and gain higher power. Whenever you spend the maximum amount of cursed energy you can using Blazing courage, your weapon’s reach increases by 5 feet. Additionally, whenever you miss an attack by 2 or less, the target takes half as much damage that it would have taken from the additional fire damage.

At 11th level, this works on a 3 or less, on a 4 or less at 17th and on a 5 or less at 20th level.


#### Intense Heat


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, your flames burn brighter and stronger than ever, with no enemy leaving unscarred from a combat encounter with you. Whenever you hit a creature with a melee weapon enhanced by blazing courage, they must make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, they receive First Degree burns. On a success, they receive no additional conditions. If they fail by 5 or more, they receive Second Degree burns.

If it was a critical hit, the creature makes the saving throw with disadvantage, and receives Second Degree burns on a failure and Third Degree burns if they fail by 5 or more.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Hell Wave

Extension Technique (S-1)
At 14th level, your technique was simple to understand, though Tokyo believed in your potential to be something more. You have created an extension technique that covers a large area with a wave of flames, destroying everything in its path. As an action for 10 cursed energy while Blazing Courage is active, you force all creatures in a 10 foot cone to make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, they take 11d10 fire damage, are knocked prone and receive Second Degree burns. On a success, they take half as much damage, are not prone and do not receive any burns.

This technique deals maximum damage to objects and structures, It ignites flammable objects in the area that aren’t being worn or carried.

At 17th level, their damage increases to 15d10, and at 20th level it becomes 20d10.


#### Inferno Sorcerer


**Technique Improvement**

Finally at 20th level, your flames have reached their peak, now being seen as a symbol of your unstoppable courage. You ignore resistance to fire damage when dealing damage with your Innate Technique.


#### Freezing Resolve


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat (S-1)**

Your mastery of Reverse Cursed Technique has led to you unlocking your cursed technique reversal. You are now able to imbue your weapon with ice instead of fire. You can spend up to two times your Charisma modifier (minimum of 1) in cursed energy as a bonus action while holding a melee weapon, freezing said weapon for 1 minute. For the duration, the weapon deals 1d6 cold damage on a hit per 1 cursed energy spent.

At 5th level, you now deal 1d8 cold damage per 1 cursed energy spent, this increases to 1d10 at 11th level, and to 1d12 at 17th level.

You can deactivate this at will (no action required).

If you're from Tokyo Jujutsu High or have Maximum Output, you cannot increase the damage of this feature using any of its enhancements or through Maximum Output. Additionally, when using Improved Cursed Energy Output on this feature, you may not pick the benefit that increases its damage.

If you use your Reversal while Cursed Weapon Enhancement is active, its damage type is cold instead of force damage. You cannot use Freezing Resolve on a weapon that Blazing Courage is active on.

Coldsteel Poise At 6th level, you have mastered extending your ice outside of your blade as well as fixing your blade. If your weapon is suffering any penalties from being broken in some kind, when you activate freezing resolve those penalties are removed until the technique ends.

Also, while Freezing Resolve is active, you may spend 6 Cursed Energy as an Action to emit an aura of ice within a 10 ft radius centered on you that lasts for 1 minute. While the aura persists you have resistance to cold and fire damage, and any creature that enters the ring of ice or starts its turn on the radius must make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, they take your Freezing Resolve damage dice added twice in cold damage. On a success, they take half as much damage. If they fail by 5 or more, they become Chilled for 1 minute. Creatures may repeat the Constitution saving throw at the end of their turns to end the Chilled condition.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Earth Manipulation</summary>
`arms.png`
Stone Arms, source [35]
This technique allows you to manipulate stone in various ways. 

#### Earth Manipulation

Lapse Technique (S-2)
Starting at 1st level, your technique allows you to manipulate stone. You are able to place both hands on the ground and shape the stone around yourself. As an action for 3 cursed energy, you may shape the stone around yourself to make 5 different 5-foot by 5-foot cubes of stone. These cubes can originate at any point within 30 feet of yourself and all the cubes must be connected. You can move a creature using these cubes. When creating the cubes, you may choose for them to be created in a path that would push a creature in the direction of their creation. The creature must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they are pushed to the end of the direction the cubes are being made. On a success, they dodge and are moved to an adjacent square. Each cube has an armor class equal to your cursed energy DC and hit points equal to your Charisma modifier times 5.

The number of cubes created increases to 10 at 5th level, to 15 at 11th level, to 20 at 17th level, and to 25 at 20th level.


#### Expanded Manipulation


**Extension Technique**

At 3rd level, you gain access to new ways to manipulate the earth around yourself. Pick one of the following to learn:

Tremor.(S-1) Once per round, replacing an attack in the attack action for 3 cursed energy, you may punch the ground to create a small tremor. Creatures in a 20-foot radius around yourself must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take 3d10 bludgeoning damage and are knocked prone. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not prone.

This damage increases to 4d10 at 5th level, to 5d10 at 11th level, to 6d10 at 17th level, and to 7d10 at 20th level.

Earthen Armor. You have learned how to cover yourself in stone and rock in order to help defend yourself. As a bonus action for up to your proficiency bonus in cursed energy, you may coat yourself with this armor. For 1 minute, you gain a bonus to your armor class equal to half the energy spent(rounded down). The amount you can spend on Earthen Armor cannot be increased in any way.

Shattering Strike.(S-1) Once per round, in place of an attack in the attack action for 1 cursed energy, you create a large rock send it flying at a target within 20 feet. The creature must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take 1d12 bludgeoning damage and are knocked prone. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not prone.

This damage increase to 2d12 at 5th level, to 3d12 at 11th level, to 4d12 at 17th level, and to 5d12 at 20th level.

Sweeping Smash.(S-1) Once per round, in place of an attack in the attack action for up to your Charisma modifier in cursed energy, you send rocks flying in a sweeping motion out in front of you. All creatures in a 15 foot cone must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take 1d8 bludgeoning damage per energy spent and are pushed back 5 times the amount spent feet. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not moved.

At 11th level the limit on how much you can spend increases to double your Charisma modifier.

Wall of Stone.(S-2) As an action for 4 cursed energy, you may make a wall of stone. This wall is made of ten 10 foot by 10 foot panels that are 1 inch thick. Each panel must be touching at least one other panel. The wall has an AC equal to your cursed energy DC and a number of hit points equal to 10 times your Charisma score.

Pulverize.(S-2) As an action for 6 cursed energy, you manipulate the earth to crash down on top of a creature within 10 feet of yourself. The target must make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, they take 5d10 bludgeoning damage and are confused until the start of their next turn. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not confused.

This damage increases to 7d10 at 5th level, to 9d10 at 11th level, to 11d10 at 17th level, and to 13d10 at 20th level.

You may pick one more option at 6th level and once more at 10th level.


#### Harder Stone


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, your stone has grown more durable. The amount of hit points each cube has in your lapse is now equal to 10 times your Charisma modifier.


#### Stone Grasp

Extension Technique (S-2)
At 10th level, you have learned a new way to use the stone you manipulate. You are now able to create two big arms that can catch a target. As an action for 10 cursed energy, you can force a target within 60ft to make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they are wedged between the arms taking 10d12 bludgeoning damage, and are restrained, becoming stuck between the hands until the start of your next turn. On a success, are not restrained and take half damage.

This technique’s damage increases to 15d12 at 17th level, and to 20d12 at 20th level.

You may also keep the hands active, once you have grabbed an enemy using this attack. If you do, then the restrained condition does not end at the start of your turn. Every turn you have it active you can use a bonus action to squish the person stuck between them, forcing them to make a Strength saving throw. If they fail they will be squished taking another 5d12 bludgeoning damage. On a success, they break free. While keeping the hands up holding a creature, you must maintain concentration.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Earthquake

Extension Technique (S-2)
At 14th level, you have learned how to use your technique to cause a violent earthquake. As an action for 12 cursed energy, you may activate this technique. The effects of this depend on the type of environment it is cast on:

Cave, Cavern, or Tunnel. The roof of the location in a 80 foot radius around yourself collapses. All creatures in the the area must make a Strength saving throw to resist the rubble falling on top of them. On a failure, they take 12d10 bludgeoning damage and are restrained. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not restrained. A creature who fails the saving throw can repeat it at the start of their turns to escape the rubble and not be restrained.

This damage increases to 16d10 at 17th level and to 20d10 at 20th level.

Cliffs. The earthquake causes the cliffs to crumble in a 80 foot radius area around yourself, creating a landslide that travels horizontally as far as it fell vertically. For example if it fell 100 feet it would then be 100 feet long. Creatures at the top of this cliff take fall damage based on how far it falls. Any creature in the path of the landslide must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take 12d8 bludgeoning damage and are pushed to the end of the landslide. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not pushed.

This damage increases to 16d8 at 17th level and to 20d8 at 20th level.

Open Ground. In an 80 foot radius around yourself, 1d6 fissures open in locations chosen by the DM. Each fissure is 1d10 x 10 feet deep, 10 feet wide, and extends to the end of the 80 foot radius. A creature standing in a spot where a fissure opens must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they fall in. On a success, they move with the fissure as it opens. If a fissure opens under a building it collapses. Additionally, you can then close the fissures up after creatures have fallen into them. This causes them make a Strength saving throw. On a failure, they take 12d10 bludgeoning damage and are restrained. On a success, they take half as much damage and are pushed out of the fissure as it closes. A creature who failed can repeat the saving throw at the start of their turns to escape from the ground and no longer be restrained. All creatures in the radius who are not standing where a fissure appears must make a Dexterity saving throw or fall prone.

This damage increases to 16d10 at 17th level and to 20d10 at 20th level.

Structure or Building. Any structure or building in a 80 foot radius centered on you, takes 24d10 blugeoning damage. If the structure falls then all creatures inside of within 10 feet of the outside must make a Strength saving throw as the building falls. On a failure, they take 12d10 bludgeoning damage and are restrained. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not restrained. A creature who fails the saving throw can repeat it at the start of their turns to escape the rubble and not be restrained.

The damage the building takes increases to 32d10 at 17th level and to 40d10 at 20th level. The damage creatures take increases to 16d10 at 17th level and to 20d10 at 20th level.

River, Lake, or Marsh. In a 80 foot radius area around yourself, fissures open underwater and drain all the water turning the ground into a muddy and soggy area that traps creatures. All creatures in the area must then make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they are trapped and restrained in the area. On a success, they are unaffected. The water is then pushed back up into the area. This causes all creatures who are stuck to begin to suffocate. They can make a Strength saving throw at the end of their turns to escape being restrained.


#### Earthen Mastery


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, you ability to manipulate the earth around you has grown to its peak. Now when using your lapse the cubes created by your technique are 10 feet, by 10 feet, by 10 feet.


#### Air Manipulation


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

After mastering both Reverse Cursed Technique and your innate technique, you have learned how to use your cursed technique reversal. One major weakness of your innate ability is that all your abilities must come from the ground. Now with your reversal, you are able to manipulate the air. You gain the following usages of this:

Flight. As a bonus action for 4 cursed energy, you gain a flying speed equal to half your walking speed for a number of rounds equal to your Charisma modifier. Every turn after the one you activated this on, you must spend an additional 1 cursed energy.

Gale. As an action, you can spend up to two times your Charisma modifier in cursed energy to send a large gust of wind in a 15 foot wide and 100 foot long line. All creatures in the area must make a Strength saving throw. On a failure, they will be pushed back an amount of feet equal to the amount spent times 10 and are knocked prone. On a success, they are pushed back half as far and are not prone.

Air Slam. As an action for 10 cursed energy, you can create a large hand in the air made out of your cursed energy. You may create this hand up to 120 feet away from you. The hand occupies a 15 foot radius area and targets creatures directly below it. Creatures under it must make a Strength saving throw as the hand slams them toward the ground. On a failure, they take your 4d12 bludgeoning damage for every 20ft they fall before they hit the ground and are made prone. On a success, they take half damage and are not knocked prone.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Paralyzing Sight</summary>
`Maki_is_restrained_by_Ranta%27s_technique.png`
A woman being affected by the Paralyzing Sight technique, source [36]
The paralyzing sight technique allows its user to paralyze people just with their vision. 

#### Eyes

Due to your technique, your eyes are extremely powerful, surpassing most sorcerers' and thus are reinforced.

Eye Hit Points. Your eyes have a number of hit points equal to 1/3rd of your hit point maximum (rounded up). You regain 1 eyes hit point at the beginning of your turns, unless they're at 0 hit points. Any kind of hit point regains you receive affects, your eyes' hit points as well.

Your eyes regain all of their hit points at the end of a short or long rest.

Losing Eye Hit Points. Whenever a hostile creature (or a creature that isn't your ally), succeeds on a saving throw against your technique, your eyes lose 2 hitpoints for every 1 they rolled above your cursed energy DC (minimum of 1). If your Eye hit points are reduced to 0 and there's any excess hit point loss left, you lose an amount of hit points equal to the excess amount.

If your eyes reach 0 hit points, you won't be able to regain its hit points unless you receive the benefits from Reverse Cursed Technique, Regeneration or take a long rest. If your eyes have 0 hit points, you are blinded.

Eye Of The Beholder. At 10th level, due to the constant exposure of strain, your eyes have gotten tougher and more durable. Your eyes' hit points increase to 1/2 of your maximum hit points (rounded up).

In addition, whenever your eye Hit points reach 0 hitpoints, you can make a DC20 Constitution saving throw. On a success, they are reduced to 1 hit point instead.

You can use this feature a number of times equal to your proficiency bonus. You regain all uses on a short or long rest.


#### Paralyzing Sight

Lapse Technique (S-2)
Starting at 1st level, you can paralyze people with your sight. You envelop them in an aura that physically restrains them. As an action for 3 cursed energy, you force a creature you can see within 60 feet to make a Strength saving throw. On a failure, they are restrained until the beginning of your next turn. On a success, they are not restrained. You cannot use your cursed technique if you're blinded

This range increases to 120 feet at 5th level, to 180 feet at 11th level, to 240 feet at 17th level, and to 300 feet at 20th level.


#### Improved Eyes


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, your paralyzation becomes faster. You can now use your paralyzing sight as a bonus action, and now gain advantage on saving throws against being blinded.


#### Concentrated Paralyze


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, you have learned how to make your paralyzation last for longer amounts of time in exchange for standing still. When you use your Paralyzing Sight, you pay 3 additional cursed energy to enter a deep concentration state, making the creature become restrained for 1 minute. The creature must make a Strength saving throw at the beginning of their turns, ending the condition earlier on a success. You count as concentrating as if you were concentrating on a spell and you must maintain the S-2 components from Paralyzing Sight while concentrating.

If the creature manages to break free before the duration, you take great backlash on your eyes. Your eyes lose 2 hitpoints for every 1 rolled above your cursed energy dc(minimum of 1) times the amount of rounds it took for them to break free. For example, if they broke free after 5 rounds and rolled a 18 against your 16 dc, you lose 10 eye hit points.


#### True Paralyzation


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, your technique has evolved to new heights. The creatures who fail your paralyzing sight saving throw now become paralyzed instead of restrained. When paralyzed by this technique, you do not automatically fail the Strength saving throw to break free from the technique.

At 17th level, they also become paralyzed when you use concentrated paralyze.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Object Paralyzing


**Technique Improvement**

At 14th level, you are now able to paralyze objects. When you use your lapse technique, you can choose an object to be the target. When you do this, the object cannot move until the beginning of your next turn.

Additionally, you can use your lapse in this way as a reaction to a projectile targeting you. You can cast your technique as a reaction and make a Charisma saving throw. If the save is higher than the attack roll or DC of the projectile, then it is halted midair. It falls to the ground at the start of your next turn.


#### Enduring Body


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, the constant usage of your technique has led to your body growing used to the strain. When you or your eyes lose hitpoints due to your technique, you may reduce the amount of hitpoints lost by your Constitution modifier added twice. Additionally, you may reduce the somatic component of the technique by 1 (to a minimum of 1).


#### Hyperactive Sight


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

With your mastery over Reverse Cursed Technique, you have learned your cursed technique reversal. You have now obtained the ability to turn your Paralyzing Sight into Hyperactive Sight. As an action (S-2) for 5 cursed energy, you target a creature within your sight range and then accelerate their system with your bare eyes. For the next minute, the creature affected by your Hyperactive Sight has its speed and number of reactions doubled, and it also gains a free action to take the Disengage or Dash action. You count as concentrating as if you were concentrating on a spell while keeping this bonus up. After 1 minute has passed, you need to succeed on a Constitution saving throw, on a failed save you gain one level of exhaustion and you cannot use Hyperactive Sight against the same target for the next round, on a success nothing happens.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Missile Fists</summary>
`Jinichi_Zenin%27s_technique.png`
A missile fists user using his innate technique, source [37]
The missile fists technique allows you to launch a barrage of giant fists against your opponents. 

#### Missile Fists

Lapse Technique (S-1)
At 1st level, you can unleash massive fists as a method of attack. Once per round, when throwing an unarmed strike as part of the attack action for 2 cursed energy, you are able to create a large fist that is then launched at a target within 60 feet of you. Make a ranged cursed energy attack roll against a target in range. On a hit, the fist deals 2d8 bludgeoning damage. You may also launch the fist by replacing an attack in the attack action for 2 cursed energy without throwing a strike.

This damage increases to 3d8 at 5th level, to 4d8 at 11th level, to 5d8 at 17th level, and to 6d8 at 20th level.


#### Detonating Assault


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, your onslaught becomes destructive. Whenever you hit a creature with one of your summoned fists, creatures within 5ft of the impact must make a Constitution saving throw or take half of the damage caused by the fist as thunder damage.


#### Unrelenting Fists

Extension Technique (S-1)
At 6th level, you can unleash a wave of fists that attack enemies in front of you. As an action for 6 cursed energy, you can shoot these fists in a 20 foot cone. All creatures in the area must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take 6d10 bludgeoning damage. On a success, they take half as much damage.

This technique deals double damage against objects and structures.

The damage of this technique increases to 8d10 at 11th level, to 10d10 at 17th level, and to 12d10 at 20th level.


#### Cataclysmic Barrage

Extension Technique (S-1)
At 10th level, you have learned another way to use your technique. You are able to unleash a massive amount of fists from the sky that rain down on your enemies. As an action for 10 cursed energy, you can select a point within 60 feet. All creatures in a 30 foot radius centered on that point must make a Strength saving throw. On a failure, they take 13d12 bludgeoning damage and are knocked prone. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not prone.

This technique deals double damage against objects and structures.

The damage of this technique increases to 16d12 at 17th level, and to 19d12 at 20th level.

Tokyo Feature: Titan's Grasp
Extension Technique (S-1)
At 14th level, your mastery of Missile Fists allows you to summon a massive fist capable of manipulating your surroundings and controlling the battlefield. As a bonus action for 6 Cursed Energy, you can conjure a giant spectral fist within 60 feet of you, which lasts for 1 minute or until you dismiss it. This fist can perform the following actions:

Grasping Crush. (S-1) The fist attempts to restrain a creature within range. Make a melee cursed energy attack roll contested by the target’s Strength (Athletics) or Dexterity (Acrobatics) check. On a success, the creature is restrained, and the fist holds them in place until the start of your next turn.

Barrier Fist. (S-1) As a reaction to an attack targeting you or an ally within 5ft, you may move the hand to cover you or them, granting three-quarter cover until the start of your next turn. Additionally, any damage that hits you while covered by the hand is reduced by your Charisma modifier added twice.

Fist Lift. (S-1) You can use the fist to lift or move a creature or object within range. The fist can carry a creature or object weighing up to 5 times your carrying capacity, moving it up to 30 feet to a point within the fist’s range. If the target is unwilling, they must succeed on a Strength saving throw to avoid being moved.


#### Devastating Fists


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, your Lapse Technique has reached its pinnacle, allowing you to summon immense fists with unparalleled efficiency. If you miss the ranged cursed energy attack roll when using your lapse technique, you may reroll it for 1 cursed energy attack roll. This may be used only once per fist. Additionally, when a creature is hit by an attack roll or fails the saving throw from your lapse technique or from Unrelenting Fists, they are knocked back an amount of feet equal to 5 times your Charisma modifier.


#### Foot Shield


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

After mastering the Reverse Cursed Technique, you gain a defensive ability. As a reaction to being attacked, you can spend an amount of Cursed Energy up to your Charisma modifier added four times to summon a large foot in front of you. The foot has hit points equal to 10 times the amount of Cursed Energy spent, and its Armor Class is equal to your Cursed Energy Save DC. The foot absorbs damage in place of you, automatically failing any saving throws. This foot lasts until its hit points are depleted or until the beginning of your next turn.

At 17th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier added five times and at 20th level, you can spend up to your Charisma modifier added six times.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Immortality</summary>
`24444b0a5e4a525b64298779517a2612.jpg`
Tengen, the immortal sorceress, source [38]
This innate technique makes the user immortal to age, although it forces the user to fuse itself with a vessel to stop it from evolving. 

#### Multifaceted

This Innate Technique is quite limiting in its capabilities. As such, Immortality only provides its Lapse Technique as a feature. After level one, a character should then gain a Sorcerer Path, which they will level in as they progress for each level after the first. For example, a 20th level character would possess one level in Immortality, and 19 levels in the Sorcerer Path. If you reach Epic Levels, then as normal, the Subclasses will not continue to level past 20.

Design Note: When using the Sorcerer Path + Innate Technique rule, the character would still gain a sorcerer path of their own as well as the Immortality technique, and after level one, choosing another Sorcerer Path, which would be one level behind their other regular Path.


#### Immortality


**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 1st level, your innate technique has made you immortal. You're immune to death by old age. You however continue aging and magical aging can affect you.

Every 500 years that pass, you must assimilate yourself with a Star Plasma Vessel. To assimilate is an 1 hour ritual, in which you and the vessel must be touching each other. After the hour passes, the vessel will die and become one with you, granting you another 500 years.

In case you do not make the ritual in time, you will "evolve", becoming something that isn't curse or human. Your race will change to Cursed Spirit, however you won't gain one of its racial traits or qualify for its feats. You will have a more curse like appearance, although keeping a humanoid form. You keep the Ability Score Increase, the walking speed and the size traits from your old race.


#### Mortality

Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique feat.

You have learnt the reversal of your technique, being able to invert the capability of being immortal. As a bonus action for 5 cursed energy, you are able to negate the effects of your lapse technique, making you vulnerable to death by old age once more for 1 minute.

In the case of where the ritual failed and the process of turning into humanity's enemy is accelerated, the effects of turning into a cursed spirit is halted until you disable the reversal.

At the end of the 1 minute, you may pay 1 cursed energy to extend the duration by another minute.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Love Rendezvous</summary>
`7c03098d26e6f4f835105f7652522843@2x.jpg`
A Love Rendezvous user, source [39]
Love Rendezvous is a technique based on the Southern Cross. It marks creatures or objects with cursed energy and makes the lowest star have to pass through the other ones before they can move. 

#### Love Rendezvous

Lapse Technique (S-1)
Starting at 1st level, you have a very complex technique, and you have learned the basics of how to use it. As a free action when you touch a creature or object(this can be done with yourself) that has cursed energy or as part of hitting an unarmed strike, you can spend 1 cursed energy to mark the target's cursed energy with a star of your choice. Only one star can be placed in one creature's cursed energy at a time, however the same star can be applied to different people's cursed energy at an time. You can mark a number of creatures with the same star up to your Charisma modifier. If you mark a creature, everything that has their cursed energy will be marked as well, like shikigami, cursed tools etc. The following stars are available to mark: ★Imai, ★Acrux, ★Mimosa, ★Ginan and ★Gacrux.

When you mark a creature, a sparking light will appear above their head. They can also find the star's name marked on their body if they make a Wisdom (Perception) check against your Cursed Energy DC.

Anyone appointed with a star must follow a sequential order if they want to attempt approaching another star. When they make their movement, they must move directly towards the next star in order and touch them, which allows them to move on to the next star in order. Failing to do this will result in them being unable to close the distance towards their target however they still expend any movement until the technique is disabled and the objects and creatures marked with the same star will be attracted to the point with the highest cursed energy, being all rapidly thrown at that point. The highest cursed energy amount will take 1 dice of bludgeoning damage per 5ft. the other stars have travelled to a maximum of 100ft and their size, while the objects and creatures that crashed into it will take half as much damage. If the creature goes in the correct order, nothing happens and the stars they've touched disappears and upon touching all stars in the correct order, the stars on them disappears. See the following chart for measure of damage:

| Size | Damage Die |
| --- | --- |
| Tiny | d4 |
| Small | d6 |
| Medium | d8 |
| Large | d10 |
| Huge | d12 |
| Gargantuan | 2d12 |
However the amount of dice that can be gathered is capped. You cannot gain more than 5 damage dice. At 5th level,you cannot gain more than 9 damage dice. At 11th level, you cannot gain more than 13 damage dice. At 17th level, you cannot gain more than 20 dice.

The order of the stars is as follows: ★Imai → ★Acrux → ★Mimosa → ★Ginan → ★Gacrux.

If a creature has shikigami summoned, the shikigami are marked with the same star as their summoner. If a creature marked with a star attempts to target you out of this order with a cursed energy attack roll, or if it forces you to make a saving throw against a feature, the cursed energy cannot close the distance between you and the attack. Because the cursed energy cannot reach you, it is immediately pulled back toward its source and targets the creature that created it instead. The creature then rolls against its own AC for attack rolls or its own DC for saving throws. You take no damage as long as the attacking creature violates the star order.

For example, if an ★Imai attempts to target a ★Ginan with Cursed Energy Ray without first targeting ★Acrux and then ★Mimosa, the cursed energy ray cannot reach the ★Ginan. It snaps back and targets the ★Imai instead until the correct order is followed.

If there is no possible path for a target to follow the order, the target isn’t affected by the technique until such a possible path is created. The target is immediately affected again once such a path exists. For example, if Gacrux is placed in front of Ginan for a creature marked with Acrux, then the technique no longer functions for that creature, as there is no way to touch Ginan without approaching Gacrux. Once the creature passes Gacrux, thus opening a path for them to get to Ginan, the technique functions for them once more, and they must touch Ginan before approaching Gacrux.

The stars last for 10 minutes or until you remove one or more of them at will. This technique functions properly while each active star is within 90 feet of the next star in the sequence. If an active star isn't within the range, the technique stops functioning for that specific star.

At 5th level, they need to be within 120ft of the next star in sequence. At 11th level, they need to be within 150ft. At 17th level, they need to be within 180ft.

Creatures won't know they're being affected by the technique until they suffer the consequences from not following the order. After that, they can make the perception check to find about the star. After they found out about the star, they can make a Wisdom (Insight) check with disadvantage against your cursed energy DC to try to understand your technique unless they have the Cursed Energy Tracker feature or are familiar with the southern star constellation, which then makes them roll normally.

In addition, your craftiness in using your technique has led you to utilize cursed energy infused objects to mark with. You will gain the Object Infusion option from the Advanced Energy Usage feature early, however, you will only gain the ability to imbue objects that are Tiny, Small, or Medium size from the feature. If you ever gain the feature by normal means, you will gain its full capabilities instead.

Effective Marking. At 5th level, you learned how to mark creatures in a quicker and more effective manner. You may mark creatures with stars as a reaction. Also, you gain a number of additional reactions equal to your Charisma modifier that can only be used to mark other creatures.


#### Gravity Force


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, your technique's attraction has grown stronger. The damage of creatures colliding now overcomes resistances but not immunities to bludgeoning damage.

Additionally, when creatures marked with stars collide due to your technique, they take additional damage equal to the damage die of their size.


#### Shining Stars!


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, you became better with your technique, now being able to have more stars. The amount of stars you can put of the same type is increased to your Charisma modifier added twice.


#### Cosmic Pull


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, your understanding of the gravitational forces between the stars deepens, enhancing the way your marked targets interact. When creatures marked with stars collide due to the attraction of your technique, they must make a Strength saving throw against your Cursed Energy DC. On a failed save, the affected creatures are also knocked prone and have disadvantage on their next attack roll or saving throw.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Shooting Stars

Extension Technique (S-1)
At 14th level, having to touch your opponents has become a very hard task as you've started to face tougher opponents, so you developed a method to counter this. As a bonus action, you can spend 4 cursed energy to target 4 creatures within 30ft. of you and throw small stars at them. Make a ranged cursed energy attack roll. On a hit, they are marked with one star of your choice.


#### Universal Force


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, your technique attraction feels like a black hole. Marked creatures and objects now take force damage instead of bludgeoning damage when they're attracted to each other. It still overcomes resistances but not immunities.

Additionally, the number of creatures you can mark with the same star is increased to thrice your Charisma modifier.


#### Hateful Separation


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat (S-1)**

Because of your mastery over Reverse Cursed Technique, you have unlocked your cursed technique reversal, now making your technique repel instead of attract. When you use Love Rendezvous, you may spend 2 cursed energy to instead turn it into Hateful Separation, causing the order of the starts to be inverted.

Anyone appointed with a star must follow a reversed sequential order if they want to attempt approaching another star. When they make their movement, they must move directly toward the previous star in order and touch them, which allows them to move on to the next star in reverse order. Failing to do this will result in them being unable to close the distance toward their target, though they still expend any movement until the technique is disabled. The objects, creatures, and cursed energy marked with the same star will be repelled toward the creature with the lowest cursed energy, being all rapidly launched toward that point.

The creature with the lowest cursed energy will take 2 dice of bludgeoning damage per 10ft the other stars have traveled and their size, while the objects and creatures that are repelled and crash into the target will take half as much damage. If the marked creature goes in the correct reversed order, nothing happens, and the stars they've touched disappear. Upon touching all stars in the reversed order, the stars on them disappear. See the following chart for the measure of damage:

| Size | Damage Die |
| --- | --- |
| Tiny | d4 |
| Small | d6 |
| Medium | d8 |
| Large | d10 |
| Huge | d12 |
| Gargantuan | 2d12 |

#### The reversed order of the stars is as follows:


★Gacrux → ★Ginan → ★Mimosa → ★Acrux → ★Imai.**
If a creature has shikigami summoned, the shikigami are marked with the same stars as the summoner's. If a creature marked with a star attempts to target you in the incorrect reversed order with a cursed energy attack roll or forces you to make a saving throw against a feature, the attack or feature is repelled toward the creature with the lowest cursed energy. For attack rolls, the attacker instead targets the creature with the lowest cursed energy, rolling against their AC. For saving throws, the repelled effect forces the attacker to roll against the DC of the creature with the lowest cursed energy.

As long as the cursed energy of the feature belongs to the mark, the feature cannot close the distance between the attacker and their intended target, allowing the intended target to take no damage from the attack as long as the reversed sequence is violated.

If an ★Gacrux attempts to target ★Acrux with a cursed energy ray without first moving through ★Ginan and ★Mimosa, the cursed energy ray will instead be repelled toward the creature with the lowest cursed energy in the area. This repulsion continues until the attacker follows the reversed sequence correctly.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Jet Plane Hair</summary>
`Hanyu%27s_cursed_technique.png`
A Jet Plane Hair user flying with her hair, source [40]
The Jet Plane Hair technique allows its user to fly using just their hair. 

#### Jet Plane Hair


**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 1st level, your technique allows you to transform your hair into a jet plane, granting you flight capabilities. As a bonus action for 2 cursed energy, you gain flying speed equal to your walking speed for 1 minute. You can keep flying in the air. While you are flying with this technique, you can take the dash action as a bonus action. At the end of the minute, you can spend 1 additional cursed energy to keep flying for 1 additional minute.


#### Boost


**Extension Technique**

At 3rd level, you have learned how to use the impulse of your technique to go even faster, destroying anything in the way. While Jet Plane Hair is active, you can use a bonus action to spend an amount of cursed energy up to your Charisma modifier. Until the end of your turn, your flying speed increases by 10 feet for every cursed energy spent this way.

While your flying speed is increased this way, you are under the Momentum Damage rule. However, the bonus damage does not require you to move the initial 300 feet before it begins to build. In addition, your momentum does not need to be built up in a straight line, and you can use your Charisma modifier rather than your Strength or Dexterity modifier for the bonus damage added.

As an action, you may pass by a creature while moving with the increased flying speed from this feature. The creature must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, the creature takes damage equal to your current Momentum Damage. On a success, it takes half as much damage. A creature can only be forced to make this saving throw once per round.

Whether a creature succeeds or fails, you may continue flying.

At 11th level, your flying speed increases by 20 feet for every cursed energy spent.


#### Free Bird


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, like a free bird, you can fly in the sky at tremendous speeds. You don't generate opportunity attacks while flying with Jet Plane Hair, and your flying speed is now twice the amount of your walking speed.


#### Expert Pilot


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, your capability to use your technique has grown to quick levels. You may now activate Jet Plane Hair as a reaction to falling, which reduces the damage by 10 times your Jujutsu Sorcerer level.

Additionally, while the technique is active, you may dash as a free action.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Wind Tornado


**Extension Technique**

At 14th level, you create an extension technique that uses your propulsion to make a tornado. As an action for 10 cursed energy, you choose a point within your flying speed range and fly over to said point, forcing all creatures that are within a 30ft. cylinder of that area to make a Strength saving throw. On a failure, they take 14d8 bludgeoning damage and are pulled up in 30ft. in the air, instantly falling at the end of your turn. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not pulled. You end this technique 5ft. close to the area that the tornado happened.

At 17th level, the damage increases to 17d8, and to 20d8 at 20th level.


#### War Jet


**Technique Improvement**

Finally at 20th level, your hair has evolved to become a war jet, turning it into a much more effective weapon. You now gain a flying speed equal to thrice your walking speed while the technique is active, and the damage of the Boost feature now deal force instead of bludgeoning.


#### Submarine Hair


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

Because you have mastered Reverse Cursed Technique, you can now use your cursed technique reversal. You can now use your hair differently. You can now turn your hair into a submarine. As a bonus action for 4 cursed energy, you gain a swimming speed equal to your walking speed for 1 minute. While you are swimming with this technique, you can take the dash action as a bonus action. At the end of the minute, you can spend 1 additional cursed energy to keep swimming for 1 additional minute. In addition, the air inside of your hair allows you to breathe underwater for an amount of time equal to your Charisma modifier in minutes. You may not have both your lapse and reversal activated at the same time. You may only have one at a time and can swap between them by paying the cost of activation.

As an action for 4 cursed energy while submarine hair is active, you can shoot 2 missiles. Each missile travels in a 30ft. Line and explodes at the end into a 15ft. circle. Each creature in one of the circles must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take 4d8 fire damage. On a success, they take half as much damage.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Helicopter Hair</summary>
`heli.png`
A user of Helicopter Hair, source [41]
This technique allows you to make your hair into helicopter blades. 

#### Helicopter Hair


**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 1st level, your technique allows you to transform your hair into helicopter blades, allowing you to fly. As a bonus action for 2 cursed energy, you gain a flying speed equal to your walking speed for 1 minute. You can keep flying in the air. While you are flying using this technique, you can take the dash action as a bonus action. At the end of the minute, you can spend 1 additional cursed energy to keep flying for 1 additional minute.


#### Helicopter Blades


**Extension Technique**

At 3rd level, you have learned how to extend the blades of your helicopter hair. It can now be used to slice people. As an action for 3 cursed energy, you can extend your blades and attempt to slash an enemy. You make a melee cursed energy attack roll against one enemy within 5ft of you. On a hit you deal 4d8 slashing damage.

This technique's damage increases to 6d8 at 5th level, to 8d8 at 11th level, to 10d8 at 17th level, and to 12d8 at 20th level.


#### Blade Rush


**Extension Technique**

At 6th level, you have learned a new way to use your hair. Instead of using it to fly upwards, you can now turn it on its side to fly towards an enemy. As an action for 8 cursed energy, you dash forward in a 10ft wide and 60ft long line. Enemies in this line must make a Dexterity saving throw, taking 8d10 slashing damage. On a success, they take half as much damage.

This technique’s damage deals double as much damage against objects and structures. This technique's damage increases to 11d10 at 11th level, to 14d10 at 17th level, and to 17d10 at 20th level.


#### Fortunate Son


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, you have begun to master your hair technique. You now don’t generate opportunity attacks while flying with your helicopter hair, and your flying speed is twice the amount of your walking speed. In addition, when using your helicopter blades your range increases to 10ft.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Wind Tunnel


**Extension Technique**

At 14th level, you have learned a new way to use your blade rush. Instead of rushing forward, you now blast air in the same direction. As an action for 3 cursed energy, you can blast the air in front of you in a 10ft wide and 120ft long line. Creatures in the targeted area must make a Strength saving throw or be pushed back to the end of the 120ft. Creatures pushed back in this fashion take 2d6 bludgeoning for every 10ft traveled. On a success, they are pushed back only 60ft and do not take damage.


#### Black Hawk


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, your hair has improved again. Your walking speed now triples when turned into a flying speed. Your helicopter blades have also become better. They now have a range of 15ft and you can attack twice when using them.


#### Airboat Hair


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

Because you have mastered Reverse Cursed Technique, you can now use your cursed technique reversal. You can now use your hair differently. You can now turn your hair into an airboat. As a bonus action for 4 cursed energy, you gain a faster walking speed equal to double what your flying speed would be. Using this form you can ignore difficult terrain and you do not provoke opportunity attacks. This form lasts for 1 minute. While this technique is active, you can take the dash action as a bonus action. At the end of the minute, you can spend 1 additional cursed energy to keep this technique active for 1 additional minute.

While this technique is active, you can spend 4 cursed energy while taking the dash action to force any creatures you pass by to make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take 4d8 bludgeoning damage and are knocked prone. On a success, they take half damage and are not prone. A creature can only be subject to the saving throw once per turn.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Sugar Manipulation</summary>
`sugar.png`
A user of the sugar manipulation technique creating a flan, source [42]
This technique allows you to increase your sugar levels and create it into a physical form, this can also be used on other people but over-usage can lead to hypoglycemia. 

#### Creation of Sweets

Lapse Technique (S-1)
Starting at 1st level, you can increase the sugar levels in your own body and output it into a physical form. As a bonus action for up to 2 cursed energy, you can create a dessert food of your choice. This food can be eaten by up to your proficiency bonus people. As a bonus action, someone can eat your dessert food. When someone eats the dessert they gain temporary cursed energy equal to the amount spent. A person can only benefit from eating 1 dessert food at a time. The amount you can spend on this feature increases, to 4 cursed energy at 5th level, to 6 cursed energy at 11th level, to 8 cursed energy at 17th level, and to 10 cursed energy at 20th level.

There are downsides to using too much sugar. If you spend more than your constitution score in cursed energy on your innate technique in less than a minute, then you will have low blood sugar. While you have low blood sugar, your speed is decreased by 5 feet and you gain a level of exhaustion. If you want to spend more cursed energy on your technique after your blood sugar has been lowered you must make a DC 20 Constitution saving throw. On a success, you use your technique as usual. On a failure, your speed decreases by 5 feet again and you gain another level of exhaustion. After a minute, your sugar levels go back to normal and you regain your normal speed and you lose the levels of exhaustion. You may eat desserts or items that are full of sugar to cure the exhaustion caused by the technique. If you eat an amount of desserts or items full of sugar equal to your Constitution modifier, you may remove one level of exhaustion caused by the technique.


#### Sugar Control


**Extension Technique**

At 3rd level, you can increase the sugar levels inside of your body and the body of others. As an action for 5 cursed energy, you can increase the sugar levels of yourself or a willing creature within 5ft of you. When someone has their sugar levels elevated they gain these benefits for 1 minute:


#### Their speed increases by 10ft

They gain an extra action that can be used to dodge, dash, disengage, or make 1 extra melee or weapon attack.
When using a cursed energy-fueled feature, its cost is reduced by half of your Charisma modifier (rounded down), to a minumum of 1.
After a minute the elevated sugar levels end and the creature will gain a level of exhaustion.


#### Pudding Pad


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, you have now learned how to make a much larger dessert. As a reaction for 10 cursed energy, you can create a dessert within 30 feet of you that is 15 by 15 feet in size. This can be used as a reaction to a line or cone that forces a Dexterity saving throw, a ranged attack roll, or someone falling. This cake has 100 hit points and can block damage or decrease the amount of fall damage someone would take.


#### Sugar Rush


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, you have improved the ability to increase the sugar in one’s body. Now when you increase your sugar or someone else, they gain more benefits. As an action for 10 cursed energy, you can increase the level of sugar in yourself or a willing creature within 5ft of you further. Someone with these new elevated sugar levels gains the following benefits for 1 minute:

Their speed doubles.
They gain an extra action that can be used to dodge, dash, disengage, or make 1 extra melee or weapon attack.
When using a cursed energy-fueled feature, its cost is reduced by half of your Charisma modifier (rounded down) to a minimum of 1.
They will gain a +2 bonus to their AC

#### They will have advantage on Dexterity Saving Throws

After a minute the elevated sugar levels end and the creature will gain 2 levels of exhaustion. This feature does not and cannot stack with Sugar Control.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Sweetful Addiction


**Technique Improvement**

At 14th level, your studies at the Tokyo school have allowed to learn a new way of manipulating one's sugar. You are now able to apply 1 buff from your 3rd level feature over a long amount of time. Over a long or short rest for 15 cursed energy, you may apply 1 benefit from your 3rd level feature to a creature. This lasts until the affected creature takes a long rest. You can apply these longer benefits to up to your proficiency bonus people including yourself.


#### Sugar God


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, you have come to understand the true nature of manipulating sugar. As an action for 15 cursed energy, you can increase your sugar on a willing creature within 5ft of you and give them the following benefits for 1 minute:

Their speed doubles.
They gain an extra action that can be used to dodge, dash, disengage, or make 1 extra melee or weapon attack.
When using a cursed energy-fueled feature, its cost is reduced by your Charisma modifier, to a minimum of 1.
They will gain a +2 bonus to their AC

#### They will have advantage on Dexterity Saving Throws

They will have advantage on Wisdom (Perception) checks.
They will gain an additional reaction.
They will do an extra amount of damage on melee unarmed and weapon attacks equal to xd6. With x being your Charisma modifier. The damage type of this extra damage is the same as the attack it is being added to.
After a minute the elevated sugar levels end and the creature will gain 3 levels of exhaustion. This feature does not and cannot stack with Sugar Control and Sugar Rush.


#### Sugar Drainage


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat (S-1)**

Because you have mastered Reverse Cursed Technique, you have unlocked your cursed technique reversal. You can now drain the sugar from people. As an action for 10 cursed energy, you can touch a creature and force them to make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, they will lose sugar until the end of their next turn. They will then be affected by the following:

They gain a level of exhaustion up to three levels of exhaustion.
Their speed is decreased by 10 feet.
Their cursed energy-fueled features have their cost increased by half of your Charisma modifier.
On a success, they are unaffected.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>The Law</summary>
`f22008192e1f8e339af2e5b2719734b7.jpg`
Higuruma, the sorcerer genius, along with his shikigami, Judgeman. source [43]
The law technique is based around using a non lethal domain to give your opponents a fair trial. 
Domain Expansion: Deadly Sentencing (S-1, V-1)

#### Domain Expansion

Starting at 1st level, your Domain Expansion came along with your technique, it is a place where only true justice will thrive. As an action, you open your domain in a 30 ft. radius and you may choose 1 person in this radius to be the target of your domain. Your domain reflects a small courtroom where the user and their target stand across from one another playing the roles of prosecutor and defendant. There are numerous guillotines in the background with chopped-up debris beneath them. This domain is considered non-lethal.

While inside Deadly Sentencing, the sure hit effect makes it so no forms of violence are allowed from any creature, including the caster.

The trial-based cursed technique imbued into the domain allows you to prosecute your target with a shikigami called Judgeman serving as your namesake. On the turn the domain is cast, Judgeman will announce the charges presented against the target, who becomes the defendant, which are actual crimes the defendant may or may not have committed (chosen by the Dungeon Master). The caster, who becomes the prosecutor, is then given an envelope containing evidence against the defendant. Judgeman automatically knows everything about every creature in the domain, including if the defendant is truly guilty, but this information isn't shared with the caster. The verdict of the trial depends only on the arguments presented by both sides.

After the accusation is reviewed, the defendant is given one chance to defend their case. They must use their action to do so. They have three options: silence, confession, or denial. If the defendant chooses to remain silent or confesses, they are automatically found guilty, unless they remain silent after sufficient evidence has been presented to prove them innocent. Choosing to deny allows the defendant to speak truthfully or lie, requiring a Persuasion or Deception (Charisma) check. The Dungeon Master may instead allow the defendant to omit the skill checks and roleplay their defense.

The prosecutor then has one chance to make a rebuttal based on the evidence submitted by Judgeman. They must use their action to do so. The prosecutor may open the envelope and present the evidence submitted, using it to spin a narrative that labels the defendant as guilty. This requires the prosecutor to roll a successful Persuasion or Deception (Charisma) check against the defendant's defense. The Dungeon Master may instead allow the prosecutor to roleplay their rebuttal in favour of rolling a skill check.

After both parties make their statements, Judgeman will deliver a lawful verdict at the end of the current round of initiative. If the defendant succeeds the skill contest or convinces the Judgeman via roleplay, they are found Not Guilty by Judgeman, causing the domain to collapse, you to enter burnout, and you to gain a level of exhaustion for 1 minute.

If the prosecutor succeeds, the defendant is found guilty, the domain ends without causing burnout, and one of the following punishments is applied depending on the severity of the crime (Confiscation for petty crimes, Death Penalty for serious crimes like murder, and both Confiscation and Death Penalty for most heinous crimes, like mass murder. The Dungeon Master has the final say on what punishment is appropriate.):

Confiscation. The guilty target is unable to use their Cursed Technique features for 1 minute. During this minute, all other Cursed Energy fueled features the creature uses cost double the normal amount of cursed energy. In case the guilty target doesn't have a Cursed Technique, they are instead unable to use any cursed energy fueled features for one minute.
