Death Penalty. The worst penalty someone can receive, they will receive the same consequences as the confiscation feature for 2 minutes. However, your gavel will transform into a executioner's sword, acting as a longsword. This sword is a tool of justice, so you can only attack the guilty creature with it. You use your Charisma modifier for its attack and damage rolls, and deals 5d12 radiant damage on a hit and the target must make a Constitution saving throw and receiving one level of exhaustion on a failure. This radiant damage overcomes resistances and immunities. After the 2 minutes end, your sword will also disappear.

While under the Confiscation punishment but not Death Penalty, the creature may make an Wisdom (Insight) check against your cursed energy DC as an action. On a success, they figure out your technique and can now ask for a retrial as a bonus action.

Your domain costs nothing the first time you use it in the day, costing 30 cursed energy after it. However, every time you cause a creature to be guilty, you gain an additional free domain use that day, and will gain refinement points as if you have reduced them to 0 hit points. You regain all free domain uses at the end of an long rest. You cannot cast Domain Expansion while a creature is under the effect of Confiscation or Death Penalty.


#### Gavel

Lapse Technique (S-1)
At 3rd level, with your great sense of justice came a technique that defines you well. You will gain a cursed tool called Gavel. You use your Charisma modifier for any of its attack and damage rolls. As a bonus action for x cursed energy, you summon one of the following gavel types:

| Type of Action | Cursed Energy Cost | Properties |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Gavel | 1 | Light, thrown(20/60) | 1d4 + your Charisma modifier bludgeoning damage |
| Staff Gavel | 2 | Versatile | 1d6(1d8) + your Charisma modifier bludgeoning damage |
| Whip Gavel | 3 | Reach | 1d10 + your Charisma modifier bludgeoning damage |
| Giant Gavel | 5 | Heavy, two-handed, special | 2d8 + your Charisma modifier bludgeoning damage |
Whip Gavel. You may replace an attack on your attack action with the whip, making a ranged cursed energy attack roll against one creature within 20 feet. On a hit, the creature must make a Strength saving throw, becoming prone on a failure.

Giant Gavel. Instead of making an attack roll, you may use your action to force every creature in a 5ft. circle in front of you to make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure they take the damage of the Giant Gavel and are knocked prone. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not prone. You can keep pressing the Gavel down on top of the person if they fail the saving throw by 5 or more, causing them to be restrained under the gavel, at the beginning of a creature’s turn while restrained in this way, they must succeed a Strength saving throw or take the full damage again and keep being restrained. If you move from where you initially used Giant Gavel, creatures restrained under it will no longer be restrained.

Your Gavel automatically disappears after a minute passes, until combat ends if in initiative or if you dismiss it (no action required). You may only have one Gavel summoned at a time.

Additionally, it counts as a grade 4 cursed tool. At 5th level, Gavel counts as a Grade 3 cursed tool. At 11th level, Gavel counts as a Grade 2 cursed tool. At 17th level, Gavel counts as a Grade 1 cursed tool. At 20th level, Gavel counts as a Special Grade cursed tool.


#### Facts and Logic

At 6th level, you know you need to be cold and analytical to win your cases. You will gain advantage against being frightened and charmed.

You'll also gain proficiency in the Charisma (Persuasion) and Wisdom (Insight) skills, or expertise if already proficient.

Ruthless Cross-Examination. Once per short rest, when you make a Charisma (Persuasion) or Charisma (Deception) check during a trial, you can roll an additional d20 and choose the higher result, reflecting your unmatched skill in presenting arguments and cross-examining witnesses. This must be used before the results of the roll are known.


#### Sorcerer Advocate

At 10th level, your mastery of the law allows you to manipulate the flow of the trial in your favor. Whenever you use your domain, you gain the following benefits:

Legal Maneuvering. When you make a Charisma (Persuasion) or Wisdom (Insight) check during your domain, you may roll an additional d20 and choose the higher result. You can use this feature a number of times equal to your proficiency bonus per domain use. This must be used before the results of the roll are known.

Evidence Manipulation. You can choose to reroll one of your dice rolls made for evidence submissions during trials once per domain use. You must take the new result.

Intelligent Arguments. Additionally, you gain proficiency in the Intelligence (Investigation) skill. If you are already proficient, you gain expertise instead.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Corruption

At 14th level, you've learnt to win at any cost necessary. You may spend 20 cursed energy to submit fake evidence of your choice to Judgeman when you use your domain. You can only do this once per trial.


#### Perfect Lawyer

Finally at 20th level, you have perfected your lawyer abilities to the absolute maximum. Whenever you roll a persuasion check to submit subpar evidence, you add your Charisma modifier to the roll an additional time.

In addition, all of your gavels deal one additional damage tier of damage.


#### Tools of Crime


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

Your technique is very unique and powerful. It allows you to prove that other creatures are guilty and punish them for their crimes. However, now that you have mastered reverse cursed technique, you have unlocked your technique reversal. It allows you to summon tools that allow you to commit crimes more easily. You use your Charisma modifier for any attack and damage rolls used with these tools. As a bonus action for x cursed energy, you may summon one of the following tool types:

| Type of Action | Cursed Energy Cost | Properties |  |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Lockpick | 2 | Light, thrown(20/60) | 2d4 + your Charisma modifier piercing damage |
| Crowbar | 4 | Versatile | 2d6(2d8) + your Charisma modifier bludgeoning damage |
| Grappling Hook | 6 | Reach | 2d10 + your Charisma modifier slashing damage |
| Giant Bolt Cutters | 10 | Heavy, two-handed, special | 4d8 + your Charisma modifier slashing damage |
Lockpick. While using this special lockpick to unlock any door, you have advantage on the Dexterity(sleight of hand) check.

Crowbar. While using this special crowbar to pry open any container or door, you have advantage on the Strength(Athletics) check.

Grappling Hook. You may replace an attack on your attack action with the grappling hook, making a ranged cursed energy attack roll against one creature within 20 feet. On a hit, the creature must make a Strength saving throw, being pulled 5 feet towards you on a failure. Additionally, you have advantage on any checks used to climb with this hook.

Giant Bolt Cutters. Instead of making an attack roll, you may use your action to force every creature in a 10 foot cone in front of you to make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take the damage of the bolt cutters and are restrained until the start of your next turn. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not restrained. These creatures are only restrained if you continue to hold the Giant Bolt Cutters together with both hands. You may continue to use your action to hold the creatures in place, with the duration being extended until the start of your next turn. If you keep using your action to do this, creatures can make a Strength saving throw at the start of their turns to escape.

If you ever stop holding a tool, it disappears. It automatically disappears after a minute passes. You may only have one type of tool summoned at a time.

Additionally, it counts as a grade 4 cursed tool. At 5th level, Tools of Crime counts as a Grade 3 cursed tool. At 11th level, Tools of Crime counts as a Grade 2 cursed tool. At 17th level, Tools of Crime counts as a Grade 1 cursed tool. At 20th level, Tools of Crime counts as a Special Grade cursed tool.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Claw Technique</summary>
`claw.png`
A claw user, source [44]
Your cursed technique allows you to shift your fingers into dark claws. 

#### Claw Formation

Lapse Technique (S-1)
At 1st level, you’ve learned to form your deadly claws. As a bonus action for 1 cursed energy, you may activate your claws for 1 minute. While active, your unarmed strikes, Cursed Strikes, and Cursed Empowered Strikes deal slashing damage, and when you hit with an attack using your claws, you deal bonus slashing damage equal to half your Charisma modifier (rounded down).

At 5th level, you instead add your full Charisma modifier to the damage. At 11th level, you add your charisma modifier plus half your charisma modifier (rounded down). At 17th level, you add twice your Charisma modifier to the damage.

While your claws are active, you gain both a climbing speed and a burrowing speed equal to your walking speed. You also have advantage on saving throws made to resist being moved against your will, as your claws dig into surfaces for stability.

You can end this technique at will (no action required).

Quick Claws. At 5th level you’ve become more skilled at your usage of your claws. You are able to activate your claws as a free action.


#### Long Claws


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, you have learned to extend your claws for greater reach. As part of activating your Lapse Technique, you can spend cursed energy up to your Charisma modifier, adding 5 feet to your claw reach per cursed energy spent for the duration of your claws' activation. You may also use this feature as a bonus action on your turn when your claws are active. If you use Long Claws again during the duration of your Lapse Technique, the new increased reach replaces the previous reach.


#### Clutch Claws


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, your grasp tightens with the instinct of a beast. When you hit a creature with an unarmed strike while your claws are active, you can spend 1 cursed energy to attempt to grapple the target as part of the same attack. The target contests the grapple using Strength (Athletics) or Dexterity (Acrobatics) against your Strength (Athletics), as normal.

While a creature is grappled by this feature, ranged attack rolls made against them have advantage.

If the grapple ends before the start of your next turn, you can make an unarmed strike against the creature as a reaction.


#### Barbed Claws


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, you’re able increase your claws’ sharpness to a razor-like edge. Once per turn after making a successful attack, you can spend 3 cursed energy to force the target to make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure they are bleeding. On a success they are not affected.

Additionally your unarmed strike tier increases by half your Charisma modifier (rounded down) when you have your claws activated.


#### Tokyo School Feature:Drilling Claws

Extension Technique (S-1)
At 14th level, while in Tokyo, you've developed a special ranged option at the expense of your claws. When you take the Attack action, you can spend an amount of cursed energy up to your Charisma modifier and twice that amount in hit points to replace an attack in the Attack action with a ranged cursed energy attack roll against a creature within 30 feet of you. On a hit, the creature takes 8d8 piercing damage. On a critical hit, the creature must make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, they are now bleeding. On a success, they are not affected.

For every cursed energy spent, you may make one additional ranged cursed energy attack roll by replacing another attack, as you shoot one additional claw. Each claw fired removes one from your hands. If one hand has no claws remaining, you can no longer use this feature with that hand. If both hands are emptied of claws, your Claw Technique immediately ends. Your claws regenerate normally the next time you activate the technique.

At 17th level, the damage of each attack increases to 12d8 piercing.


#### Ultimate Claws


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, you’ve reached the apex of your technique, with claws that can cut through practically anything. Slashing damage dealt by your claws ignores resistance and treats immunity as resistance. If you roll a 1 or 2 on any damage die for slashing damage dealt by your claws, you may reroll that die and must use the new result, even if it is another 1 or 2. Additionally, your critical hit range when attacking with your claws is now 18–20.


#### Claw Deformation


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat (S-1)**

You have now mastered Reverse Cursed Technique and unlocked your cursed technique reversal. Normally you use your innate technique to form claws on your hands, but using your reversal you can instead dull your hands making them very smooth. As a bonus action for 2 cursed energy, you can activate this new technique for 1 minute. While this is active your unarmed strikes do their normal damage in bludgeoning and you gain a reaction. This reaction can be used if you are targeted by a melee attack roll. You must make a melee attack roll back against their roll. If your roll is higher than the creature attacking you, then the attack glances off of your smooth hands and misses.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Contractual Re-Creation</summary>
`45-CR-C.png`
Reggie Star about to burn receipts for his technique activation.
Contractual Re-Creation allows its user to reproduce the subject of any contracts they may find, such as receipts. 

#### Contractual Re-Creation

Lapse Technique (S-1)
Starting at 1st level, you have learned the basics of your technique in materializing things from receipts. You start out with 5 receipts of your choice. To materialize something from a receipt, you must use an action and spend the required amount of cursed energy for the effect. The receipt is then burned and turns into ashes, you cannot use this technique if the receipts are wet. You can summon your materializations anywhere within sight range.

There are four types of receipt effects you can receive depending on what the receipt is about:

| Materialization Size | Cursed Energy Cost |
| --- | --- |
| Tiny | 2 |
| Small | 4 |
| Medium | 6 |
| Large | 8 |
| Huge | 10 |
| Gargantuan | 20 |
Object. You summon a object from the receipt, spending cursed energy equal to its size.

Vehicle. You can summon a vehicle from the receipt, spending cursed energy equal to its size.

Reservation. You can use a reservation receipt for 4 cursed energy, giving you the same effects staying on said place for all of the duration would. For example, a 2 day spa reservation would give you the benefits of a short rest.

Structure. You can create a structure from a receipt for 20 cursed energy.

All things created through your technique last indefinitely.


#### Quicker Summons


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, you became better in utilizing your technique, obtaining a faster activation method. You can now materialize receipts as an action or bonus action.

At 20th level, you can now materialize receipts as an action, bonus action or free action.


#### Multi Summon


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, you became even better with your technique, now being able to use multiple receipts at a time. You can now use a number of receipts at once equal to your proficiency bonus, as long as you're holding them all.


#### Telepathic Control

Extension Technique (S-1)
At 10th level, you have learned how to manipulate your materializations using your mind. You can manipulate objects or vehicles as a bonus action by spending 4 cursed energy per object or vehicle manipulated, with the two working differently.

Objects. You can manipulate a number of objects you have summoned telepathically. You may move them in the air up to your sight range, and can use them normally from afar. After they're used, they disappear.

Weapon. Once per turn you may make a melee cursed energy attack roll with said object if it was a weapon, in one of your attacks on your attack action. On a hit, it deals 6 dice of the weapon's damage type. This increases to 10 dices at 17th level and to 14 dices at 20th level.
Vehicles. You can order a vehicle to ride up to a certain distance, after it does this it vanishes.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Surprise Materialization

Extension Technique (S-1)
At 14th level, you have created a extension for your technique, a triumph if you may call it. As a reaction against any hostile action being taken against you, you can materialize one of your receipts for twice the cursed energy cost in a surprise attack. You force the creature to make a Dexterity saving throw with disadvantage. On a failure, they take 4d12 bludgeoning damage per size of the object. On a success, the creature takes half as much damage.

The objects vanish right after hitting the creature.


#### Master of Receipts


**Technique Improvement**

Finally at 20th level, you have become a master at utilizing your receipts. The cost of materializing things is halved, and now objects you control through telepathic control can be used a number of times equal to your proficiency bonus.


#### Contractual Transformation


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat (S-1)**

You have learned how to reverse your technique, transforming things into receipts. As a bonus action for an amount required of energy depending on the object's size, you can transform it into a receipt as long as it could be realistically sold. You cannot do this to magical objects, cursed objects or to cursed tools, or if a creature is holding the object.


#### The cost of a object is defined bellow:


| Materialization Size | Cursed Energy Cost |
| --- | --- |
| Tiny | 4 |
| Small | 8 |
| Medium | 12 |
| Large | 16 |
| Huge | 20 |
| Gargantuan | 40 |
This reversal is affected by the Master of Receipts feature.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Explosive Flesh</summary>
`explosive.png`
A explosion caused by a Explosive Flesh user, source [45]
The Explosive Flesh innate technique allows you to remove parts of your body while making them become deadly bombs. 

#### Aptitude for Healing

Due to the nature of your dangerous technique, you are better at learning how to cope with that. You will gain the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat.


#### Explosive Flesh

Lapse Technique (S-1)
Starting at 1st level, your innate technique allows you to make any part of your body a deadly bomb. You may spend cursed energy to quickly remove and throw one of your body parts at a specified point. You can remove the body part as some form of action described below, and throw it as part of the same action:

| Body Part | Cost | Action cost | Effect |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Tiny Part(Teeth). | 1 Cursed energy + you lose 2 hit points. | A bonus action | You can throw the tiny body part you removed at any point within 120ft. of you, forcing every creature in a 5ft. circle from the area make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they receive xd6 fire damage and are knocked prone. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not knocked prone. |
| Small Part(Eyes, Ears, Nose). | 2 cursed energy + you lose an amount of hit points equal to your Constitution modifier added twice (minimum 2). | A bonus action | You can throw the small body part you removed at a point of your choice within 100ft., forcing every creature in a 10ft. circle of said point to make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take xd8 fire damage and are knocked prone. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not knocked prone. |
| Medium Part(Hands, Feet). | 4 cursed energy + you lose an amount of hit points equal to your Constitution modifier times 4 (minimum 4). | An action | You can throw the medium body part you removed at a point of your choice within 80ft., forcing every creature in a 15ft. circle of said point to make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take xd10 fire damage and are knocked prone. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not knocked prone. |
| Large Part(Arms, Legs). | 6 cursed energy + you lose an amount of hit points equal to your Constitution modifier times 6 (minimum 6). | An action | You can throw the large body part you removed at a point of your choice within 60ft., forcing every creature in a 20ft. circle of said point to make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take xd12 fire damage and are knocked prone. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not knocked prone. |
After using the limb in an explosion, it will be considered removed. The damage you take from removing a body part cannot be reduced in any way. The x is your Charisma modifier.

The X of this technique increases to 1.5 times your Charisma modifier (rounded down) at 5th level, to 2 times your Charisma modifier at 11th level, to 2.5 times your Charisma modifier (rounded down) at 17th level, and to 3 times your Charisma modifier at 20th level.

The attacks from this technique deal twice as much damage to objects and structures.

Additionally, you do not have to immediately throw your limbs. You may hold on to them for a number of rounds equal to your Charisma modifier. After that time has passed, they lose their effect. You may throw them at any point before that using the appropriate action cost.

You can also throw any smaller body parts as an action instead of a bonus action.

Stronger Explosions. At 11th level, with the extensive use of your technique, your explosions became deadlier. When a creature fails the saving throw of your explosions, they will get the deafened condition for 1 minute. They can make a Constitution saving throw at the beginning of their turns, ending the condition early on a success. Also, your explosions deal three times as much damage to objects and structures and their explosion range is increased by 5ft.

At 14th level, the creature will also gain the blinded condition until the start of their next turn on a failure.


#### Reduced Self-Harm


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, you have learned to minimize the damage you take when using your technique. You decrease the hit point loss from using any part of your body as an explosive by an amount equal to your Constitution modifier (minimum 1 hit point).

At 11th level, this reduction increases to an amount equal to your Constitution modifier added twice.


#### Reactive Explosions


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, your practice with your explosions allowed you to make them in an instant. You may now instantly explode one of your limbs for the same price it would require normally, however only requiring a reaction to receiving or hitting a melee attack roll as the limb explodes on your body. All creatures make the Dexterity saving throw with the circle being centered on you.


#### Another One Bites the Dust


**Extension Technique**

At 10th level, your explosive prowess has reached new heights, allowing you to trigger massive blasts from your removed body parts. As an action, you can spend an amount of cursed energy equal to the cost of the body part in your Lapse Technique to trigger a delayed explosion. Choose a body part you have removed within 30 ft. of you. After you choose the body part, it detonates at the end of your next turn, forcing all creatures within a 20 ft. radius to make a Dexterity saving throw with disadvantage. On a failure, they take fire damage equal to the original amount based on the body part increased by 1 die tier and are knocked prone. On a success, they take half damage and are not knocked prone.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Double Blast


**Extension Technique**

At 14th level, you have learned how to explode one body part to boost the range of another body part. Instead of launching the body part at someone, you may have it explode and boost another body part. When you do this, you must use the action cost for both explosions. The 1st explosion detonates within 10 feet of yourself, and then the second is launched up to a number of feet equal to the throwing range of both body parts added together.


#### Terrorist


**Technique Improvement**

Finally at 20th level, your fame has spread around the sorcerer world, your explosions are a danger to nations. Your explosion radius for every body part is increased by 5 feet, and they cause First Degree burns on a failed Constitution save in addition to the other effects.

In addition, they now deal four times as much damage to objects and structures.


#### Compressive Force


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

After mastering Reverse Cursed Technique, you have learned your cursed technique reversal. You gain the ability to implode the body parts of creatures you touch compressing them with great force. Once per turn for X cursed energy when you make a melee weapon attack you can try to implode the body parts of other creatures. On a hit, they must make a Charisma saving throw. On a failed save, you can choose a body part of the creature to implode, causing damage as per your original abilities. On a success, nothing happens. The Cursed energy cost for this feature is equal to the Explosive Feature’s cost depending on the body part targeted doubled and the damage dice for Explosive Flesh is doubled when using this Technique. The limb that is targeted cannot be the skull, neck, or torso.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Scorpion Hair</summary>
`scorpion.jpg`
A scorpion hair user, source [46]
The scorpion hair technique focuses on having long hair with a sharp tip at the bottom that can be used to sting enemies. 

#### Scorpion Hair


**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 1st level, you have developed a rather weird yet unexpectedly formidable innate technique. Your hair will grow longer, and you must use a large ponytail, or else your technique won't work. At the tip of your hair, there will be a sharp tip, formatted around the form of a scorpion tail. Once per turn as part of your attack action, you can spend 1 cursed energy to replace an attack with your scorpion hair. Make a melee cursed energy attack roll. On a hit, it deals 1d8 piercing damage. Additionally, when making attacks with scorpion hair, your reach increases by 5ft.

At 11th level, you are able to replace more than 1 attack in the attack action with this feature.

At 3rd level, the damage increases to 1d10, to 1d12 at 6th, to 2d8 at 10th, to 2d10 at 17th, and to 2d12 at 20th level.


#### Swift Maneuvers


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, you're becoming experienced in fighting with your hair. You can now do the following maneuvers:

Hair Grab. When you hit a scorpion tail attack, you may do no damage in exchange you can attempt to grapple the targeted creature. If you're grappling a creature with your hair, you cannot attack another creature with your scorpion hair outside of the grappled creature.

Vital Strike. As part of making a Scorpion Hair attack, you can spend 2 cursed energy to aim at a vital point of the target. On a hit, the attack deals an additonal amount of damage equal to the base piercing damage of your scorpion hair.

Scorpion Defense. As a reaction for 4 cursed energy to an attack targeting you, you can make a melee cursed energy roll against said attacker.


#### Scorpion Poison


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, your hair is carrying more and more of the characteristics of a scorpion, having poison imbued in its tip. Whenever you hit a creature with your scorpion hair you can spend an additional 5 cursed energy to force the target must make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, they take 5d10 poison damage and are poisoned for 1 minute. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not poisoned. They may remake the Constitution saving throw at the beginning of their turns, ending the condition on a success.

At 11th level this increases to 6d10, to 7d10 at 17th, and to 8d10 at 20th level.


#### Stronger Poisons and Adept Maneuvers


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, your poison has gotten stronger and deadlier. Whenever someone fails the Constitution saving throw for your poison, they will also be dealt 1d6 poison damage at the end of their turns for 1 minute. They may remake the saving throw at the beginning of their turns to end this condition early.

Improved Maneuvers. Along with enhancing your poison, you've become much more adept at using your hair in combat. You gain the following maneuvers:

Stinger Step. As an action for 4 cursed energy, you may move up to your speed without provoking opportunity attacks. During this movement, you can make one scorpion hair attack against one creature you pass within your reach. This maneuver may also be used with your reversal technique Scorpion Antidote.

Scorpion Stance. As a bonus action for 3 cursed energy, you enter a defensive stance until the beginning of your next turn. Whenever a creature enters your reach for the first time on a turn, you may make 1 scorpion hair attack against them.

Disarming Sting. For 3 cursed energy, when you hit a creature with an attack that was made with your hair, you can force them to make an Athletics or Acrobatics (target's choice) check against your attack roll. On a failure, you can disarm them of one object they are holding.

Deaths Embrace. As a part of grappling a creature, you may spend 6 energy in an attempt to squeeze the life out of them. The target must make a Strength saving throw against your Cursed Energy DC. On a failure, they take your unarmed strike damage and are restrained. The creatures may make a Strength saving throw at the end of their turns to break free, they take your unarmed strike damage at the start of every turn they are still restrained.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Poison of the Scorpion King


**Technique Improvement**

At 14th level, you've figured out how to expand on your poison. Your poison has evolved to become much more deadly, making the body of the target you stung decay. Whenever you deal poison damage with your Scorpion Hair, the target will take an additional amount of poison damage at the start of their turns while they are poisoned by you. This poison damage is equal to Xd10 with X being your Charisma modifier.


#### Death Sting


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, you have finally reached the peak of your poison, making it so deadly that one sting could end your opponent's life. The poison damage dealt by your technique now ignores resistance. Also, if your target fails the Constitution saving throw they will gain one level of exhaustion up to three levels of exhaustion.


#### Scorpion Antidote


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

Your mastery over Reverse Cursed Technique and poisons has led to you developing your cursed technique reversal. You may now use your hair as an antidote instead of a poison. You may spend 10 cursed energy to replace one attack in the attack action with an antidote delivery to a creature within your scorpion hair's reach. You may remove one condition from them that originates from a poison, such as poisoned or paralyzed, as well as granting them resistance to poison damage and advantage in any saving throws related to poison for 1 minute.

At 11th level you may replace more than one attack in the attack action with Scorpion Antidote, expending cursed energy for each attack replaced.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Comedian</summary>
`cf8380cee9021c0284d4525ddae21fff.jpg`
The funniest thing that has ever existed, source [47]
Why would you need an explanation? How about a joke instead? 

#### Humorous Feelings

Your technique is very powerful, but it is not without its drawbacks. In order for any part of your technique to work, you must believe that you are being funny. Every time you use your technique on a creature, they can make a Wisdom(Insight) check against your cursed energy DC. If they roll higher, they learn the secret about your technique. They are then able to use a free action on their turns to try and convince you that you are not funny. They must make a Charisma(Persuasion) check and you make a Wisdom(Insight) check. If you roll higher, you think you are still funny and can continue. If they roll higher, you think you are not funny and can no longer use your technique against the creature for 1 minute. At the end of your turns, you are able to make a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw. On a success, you start to think you are funny again and can use your technique again. On a failure, you still think you are not funny.

Additionally, you may only use your technique to continue being funny. If you use it in a way that is not funny and you know that, then the technique is not able to be used.


#### Comedian


**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 1st level, dude, you're too funny! Not even reality itself is handling how funny you are right now! As a reaction to being damaged for 3 cursed energy, you can activate your comedian technique. This allows you to choose a funny way for the attack to miss or not affect you as much. Such as becoming covered in sauce so attacks slide off of you. When this happens, you take ¼ as much damage from the damage source.

You gain an additional number of reactions for this ability equal to your proficiency bonus.


#### Physical Comedy


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, your comedic bits are on another level! You are now able to manipulate the environment around you in 2 different ways:

Offensive Humor. Once per round, when you attack a creature you may spend 1 cursed energy to make the attack funny. This can add an extra effect to the attack like being able to attack from further away due to being launched forward to land a kick. The effect of whatever you do is determined by the DM.

Prop Gag. As a free action for 1 cursed energy, you can create a number of medium or smaller objects equal to your Charisma modifier within a 30 foot radius. These objects last for 1 minute or until you dismiss them for no action required.


#### Suffering is not Funny


**Extension Technique**

At 6th level, you know that people can’t have fun if they are hurt! As a bonus action for up to your Charisma modifier in cursed energy, you are able to heal 10 hit points per cursed energy spent.

The amount you can heal increases to your Charisma modifier times 2 at 11th level.

Additionally at 11th level, you are able to heal creatures within a 100 foot radius around yourself.


#### Comedy Show


**Extension Technique**

At 10th level, your comedy is getting better! You are now able to change a lot more in your environment to allow your humor to flourish. As an action for 10 cursed energy, you can activate your technique. For 1 minute, you gain the following effects:

Your lapse technique does not need reactions or cursed energy.
Physical Comedy does not cost cursed energy.
Suffering is not Funny does not cost cursed energy.
Additionally, the environment in a 100 foot radius centered on you is completely changed to whatever you choose to continue your comedy show. Once per round at the start of your turn, you can change the environment. The environment can be filled with creatures and objects of your choice. Additionally, a damaging effect of your choice can be created. Such as a truck driving by fast. The damage of this ability should not exceed a number of damage die equal to 3 times your Charisma modifier. The tier and amount of damage die is determined by the DM based on the effect.

After the minute is up, you can spend 3 more cursed energy to extend the duration by another minute.

However, this technique once again has a drawback. If someone succeeds the check to find out your techniques weakness, they also learn this extensions weakness. The weakness is that creatures inside can also shape the environment on their turns.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Self Confidence


**Technique Improvement**

At 14th level, you have realized that you really are funny! You gain proficiency in Wisdom (Insight) or expertise if you are already proficient.


#### Master of Humor


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, your comedy is above everyone else! You now have an amount of extra reactions for your lapse equal to 1.5 times your proficiency bonus. In addition, Comedy Show lasts for 2 minutes instead of 1.


#### Tragedy


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

Because of your mastery over Reverse Cursed Technique and you having finally woken up to reality. You realize that nothing ever goes as planned in this accursed world. The longer you live the more you realize, that the only thing that exists in this reality is merely pain and suffering. You are now able to use the extra reactions from your lapse for your reversal. As a reaction to someone being attacked for 6 cursed energy, you are able to increase the damage they take by ¼. You create a way for them to take more damage due causing them more suffering. You cannot use your reversal and your lapse in the same round as the suffering of others ruins your mood.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Technique Extinguishment</summary>
`11249439a558d58ca929d09bcb5aefb0.jpg`
A technique extinguishment user prepared to fire off a Jacob's Ladder, Source [48]
This Innate Technique bestows its user with the gift of angelic power, capable of smiting those they considered as ‘villains’ and hamper the user of particular target’s cursed techniques. 

#### Cursed Energy Targeting

Technique Extinguishment's effect targets cursed energy directly, and as such, Domain Amplification cannot negate the radiant damage and the effects of Technique Extinguishment from this technique.

Jacob's Ladder
Lapse Technique (S-1)
Starting at 1st level, you have one of the most powerful techniques that have ever existed, having the power to completely nullify jujutsu. As an action for 4 cursed energy, you summon a trumpet of light and blow into it, and you may choose a point within 60ft of you. All creatures within a 10 foot radius of that point must make a Charisma saving throw. On a failure, they take 2d10 radiant damage and cannot use any cursed technique features, and any effects coming from those features they currently have active on them are disabled until the end of their next turn. On a success, they take half as much damage, and don't have their cursed technique features negated. A creature who has their cursed technique negated has to spend double the cursed energy on every feature which costs cursed energy, although this doesn't affect a creature who lacks a cursed technique. If a creature who is under the effects of Technique Extinguishment is killed, should they be revived, they must use the burnout Recovery feature in order to regain their technique.

At the start of each of your turns after using Jacob’s Ladder, you can use your action to maintain the beam by expending 2 cursed energy. When you do so, the beam persists in its radius.

While you're maintaining the beam and a creature starts its turn in the beam’s area, or enters it for the first time on a turn, the creature must make a Charisma saving throw against the beam. On a failed save, the creature takes the original Jacob’s Ladder damage plus an additional 1d10 radiant damage for each turn the beam has been maintained that which cumulates with previous turns, suffers the technique extinguishment of Jacob's Ladder and has disadvantage on the next Charisma saving throw it makes against Jacob’s Ladder. On a successful save, the creature takes half damage and suffers no additional effects.

Maintaining the beam in this way requires concentration.

If a creature is holding a cursed tool, and is hit with Jacob's Ladder, the cursed tool ceases to function until the end of the creature's next turn. A cursed tool only ceases to function if it has a cursed technique within it, for example, the Chain of a Thousand Miles. You cannot cease the function of the Inverted Spear of Heaven.

If the cursed tool effected isn't being held at all, it instead ceases to function until the end of your next turn.

Additionally, you deal twice as much damage with your technique extinguishment against Cursed Vessels, and Death Paintings.

This techniques damage increases to 4d10 at 5th level, to 6d10 at 11th level, to 8d10 at 17th level, and to 10d10 at 20th level.

If you use this technique with Maximum Output, its beam of light range increases to a 15 foot radius.


#### Divine Grace


**Extension Technique**

At 3rd level, you've learned to use your technique in other ways that aren't just pure damage. As a bonus action or as part of entering initiative for 5 cursed energy, you may emit a bright light capable of offsetting your enemies. You count as shedding bright light in a 15 foot radius and all creatures within this radius must make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, they take your martial arts damage as radiant damage and are blinded until the beginning of their next turn. On a success, they take half as much damage and aren't blinded. A creature that moves into that radius for the first time or starts its turn in the radius must make the Constitution saving throw.

The damage from this feature cannot be enhanced in any way.

At the beginning of your turns, you must spend 2 cursed energy to keep this feature active.


#### Angel's Incarnate


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, your cursed technique allows you to manifest wings that resembles that of an angel. As a free action for 2 cursed energy, you may manifest wings on your back that grant you a flying speed equal to your speed for 1 minute and this also sprouts a halo above your head. At the end of the minute, you may spend 1 cursed energy to extend it by another minute.

Additionally, due to the nature of your cursed technique, you may freely walk through barriers such as Basic Barriers and Curtains without needing to destroy them and without being detected by the creator of the barrier. You cannot pass through the Barrier of a Domain Expansion without finding the Barrier's Edge first.


#### Expanded Targeting


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, you've managed to radically improve the range and target concept of your technique. You may now choose a point within 90ft of you when using Jacob's Ladder and all creatures within a 15 foot radius of that point must make the saving throw. If you use Maximum Output, the radius becomes a 20-foot radius.

You may also use this technique as a way to supress cursed objects. As an action for 6 cursed energy, you can summon a trumpet of light and blow into it, making a beam of light targeting a cursed object or cursed tool. Make a ranged cursed energy attack roll against the cursed energy save DC, on a hit you will supress the object causing it to lose all effects for 24 hours, removing any curses that were affecting creatures because of it or removing creatures imprisoned inside. You cannot use this feature on Incarnation based cursed objects such as Sukuna's Fingers.


#### Tokyo School Feature:Jacob's Retribution


**Technique Improvement**

At 14th level, while you were in Tokyo, you managed to improve the effects of your technique’s ability to extinguish cursed techniques. If a creature that has failed the Charisma saving throw of Jacob's Ladder fails another one while under its effects and being forced to spend twice as much cursed energy, they must now spend four times as much cursed energy for any cursed energy related features for the duration of the effect of being unable to use their cursed technique.


#### Celestial Retribution


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, you have ascended to a divine state, significantly enhancing your Technique Extinguishment. The range of Jacob's ladder increases to 120 feet and the radius increases to a 20 foot radius. If you use Maximum Output, the radius becomes a 25-foot radius.

Creatures that are under the effects of having their technique disabled through Technique Extinguishment now also cannot regain cursed energy for the duration through features that recover cursed energy.

Additionally, for the duration of having failed the saving throw for Technique Extinguishment, creatures are now vulnerable to radiant damage.


#### Technique Ignition


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

After mastering Reverse Cursed Technique, you have now unlocked your technique reversal. Instead of extinguishing someone’s technique, you may force them to activate it at a cost. As an action for 8 cursed energy, you may make a ranged cursed energy attack roll against a creature within 120ft of you. On a hit, the creature takes 12d10 radiant damage must make a Charisma saving throw. On a failure, the creature must spend twice the amount of cursed energy on a cursed technique feature of the DM's choice that cannot be reduced and any effects that feature causes fails. On a success, nothing happens.

At 17th level, the creature takes 16d10 radiant damage. At 20th level, the creature takes 20d10 radiant damage.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Star Dictator</summary>
`dhruv-lakdawallas-ct-and-de-theory-v0-smd337t16evb1.jpg`
The shikigami of a Star Dictator user, source [49]
This technique allows you to create an inviolable barrier using your shikigami's paths as a method of attack. 

#### Star Dictator

Lapse Technique (S-1)
At 1st level, you gain the ability to create an inviolable barrier using the path your shikigami traces. As an action, you may summon up to 4 Small Pteranodon Shikigami from your hair by spending 2 cursed energy for each shikigami. Your shikigami are what allows you to define an inviolable barrier using their orbiting pattern and you may have multiple orbits. You may command these shikigami using the Summon Commanding Rules.

When commanding any Shikigami from your technique, you may command a number of them equal to twice your Charisma modifier.

When commanding the shikigami, you may make them forgo all their actions using a simple command in order to move in an orbiting path to form the barrier at any point within 30 feet of you.

Shikigami assigned to the same orbit must remain evenly spaced around a shared center point, forming a continuous orbit. When one shikigami moves along the orbit, the others move with it to maintain their spacing.

When these Shikigami orbit, they create a trajectory of thin moving paths that shift as the shikigami move and you may have a number of trajectories up to the number of shikigami you have summoned. The maximum distance these trajectories can extend from the center point depends on the number of active shikigami:

| Shikigami | Maximum Trajectory Reach | Damage |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 15 ft | 3d6 slashing |
| 2 | 30 ft | 6d6 slashing |
| 3 | 45 ft | 9d6 slashing |
| 4 | 60 ft | 12d6 slashing |
You may choose to have the trajectories occupy a smaller area than their maximum reach. When a creature enters a trajectory for the first time on a turn or when it is intersected by the trajectory as it moves, it must make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, it takes the listed slashing damage and is Bleeding. On a success, it takes half as much damage and isn't bleeding. A creature may only be forced to make this saving throw once per round regardless of how many trajectories intersect it however for every trajectory above 1, the DC increases by 1 to a maximum of half your proficiency bonus, rounded down.

The shikigami cannot immediately form their maximum reach and on the first turn the barrier is formed, the maximum trajectory reach is 30ft or its normal maximum whichever is lower. At the end of your turns, the maximum reach increases by 30 feet up to the maximum reach possible with the maximum amount of shikigami.


#### Mole Rat Shikigami


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, you have unlocked a new type of Shikigami that takes the form of a large naked mole rat that can be used to form your barrier that operates differently. As an action for 6 cursed energy, you may summon a Mole Rat Shikigami that you may control using the Summon Commanding Rules along with your Pteranodon Shikigami. You may only have 1 Mole Rat Shikigami summoned at a time.

When commanding the shikigami, you may cause it to forgo its actions using a simple command to physically trace its path by moving along the ground to create a trajectory loop that is completed when the shikigami returns to its starting point and has a minimum of a 10 foot radius, which forms your barrier along the path.

Once the barrier is formed this way, the path now becomes a persistent trajectory for 1 minute that stay in place and deal damage as per your Star Dictator feature. If a creature starts its turn intersecting a persistent trajectory, it is also forced to make the Constitution saving throw.

Additionally, your Small Pteranodon Shikigami no longer need to maintain trajectory by remaining in orbit as long as a persistent trajectory from the Shikigami exists and can move freely and act normally. You may still create additional trajectories using your Pteranodon Shikigami as normal and any trajectory that exists follows the normal rules of Star Dictator.


#### Efficient Summoning


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, you have learnt how to summon more of your shikigami. When you summon the Small Pteranodon Shikigami, you may now summon up to 6 of the shikigami.

Additionally, if you have 5 or more Pteranodon Shikigami summoned, your Star Dictator feature improves as follows:

| Shikigami | Maximum Trajectory Reach | Damage |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 5 | 75 ft | 15d6 slashing |
| 6 | 90 ft | 18d6 slashing |

#### Improved Shikigami


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, you have improved both of your shikigami. You now are able to summon better versions of them in place of the old ones. As an action for 10 cursed energy, you may summon a Large Mole Rat Shikigami or as action for 4 cursed energy each, you may summon up to 6 Pteranodon Shikigami. These shikigami function the same as the normal versions and you cannot have both the improved shikigami and the normal shikigami versions summoned at once.

Additionally, as your Pteranodon Shikigami are enhanced, their destructiveness is enhanced too. When the Pteranodon Shikigami are summoned, you instead use the following:

| Shikigami | Maximum Trajectory Reach | Damage |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 15 ft | 3d8 slashing |
| 2 | 30 ft | 6d8 slashing |
| 3 | 45 ft | 9d8 slashing |
| 4 | 60 ft | 12d8 slashing |
| 5 | 75 ft | 15d8 slashing |
| 6 | 90 ft | 18d8 slashing |
These shikigami still cannot begin with their maximum trajectory reach. The reach increases over time as described in your Star Dictator feature.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Pteranodon Punishment

Extension Technique (S-1)
At 14th level, you have learned a way to use your shikigami in a direct attacking fashion by sacrificing them. As an action for 15 cursed energy, you may launch all Pteranodon Shikigami you currently have summoned in a 45 foot cone forcing all creatures in the cone to make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, a creature takes 12d10 slashing damage and is Bleeding. On a success, it takes half as much damage and doesn't gain the condition. For every Pteranodon Shikigami after the first one, the damage dice increases by one. When using this feature, you may split it into a number of shikigami up to your Charisma modifier (minimum of 1) which doubles the range and changes it into an attack roll for each split shikigami. When you split Pteranodon Punishment, you may choose to distribute the total damage however you wish across however many shikigami you are splitting. For example, if a Pteranodon Punishment deals 14d10 force damage and you split it into two beams, one of them could deal 7d10 while the other deals 7d10. You could also make one deal 10d10 and make the other deal 4d10. You cannot split this feature if you only have one Pteranodon Shikigami.

After using this feature, the Pteranodons are reduced to 0 hit points.


#### Mastered Barrier


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, you've mastered your technique to the point of perfection. You may now have up to 2 Large Mole Rat Shikigami active at once and up to 8 Pteranodon Shikigami.

Additionally, as your Pteranodon Shikigami are enhanced, their destructiveness is enhanced too. When the Pteranodon Shikigami are summoned, you instead use the following:

| Shikigami | Maximum Trajectory Reach | Damage |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | 30 ft | 3d10 slashing |
| 2 | 60 ft | 6d10 slashing |
| 3 | 90 ft | 9d10 slashing |
| 4 | 120 ft | 12d10 slashing |
| 5 | 150 ft | 15d10 slashing |
| 6 | 180 ft | 18d10 slashing |
| 7 | 210 ft | 22d10 slashing |
| 8 | 240 ft | 25d10 slashing |
These shikigami still cannot begin with their maximum trajectory reach. The reach increases over time as described in your Star Dictator feature.


#### Star Guardian


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

You have now mastered Reverse Cursed Technique, thus unlocking your cursed technique reversal. You have now learned an opposite use of your shikigami. As a part of summoning any of your pteranodon type shikigami, you can double the cursed energy cost to give them a new effect. The action cost for controlling is the same as for the normal shikigami, however, their radius will be halved (rounded up to the nearest 5), and instead of forming a domain, their path forms an anti-domain. While inside of a Domain Expansion, the sure-hit feature and sure-hit effect is negated against the shikigami and creatures inside the shikigami's anti-domain.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Sky Manipulation</summary>
`8498165-3118272879-FM89L.jpg`
A Sky Manipulator using the sky itself as clothes, source [51]
The Sky Manipulation Innate Technique allows the user to manipulate space as if it were a surface, which can be used offensively or defensively. 

#### Sky Manipulation

Lapse Technique (S-1)
Starting at 1st level, your innate technique manifests, making you able to manipulate the sky around you. You are able to fold the sky like cloth which allows you to redirect attacks away from you. As a reaction to an attack roll, you can spend 3 Cursed Energy to add +4 to your AC by distorting the sky for that one attack.

If this technique makes a ranged attack miss, you can make a ranged cursed energy attack roll against any creature of your choice within the original attack's range. For example, if you block a ranged attack with a range of 60 feet, you can pick another target within 60 feet of yourself. On a hit, the target takes the attack’s damage roll. You may also use this feature if the attack roll originally misses without having used the technique to raise your AC.

If this technique makes a melee weapon attack miss, it will render the limb used in the attack useless, making it count as broken until the beginning of the creature's next turn or until the creature moves away from the area where its limb is caught. You may also use this feature if the attack roll originally misses without having used the technique to raise your AC.

If you have taken the dodge action, you may use this reaction on creatures of your choice within your reach.

Energy Redirection. At 5th level, you have learned how to redirect energy back to your opponents. You can now use this reaction when you are forced to make a cone or line saving throw, adding a +4 to the roll. If you pass, you and all other creatures the effect had not reached are not affected, and you force all creatures to make the saving throw from your range, using your cursed energy DC for it. For example, if you were forced to make a line saving throw and pass, you force all creatures in a line starting from you to make the saving throw.


#### Sky Cloth

Technique Improvement (S-1)
At 3rd level, you have become better at manipulating the space around you. As a bonus action for 4 Cursed Energy, you may cover part of your body, grabbing the sky like cloth, making it seem like the parts covered are invisible for 1 minute.

While you have this cloth covering you, you gain a fly speed equal to your speed. You may keep hovering in the air. If an effect would reduce your speed to 0, you start falling.

After the minute ends, you can spend 1 cursed energy to make it last 1 more minute.

Sky Armor. At 5th level, your Sky Cloth allows you to become slightly more durable. While under the effects of Sky Cloth, your AC is increased by half your proficiency bonus, rounded down.

Spatial Landing. At 11th level, your sky cloth protects you from the nastiest falls. As a reaction to falling while having your Sky Cloth activated, you can reduce the damage by 5 times your jujutsu sorcerer level.


#### Shattering Impact


**Extension Technique**

At 6th level, you have begun to master your technique. You have learned that there is another way you can manipulate the sky instead of making it fold like paper. You are now able to freeze the sky. However, this is hard for you to pull off. Once per round on your turn when making a melee weapon attack for 3 cursed energy, you can shatter the sky like ice at the moment of impact. The target takes an additional amount of force damage equal to half the original attack’s damage and is forced to make a Strength saving throw. On a failure, they are knocked directly back a number of feet equal to 5 times your Charisma modifier. On a success, they are not moved.

This technique deals double damage to objects and structures.


#### Thin Ice Breaker

Extension Technique (S-2)
At 10th level, you have developed an advanced extension technique for your sky manipulation. You have mastered the ability to freeze the sky itself like ice, containing any creatures inside. You are then able to shatter the sky, cracking like a thin layer of ice and causing massive damage to those caught inside.

As an action, you can freeze the sky, expending 10 Cursed Energy. You can then shatter the sky, damaging all inside. Creatures in a 10-foot cube originating within 5 feet of yourself must make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, the creatures takes 12d10 force damage and are knocked back 10 times your Charisma modifier feet (minimum 10 feet) and fall prone. On a success, the creatures take half as much damage and are pushed half the distance but remains standing.

This technique deals double damage to objects and structures.

At 17th level, the force damage increases to 16d10. At 20th level, the force damage increases to 22d10.


#### Tokyo Feature: Expert Manipulation


**Extension Technique**

At 14th level, your training at the Tokyo school has led to you learning two new ways to use your technique. They are as follows:

Spatial Movement. You are able to fold space and then unfold it while holding on. This drags you forward with the sky as it unfolds. As a bonus action for 3 cursed energy, you can do this and be moved 30 feet in any direction of your choice. This movement does not provoke opportunity attacks.

Creature Entrapment. You are able to fold the sky over a creature, trapping them in place. As an action for 6 cursed energy, you can try to fold the space over 1 creature within 5 feet of you. The creature must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they are restrained until the start of their next turn. On a success, they are unaffected.


#### Untouchable Sky Guardian


**Technique Improvement**

Finally, at 20th level, you become one with the sky, nothing shall hit you as you are protected by it. The AC you gain with Sky Cloth is increased to your full proficiency bonus.


#### Ground Nullification


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat (S-1)**

With great mastery over the Reverse Cursed Technique, you have found the reversal of your technique. Instead of being able to apply properties to the sky that allow it to be manipulated, you are able to remove properties from the ground. As an action for 6 cursed energy, you may place 1 hand on the ground and attempt to nullify any effects that originate from it within a 60 foot radius of yourself. If any creature has an effect in the radius that shapes the ground or causes it to be different from what it naturally would be, that creature must make a Charisma saving throw. On a failure, the technique or effect causing the unnatural ground will be nullified. On a success, it remains unaffected. If the unnatural effect is not caused by a creature, then it is automatically nullified.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Cursed Energy Discharge</summary>
`ced.jpg`
A Cursed Energy Discharge user firing a blast of cursed energy, source [52]
The cursed energy discharge innate technique allows you to fire directed blasts of cursed energy like a cannon. 

#### Highest Output

Your cursed energy output is incredibly high.

At 11th level, your output has only gotten better with time. You gain the Improved Cursed Energy Output cursed feat.

At 17th level, your output is known throughout the history of jujutsu! You gain the Legendary Cursed Energy Output cursed feat. If you already had said feat, you gain the Divine Cursed Energy Output cursed feat.


#### Pure Output

Some of this subclass's features can be used in technique burnout. This is due to the fact that with or without your technique, your output remains the same. These features are Cursed Energy Discharge and Granite Blast. You cannot split your rays while in burnout, though. However, while in burnout they count as cursed energy manipulation features instead of technique features, and thus they are not affected by cursed technique enhancements such as Tokyo Jujutsu High, Maximum Output, Chanting and others.

Additionally, due to this, you can use those features while in technique burnout without needing to make the saving throw. Any innate technique features still need the saving throw.

Also, due to these features not being only from your technique, you can use them at any other time you are not able to use your technique. They cannot benefit from features that boost your innate technique, like while in burnout when used in this way.


#### Cursed Energy Discharge

Lapse Technique (S-1)
Starting at 1st level, your technique allows you to fire high beams of cursed energy. You gain the Cursed Energy Ray cursed feat. Furthermore, the amount of Cursed Energy you can spend on this feat is doubled. While using the feat with your technique, it counts as a cursed technique feature.

Whenever you are using this feature, you may split it into a number of beams up to your Charisma modifier (minimum of 1 beam). This makes their range double, and you must make a ranged cursed energy attack roll for each. When you split your Cursed Energy Ray you may choose to distribute the total damage however you wish across however many rays you are creating. For example, if a Cursed Energy Ray deals 5d12 force damage and you split it into two beams, one of them could deal 2d12 while the other deals 3d12. You could also make one deal 4d12 and make the other deal 1d12.

Charge. At 11th level, your technique grants you amazing output, however, you’ve found out that by waiting, you can charge even more cursed energy into a blast! Once per turn as a Bonus Action, you may choose to start concentrating on this feature or with Granite Blast. You can concentrate this way a number of rounds up to your Charisma modifier. During the concentration, you cannot use any cursed energy related feature.

When you successfully concentrate for at least one round or your maximum number of rounds pass, you can stop concentrating (no action required) and fire a ranged cursed energy attack roll or saving throw.

For every round you have concentrated this way, the maximum amount of cursed energy you can spend on those features is increased by 2.


#### Blast Manipulation


**Extension Technique**

At 3rd level, you’ve unlocked more uses for your lapse technique. It allows you to manipulate the Cursed Energy Rays you produce. You can use this in the following ways:

Redirective Blasts. Once per turn, when you miss a ranged cursed energy attack roll using Cursed Energy Ray, you can spend 2 cursed energy to reroll the attack roll.

When attacking this way, the attack rolls ignore cover.

At 5th level, you can now do this a number of times equal to your Charisma modifier (minimum of 1) per turn.

Defensive Discharge. You have learned how to use your beams defensively, potentially mitigating damage. Whenever a creature would hit a melee attack against you, you can use a Cursed Energy Ray as a reaction, targeting the attacker. If it deals more damage than the creature’s attack, the attack is negated. The Cursed Energy Ray does not deal damage to the attacker directly, just negating their attack.


#### Barrage of Fire


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, you have learned how to overpower your opponents by sending a barrage of blasts to them. If you make all of your ranged cursed energy attack rolls using your Innate Technique against a single target, the rolls are made with advantage. If the target has a passive perception higher than your Cursed Energy DC, the rolls are not made with advantage.

Additionally, if you make two or more Ranged Cursed Energy attack rolls using your Innate Technique against a single target, the Crit range for all of those attacks is lowered by 1 (This bonus does not stack for every 2 beams).

Lastly, you do not roll with disadvantage on your Ranged Cursed Energy Attack rolls using your Innate Technique even if you are within 5 ft of a hostile creature.


#### Granite Blast


**Extension Technique**

At 10th level, you have created the most powerful technique you have in your arsenal, a blast that takes full advantage of your amazing output. As an action for 10 cursed energy, make a 20/80 ranged cursed energy attack roll against a target within range. On a hit, they take 12d12 force damage. This targets any objects and structures within 5 feet of the target.

When using this technique, you may spend additional cursed energy up to your Charisma modifier, adding a d12 to the total force damage and a +10 to the total range per energy spent.

If you spend at least 5 additional cursed energy when using this technique, you may choose to make this attack a cone, forcing all creatures within range to make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take the full damage and are knocked prone. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not knocked prone.

When the technique is in this form, it deals twice as much damage to objects and structures.

At 17th level, you can spend additional cursed energy up to your Charisma modifier added twice and to your Charisma modifier added thrice at 20th level.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Blasting Knockback


**Extension Technique**

At 14th level, due to the sheer amount of output you have, you can blast people away when you need to. When you use Defensive Discharge and successfully block an attack, the creature whose attack you negated must make a Strength saving throw. On a failure, they are knocked directly back a number of feet equal to 5 times your Charisma modifier. On a success, they are not moved.


#### Perfect Output


**Technique Improvement**

Finally at 20th level, you have learned how to maximize your cursed energy output's efficiency. Whenever you spend 1 cursed energy on a feature related to your cursed technique, you count as if you have spent 2 instead. You cannot use this to make the damage die go beyond what it normally could, if you can only spend 4 cursed energy on something by spending 2 you won’t be able to spend any more cursed energy.

Additionally, you do not have a specific body part to release your beams anymore and they are no longer S-1.


#### Cursed Energy Recharge


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

After mastering Reverse Cursed Technique, you have learned your cursed technique reversal. You have now learned how to regain cursed energy. Whenever you are the target of a cursed energy effect, you may now attempt to absorb the cursed energy being used. You may use this as a reaction. You must make a Constitution saving throw with the DC being equal to 10+½ the cursed energy used in the effect before reductions. If you succeed on the saving throw, you gain cursed energy equal to what was spent. In addition, if the effect was a cursed energy attack roll or it forced a saving throw, the effect is negated. If the effect was a melee attack roll, you will nullify any features apart of it that are fueled by cursed energy. If you fail, you regain no cursed energy and you will take damage. If the effect forces a saving throw you automatically fail the saving throw of the effect you were trying to absorb. If the effect uses a melee attack roll or if it uses a cursed energy attack roll, it automatically becomes a critical hit.

This feature cannot absorb Domain Expansions.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>G Warstaff</summary>
`e244138c5bfb7d385ecb9dab73417f80.jpg`
A G Warstaff user summoning the G Warstaff, Source [53]
This technique grants the you the ability to conjure a pen-like cursed tool capable of creating panels that allows you to peer into your target's future. 

#### G Warstaff

Lapse Technique (S-1)
Starting at 1st level, you can summon a pen-like cursed tool that allows you to see into the future of your opponents. As a bonus action for 2 cursed energy, you create a oversized pen-like cursed tool, called G Warstaff. It has the reach and two-handed properties, you may use your Charisma modifier for its attack and damage rolls, and on a hit it deals 1d4 + your Charisma modifier piercing damage. Additionally, it counts as a grade 4 cursed tool. G Warstaff lasts for one minute, or until combat ends if in initiative. You may dismiss G Warstaff at will. You can only have one G warstaff created at a time.

Whenever you hit a creature with G Warstaff, you can attempt to fulfill its condition for ink. When you deal damage to a creature equal to or greater than double its Constitution score using the G Warstaff, you will successfully gain enough blood from the creature to fulfill its condition. Once the condition has been filled, as an action, bonus action, or replacing an attack on your attack action for 2 cursed energy, you can force the creature whose blood was used to make a Dexterity saving throw if they are within 10 ft of you, as you shoot a manga panel out to attach to them. On a success, the panel misses and you must fulfill the condition on the creature again. On a failure, the creature is now marked with the panel, allowing you to see their future while looking at them. You begin with one second.

Doing damage to a target who already has a manga panel using the G Warstaff increases how many seconds into their future you can see, as long as it is also equal to or greater than double their Constitution score. This increases the amount you can see their future by 1 second, and making you gain the benefits of the earlier second along with the current. While you can see into a creature’s future you gain the following benefits depending on how many seconds you currently can see as long as you're not blinded:

| Seconds | Benefit |
| --- | --- |
| 1 | The creature's attack rolls against you is made with disadvantage and you have advantage against saving throws the creature forces you to make. |
| 2 | You have advantage on attack rolls against the creature and you know all the actions of the creature's next turn at the start of their turn(your GM tells you what the creature will do in that turn). |
| 3 | The creature has disadvantage on saving throws you force them to make and you know all the actions of the creature's next turn at the beginning of your turn(your GM tells you what the creature will do in that turn before they do it). |
Further hits at 3 seconds continue to increase how far you can see but futures beyond 3 seconds are too unreliable to offer any additional benefits in combat. The number of creatures that can be affected by this technique is equal to your Charisma modifier, and you may end the technique at will. The technique ends when the combat ends.

If a target knows or understands your innate technique, or if the target has been affected by your technique for more than two rounds, they can use their bonus action to make an Wisdom (Insight) check against your cursed energy DC. On a success, you use the following rule and on a failure, nothing happens. At the beginning of the creature’s turn who is under the effect of your technique, they can make a Dexterity(Stealth) check, contested by your Wisdom(Perception) check. If they succeed you lose the benefits gained of future sight on that creature until the end of your next turn, as they now attack your Blindspot.

You may use a bonus action to cover your Blindspot, allowing you to add your proficiency bonus to your contested perception check purely for this feature until the beginning of your next turn, waiting at your own blindspots to counterattack.

At 2nd level, G Warstaff now uses your martial arts die for damage rolls. At 5th level, G Warstaff counts as a Grade 3 cursed tool. At 11th level, G Warstaff counts as a Grade 2 cursed tool. At 17th level, G Warstaff counts as a Grade 1 cursed tool. At 20th level, G Warstaff counts as a Special Grade cursed tool.


#### Predictable Attacks


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, by glimpsing seconds into the future, you're never caught off guard. If you can see at least 2 seconds into a creature’s future and you use Resistant against that creature, you may add half your Charisma modifier, rounded down, to the score being used to compare for the result.

At 17th level, you may now add your full Charisma modifier.


#### Perfect Strike


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, your opponents become extremely easy to read after being affected by your technique, and you learned how to exploit that. Once per round when you land an attack with your G Warstaff against a creature that you can see 3 seconds into the future, you may spend 1 Cursed Energy to make that attack count as a critical hit for the purposes of its damage.


#### Illuminated Actions


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, while in battle you are illuminated, knowing exactly what to do and when. Whenever you roll with advantage against a creature because of your technique, the feature's critical hit radius is reduced by half of your Charisma modifier(rounded down). Also, whenever a creature rolls with disadvantage against you because of your technique, the rolls critical failure's radius is increased by half of your Charisma modifier(rounded down). For example, if you have a +4 in your Charisma modifier and they roll a natural 3 on their roll, it counts as a critical failure.


#### Tokyo School Feature: GG True Warstaff


**Extension Technique**

At 14th level, you have created a more powerful version of your G Warstaff, being a true weapon of destruction. As a bonus action while you have G Warstaff active, you can spend 4 cursed energy to evolve it into its true form, GG True Warstaff. It gains the following benefits for 1 minute:

It deals one additional damage dice.
Its reach is 15ft. instead of 10ft.
Its critical hit radius is 19-20.

#### The Enlightened One


**Technique Improvement**

Finally at 20th level, your foresight ability has reached its maximum potential, reaching the point where you are considered enlightened. Whenever you have advantage because of your technique, you can roll 3 dices instead of 2 and keep the highest roll. Also, whenever a creature has disadvantage on attack rolls against you because of your technique, they roll 3 dices instead of 2 and keep the lowest roll.


#### View Past


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

With your mastery over Reverse Cursed Technique comes your cursed technique reversal. Just like when using G Warstaff normally, you fulfill G Warstaff's condition for ink by hitting a creature. However, instead of being able to see their future, you can see their past. As a bonus action for 4 cursed energy, you can view their manga panel and learn the actions they took over the past few weeks. You can see a number of weeks in the past equal to the amount of seconds stored in the G Warstaff.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Private Pure Love Train</summary>
`67ecd552eb49c5faf4626d42dbc68b68.jpg`
A sorcerer overflowing with infinite cursed energy from a jackpot, Source [54]

Banned Pending Rework

</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Obstacle Removal</summary>
`ezgif-5debbc8ee206d4.png`
A curse with the innate technique Obstacle Removal, source [55]
This innate technique has the ability to remove obstacles by entangling a concept with its technique. 

#### Obstacle Removal

Lapse Technique (S-2)
Starting at 1st level, you have begun to understand the terrifying power your innate technique possesses, however, you have not yet perfected it. You are able to remove obstacles that stop you from reaching your goals. As an action for X cursed energy, you are able to remove an object that is standing in your way. The cost of this feature depends on the size of what you are attempting to remove. The costs can be found in the following table:

| Object Size | Cursed Energy Amount |
| --- | --- |
| Tiny Object | 1 Cursed Energy |
| Small Object | 3 Cursed Energy |
| Medium Object | 6 Cursed Energy |
| Large Object | 9 Cursed Energy |
| Huge Object | 12 Cursed Energy |
| Gargantuan Object | 25 Cursed Energy |
Whenever you remove an object, it is teleported out of your way. When you use this feature to remove a structure or object, you must choose an unoccupied point within 30 feet of where it originally was located to reappear.

The distance you teleport an object or structure increases to 60 feet at 5th level, to 90 feet at 11th level, to 120 feet at 17th level, and to 150 feet at 20th level.

Additionally, at 11th level, your ability to remove objects has improved drastically. You are now able to remove large structures, such as a house or a building. Causing them to disappear as well.

In order to do this you must use an action for every turn in a minute. If you complete the full minute of actions, then you can spend the corresponding cursed energy to remove the structure:

| Structure Volume | Cursed Energy Amount |
| --- | --- |
| 100 Cubic Feet | 30 Cursed Energy |
| 200 Cubic Feet | 60 Cursed Energy |
| 300 Cubic Feet | 90 Cursed Energy |
| 400 Cubic Feet | 120 Cursed Energy |
| 500 Cubic Feet | 150 Cursed Energy |
| 600 Cubic Feet | 180 Cursed Energy |
| So on, so forth | 30 Cursed Energy per 100 feet extra |
You must maintain concentration during the minute you are using this feature. Also, when removing a structure, it is teleported in the same way as objects detailed above.

You are also able to spend more time in order to lower the amount of cursed energy needed. You may spend an additional minute worth of actions when using this feature. For every extra minute added the cost is reduced by 5 cursed energy (to a minimum of 1 cursed energy). You must also maintain concentration during these extra minutes.


#### Creature Removal

Technique Improvement (S-2)
At 3rd level, you have realized that you are able to remove creatures that block your goal as well.
