As an action for X cursed energy, you can force a creature within a 100 foot radius of yourself to make a Charisma saving throw. On a failure, they are removed and teleported 30 feet away to an unoccupied point. On a success, they are not affected. The cursed energy cost can be found below:

| Creature Size | Cursed Energy Amount |
| --- | --- |
| Tiny Creature | 2 Cursed Energy |
| Small Creature | 4 Cursed Energy |
| Medium Creature | 8 Cursed Energy |
| Large Creature | 12 Cursed Energy |
| Huge Creature | 20 Cursed Energy |
| Gargantuan Creature | 30 Cursed Energy |
The distance you teleport a creature increases to 60 feet at 5th level, to 90 feet at 11th level, to 120 feet at 17th level, and to 150 feet at 20th level.

Self Removal. At 5th level, your ability to remove creatures who are obstacles extends to yourself. As a bonus action for 6 cursed energy, you are able to target yourself with your technique. Depending on the obstacle you are facing you can do one of the following options. You can either gain a flying speed equal to your speed for 1 minute or teleport an amount of feet equal to the distance you can teleport creatures. Both of these allow you to remove an obstacle of yourself so you can reach your goal.

Multi-Removal. At 11th level, when you use Creature Removal, you are now able to select a number of creatures in the 100 foot radius equal to your Charisma modifier. You must pay the corresponding cursed energy amount for each creature and they must each make the Charisma saving throw. They suffer the effects as normal based on a failure or a success.


#### Technique Removal

Technique Improvement (S-2)
At 6th level, you have learned that there is another thing you can do with your technique. You are able to entangle concepts into a target’s technique. Those targets are then able to be removed. This process is very complicated and hard to pull off. As a rooting action for 12 cursed energy, you may attempt to entangle concepts into a creature’s technique. One creature within a 100 foot radius of your choice, must make a Charisma saving throw. On a failure, the target’s innate technique is removed for 1 minute. On a success, they are unaffected. Creatures can repeat the Charisma saving throw at the beginning of their turns to regain their innate technique.

Complicated techniques that have concepts that cannot be contained semantically or pragmatically, such as Bom Ba Ye, cannot be removed. What techniques that are too complicated to be removed are determined by the DM.

Unfortunately, manipulating concepts is a process that is incredibly intensive on the brain. At the end of your turns while Technique Removal is active, you must make an Intelligence (Arcana) check against a DC of the respective creature’s level or CR. For every check that you fail, you take xd12 Psychic damage. X=that respective creature’s level or CR. This damage dealt to you ignores Resistance, Immunity, and cannot be reduced in any way.

This damage lowers to d10s at 11th level, to d8s at 17th level and to d6s at 20th level.


#### Distance Removal

Extension Technique (S-2)
At 10th level, with precise experimentation, you figured out how to remove distance as a concept, essentially being able to form portals with just a trick of the mind. As a full turn action for 10 cursed energy, you may create a portal within 5 feet of you that leads to a location you've been before. The portal lasts until the end of your next turn and creatures that walk through the portal are teleported to the location within 5 feet of the portal in an unoccupied space.

Unwilling creatures cannot be forced through the portal.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Precise Erasure

Technique Improvement (S-2)
At 14th level, you have refined your technique even more. You are now able to use your technique in the following ways:

Condition Removal. As an action for 8 cursed energy, you can remove a condition affecting yourself or another creature within 60 feet of yourself. You cannot remove exhaustion.

Defensive Removal. As a reaction to yourself or a creature within 30 feet of you being targeted by an innate technique, you can spend an amount of cursed energy equal to the original cost of the innate technique before reductions, to attempt to remove the innate technique. The cost of this technique cannot be reduced other than with the Six Eyes or Flawless Fundamentals. You must make a Charisma saving throw with the DC being equal to the DC or the attack roll of the innate technique you are removing. On a success, it is nullified. On a failure it is unaffected.

Additionally, this can be used as an action on an ongoing effect. Paying the same cost as above and making the same save.


#### Lord of Obstacles


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, you have progressed extremely far into your ability to remove obstacles. When using Technique Removal, you make the arcana checks with advantage and it now lasts for 2 minutes.


#### Obstacle Creation


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat (S-2)**

You have finally learned your cursed technique reversal. It allows you to create obstacles instead of removing them. As a reaction to an attack roll or a saving throw for X cursed energy, you can create an obstacle to block the attack for you. This object appears directly in front of you. The following table details the cursed energy cost based on the obstacle created:

| Object Size | Cursed Energy Amount |
| --- | --- |
| Tiny Object | 2 Cursed Energy |
| Small Object | 6 Cursed Energy |
| Medium Object | 12 Cursed Energy |
| Large Object | 18 Cursed Energy |
| Huge Object | 24 Cursed Energy |
| Gargantuan Object | 50 Cursed Energy |
When you use this ability the object becomes the new target of the attack. The DM decides what random object is created as well as the HP amount of this object and its resistances, immunities, and vulnerabilities. If the attack breaks the object, you take the remaining damage. If you created a larger obstacle it may be able to block damage for other creatures depending on placement. The obstacle disappears after blocking damage for you.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Antigravity System</summary>
`jjk_204_3_022.png`
An antigravity user activating their reversal, source [56]
Antigravity system is a Innate Technique used to manipulate gravity, creating fields of antigravity. 

#### Antigravity System


**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 1st level, you have begun to learn the very basic parts of your technique, being able to negate gravity around yourself. As an action for 3 cursed energy, you can negate gravity in a 10-foot-radius, 30-foot-high cylinder centered on you for all creatures but yourself until the beginning of your next turn.

All creatures and objects that aren’t somehow anchored to the ground are now floating helplessly. A creature can make a Dexterity saving throw to grab onto a fixed object it can reach; thus, they are not floating. Floating creatures are unable to move on their own, unless they possess a suitable method of flight or thrust (determined by the dm). If you are able to move through the system, then it only takes half as much movement, as you can use the low gravity to your advantage (this does not apply to yourself). Floating creatures also cannot be made prone and have disadvantage on melee attack rolls.

Whenever a ranged attack or a form of knockback travels through the area, the movement through does not affect the range or the total knockback distance. For example, a ranged attack with a total range of 50 feet that travels 10 feet before entering the radius would have 40 feet remaining upon leaving the technique’s radius. If someone was knocked back 100 feet and went 80 feet before entering the radius, they would continue moving 20 feet after leaving the radius.

Additionally, when a floating creature or object is hit by a melee attack, they are knocked back an amount of feet equal to 5 times the Strength or Dexterity modifier used by the attacker.

At the end of the duration, affected objects and creatures fall back down.

At 5th level, the cylinder is now 50 foot-high, this increases to 100 foot-high at 11th level, 150 foot-high at 17th and to 200 foot-high at 20th level.


#### Antigravity Focus


**Extension Technique**

At 3rd level, you have created an extension technique to not affect all creatures at once with your antigravity system. As a bonus action after using antigravity system, you can spend 4 cursed energy to choose a number of creatures up to your Charisma modifier (minimum of 1) + your proficiency bonus to not be affected by your antigravity system.


#### Minor System


**Extension Technique**

At 6th level, your technique evolves, being able to selectively target a singular creature. As a bonus action for 4 cursed energy, you touch a willing creature, doubling their jump distance and jump height for 1 minute. You may end this at will. If used on yourself and the duration ends, you may spend 1 cursed energy to extend it for another minute.

This jump boost increases to triple the distance at 11th level, to quadruple at 17th level, and to quintuple at 20th level.


#### Long Inverted Gravity


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, your technique now lasts much longer than before. Whenever you activate your Antigravity System, it now lasts for 1 minute instead of until the beginning of your next turn. You can end your technique at will.

Whenever your technique would end, you can spend 1 cursed energy to keep it for 1 additional minute.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Object Floatation


**Extension Technique**

At 14th level, in Tokyo, you have developed a new way to minimize your systems. You are now able to apply a small system to an object, removing gravity from the object. As a bonus action for 6 cursed energy, you can touch an object and cause it to float for 1 minute. While floating, if the object is thrown, it will travel until it hits an object or until it travels an amount of feet equal to 50 times the Strength or Dexterity modifier used to throw it.


#### Levitational Apex


**Extension Technique**

Finally at 20th level, you have created an extension technique that allows you to make a larger Antigravity System. As an action for 8 cursed energy, you can create an Antigravity system that has a radius of 50 feet and a height of 200 feet. This system lasts for 1 minute, and you must maintain concentration for the duration. Creatures caught in this Antigravity System act as if they were caught inside of your lapse.


#### Gravity System


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

You have learned how to reverse your own technique, manifesting a field of intense gravity. As an action for 6 cursed energy, you force every creature within a 10-foot-radius of you to make a Strength saving throw. On a failure, they take 10d10 force damage and are knocked prone and restrained until the beginning of your next turn or until they leave the area. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not prone or restrained. For the duration of Gravity System, your speed is reduced to 0. You may deactivate Gravity System at will (no action required).

This technique deals twice as much damage to objects and structures.

You can also use this technique as a reaction to an attack being made against you or if you're forced to make a saving throw. If used against a saving throw, you reduce the damage of the saving throw by the damage of your Gravity System. If you reduce the damage to 0, the effect ends. Any remaining damage after being reduced, is transferred to you. This only works on physical saves or things affected by gravity.

Ranged attacks made inside Gravity System or whose projectiles enter it will fail if the roll is lower than your cursed energy DC added twice.

If a Dexterity or Constitution saving throw targets a creature inside Gravity System, its total damage will be reduced by your Gravity System damage die. This does not work in the case of the attack dealing thunder or psychic damage.

This technique's damage increases to 15d10 at 11th level, 20d10 at 17th level, and to 25d10 at 20th level.

Additionally, while a creature is restrained they now automatically fail Dexterity saving throws.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Bom Ba Ye</summary>
`0150eab357d84a411f8a45ffb867503d.jpg`
A Bom Ba Ye user increasing her mass before an attack, Source [57]
Bom Ba Ye allows its user to increase its virtual mass by using its cursed energy, making short work of opponents by crushing them with their weight. 

#### Black Hole

Despite your technique being an all powerful tool, you know there is a limit to your powers. If you add more than 20 virtual mass increases, you create a black hole centered on yourself, forcing every creature in a 60 foot-radius centered on you to make a Strength saving throw with disadvantage. On a failed save, the creature takes 60d12 force damage and is pulled to within 5 feet of the black hole. This saving throw repeats at the start of a creature’s turn while the black hole is active. All damage and conditions from the black hole ignore any resistances and immunities to it. On a successful save, the creature takes half as much damage and is knocked prone but isn’t pulled toward the black hole. The black hole lasts for 1 minute.

A creature that’s within 5 feet of the black hole at the start of its turn must make a successful Strength saving throw or take 30d10 force damage and have its speed reduced to 0 until the start of its next turn. On a success, the creature takes half damage and has its speed reduced to 0 until the start of its next turn. A creature that’s within 60 feet of the black hole (but not within 5 feet of it) at the end of its turn takes 30d8 cold damage. A creature reduced to 0 hit points while within 5 feet of the black hole dies immediately.

You are killed outright, but the black hole continues.

All objects and structures within 120 feet of the black hole take 60d12 force damage, and the ground in this area becomes difficult terrain. Any objects or structures within 60 feet of the black hole are immediately destroyed.

The range and radius of any effect caused by the black hole double at the end of what would have been your turn.


#### Bom Ba Ye


**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 1st level, your technique allows yourself to increase your own virtual mass. As a bonus action you may activate your technique for an amount of Cursed Energy up to your Charisma modifier (minimum of 1), increasing the virtual mass of your body by the amount of cursed energy spent until the start of your next turn.


#### You gain the following benefits from your mass:


Stronger Martial Arts. Your Unarmed Strike damage die increases by one per two mass increases you receive (if you have no damage die for unarmed strikes, you count as if you had a d4 for the purposes of this feature).

Mass Impact. At one mass increase, whenever you roll a critical with an unarmed strike the target must succeed a Strength saving throw or be knocked prone. At three mass increases, whenever you land a critical hit on a creature with an unarmed strike the creature must make a Strength saving throw or be knocked back a number of feet equal to 10 times your Strength modifier. If the creature collides with an unsecured object or structure, then the creature and the unsecured object or structure receive 1d6 bludgeoning damage for every 10 feet the creature had traveled after colliding with the surface. If it was another creature, they instead both take damage equal to half of your damage roll (rounded down). If this attack would already knock the creature back the knock back will be increased by 10 times your Strength modifier feet, additionally if you hit an object or structure with an unarmed strike the damage the object or structure takes is doubled. At seven mass increases if you destroy an object or structure it will create a cone in which all creatures will be heavily obscured in debris equal to ten times your Strength modifier feet long. Alternatively you may punch the ground and create a circle with a radius of half your Strength modifier times ten feet that lasts until the end of your turn. All creatures will be heavily obscured in debris equal to ten times your Strength modifier feet.

Increased Damage. Your unarmed strikes’ damage rolls receives a +1 for every mass increase you receive.

Grappler. For every two mass increases you receive you’ll count as one size larger for grapples. This stops once you start counting as Gargantuan.

At 5th level, you can now spend up to your Charisma modifier + half your Charisma modifier (rounded down) in your technique, this increases to your Charisma modifier added twice at 11th level, to your Charisma modifier added thrice at 17th level and to your Charisma modifier added four times at 20th level.


#### Critical Attacks


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, as your technique grows stronger, so does your power with your unarmed strikes. For every four mass increases you receive, your unarmed strike critical hit range is reduced by 1 (20 -> 19 -> 18 etc.). This cannot make your critical hit range go below 18.

Additionally, whenever you score a critical with an unarmed strike while your technique is active you roll one additional damage die.


#### Increased Strength


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, your mass now increases your overall strength instead of just your strikes. When you are at five mass increases, you have advantage on Strength checks and saving throws. For every five mass increases after, you gain a +1 in these checks and saving throws.

Additionally, you now deal twice as much damage to objects and structures while you are benefiting from Bom Ba Ye.


#### Powerful Blows


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, you learned how to more effectively utilize the massive impacts of your technique. Once per round when you hit an attack while under the effects of Bom Ba Ye you may force the Mass Impact save without the attack being a critical.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Mass Maneuvers


**Extension Technique**

At 14th level, you have learned at the Tokyo school that you can use your imaginary mass in different ways while in hand-to-hand combat. You gain the following ways to do so:

O Goshi. (S-2) You have learned that due to your imaginary mass, throwing a creature is very easy. Once per round as part of successfully grappling a creature, you can force them to make a Strength saving throw. On a failure, you throw them prone and they take your martial arts die in bludgeoning damage. On a success, they resist and are not thrown prone and take no damage.

Roundhouse Kick. You have learned how to put a lot of momentum on one devastating blow. Once per round as part of an unarmed strike, you may perform this kick. If you successfully land the kick, the creature must then make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, they are Confused until the start of their next turn. On a success, they are unaffected.

Uppercut. You have learned that due to the crazy mass of each punch you have the potential to knock creatures up. Once per round as part of an unarmed strike, you may perform this uppercut. On a hit, the target is forced to make a Strength saving throw. On a failure, they are knocked up into the air and land 20 feet directly back. On a success, they are not knocked back.

Heavy Stomp. You can also stomp the ground using your mass to destroy the ground below. Once per round as part of an unarmed strike, you can target the ground or a prone creature. On a hit the creature and the ground both take your unarmed strike damage and the area in a 15 foot radius centered on where you kicked becomes difficult terrain.


#### Perfected Virtual Mass


**Technique Improvement**

Finally at 20th level, you have reached the peak of virtual mass. Once per round, you may force a creature to make a saving throw against your cursed technique with disadvantage, unless its Strength score is higher than your Cursed Energy DC.

Additionally, while you are benefiting from Bom Ba Ye, the bludgeoning damage dealt by your unarmed strikes overcomes resistance, but not immunity.


#### Virtual Mass Decrease


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

Due to your mastery over Reverse Cursed Technique, you have learned to reverse the mass increase. This allows you to get rid of all the mass in your body. This state can be entered as a bonus action or a reaction. You may spend an amount of cursed energy up to your Charisma modifier added twice. For every 2 cursed energy spent, you increase your speed by 10 feet for 1 minute or until you use a free action to disable it.

Once per turn, while the feature is active and you land a melee weapon attack on a creature, you deal an additional 2 damage for every 10ft you move up to a maximum of 100ft.

This feature cannot be used in tandem with Bom Ba Ye.

At 5th level, you may spend up to 3 times your Charisma modifier. At 11th level, you can spend up to 4 times your Charisma modifier. At 17th level, you may spend up to 6 your Charisma modifier. At 20th level, you may spend up to 8 times your Charisma modifier.

Zero Virtual Mass. Despite your reversal being powerful there is a limit. If you spend more than 40 Cursed Energy, you reduce your mass to 0. When your mass is reduced to 0, you become incorporeal, causing your hit points to be reduced to half of their current amount for 1 minute. For the duration of this feature, you cannot be affected by damage except for psychic damage and you are able to phase through structures, objects and other creatures.

However you are unable to interact physically with other structures, objects and other creatures. Additionally your speed increases by 30 feet. You may disable this by using a free action.

You may not regain hit points while this feature is active and until the end of your next turn immediately after you disable the feature.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Solo Forbidden Area</summary>
`43c1f1e37259e4ba27b3aabc02c60315.jpg`
A Solo Forbidden Area user beginning the ritual, source [58]
The Solo Forbidden Area cursed technique is not a technique suited for combat, being used generally as a support. It enhances the cursed techniques of the user and every sorcerer around them. 

#### Solo Forbidden Area


**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 1st level, your technique allows you to improve the output of those around you. As an action, you may spend 2 CE to temporarily increase the output of yourself and allied Jujutsu Sorcerers within 20ft. You may spend more CE to increase the effect, up to an additional amount of CE equal to twice your proficiency bonus. Multiple applications of this effect do not stack. For every 2 CE spent on this effect, apply the following benefits to features that cost Curse Energy:
A feature that deals or adds to damage does +1d12 Damage. This bonus damage may only be applied once per round per beneficiary.
+10ft Range if the range is not Touch.
+5ft Radius if it covers an area; or +10ft for Lines and Cones.


#### Quickened Support


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level you may use your Lapse Technique as a Bonus Action instead of an Action. If you do, it provides benefits as though you spend 2 less CE on it


#### Maximized Output


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level when you use your Lapse Technique as an Action and spend the maximum amount of CE, any Curse Techniques it enhances gain Advantage on their attack rolls. Any saves against enhanced techniques gain disadvantage on those saves.


#### Ritual of Lights

**Technique Improvement**

You gain Proficiency in Performance. If you already have Proficiency, you gain Expertise instead. As an action (S-2) that costs 12 CE and requires Concentration, you may begin a dance that provides progressively greater benefits. On the first turn, apply Solo Forbidden Area's lapse benefit as though you spent twice your proficiency bonus in CE, and Maximized Output to yourself and all allies within 40ft until the end of your next turn. On each consecutive turn, increase the area by 10ft and the benefit from Solo Forbidden Area's benefit as though you spent 2 more CE beyond the normal maximum. On each subsequent turn, you must spend 2 times the number of consecutive  turns you have been performing.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Honored One


**Extension Technique**

You may choose to have Solo Forbidden Area only apply to a single other ally instead of yourself and all allies in the area. When you do, the benefits double. This works with Ritual of Lights.


#### Otherworldly Output


**Technique Improvement**

Finally at 20th level, you have reached the maximum potential of your technique. You may use Solo Forbidden Area and Ritual of Lights as a bonus action without penalty


#### Area of Depletion


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

Area of Depletion: Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat. Your mastery over Reverse Cursed Technique has unlocked your cursed technique reversal. This allows you to now create an area that lowers the output of creatures inside. You may spend an action an up to twice your proficiency bonus in CE to create this area. You may do so as a Bonus Action, but you are considered to have spent 4 less CE when determining its effects. You may not use Solo Forbidden Area or Ritual of Lights while using Aura of Depletion. Everyone else within 20ft must make a Charisma Saving Throw or suffer the following effects on their features that utilize Curse Energy until the end of their next turn for every 2 CE you spend:
All CE costs are increased by 1
All ranges are reduced by 5ft, to a minimum of 5ft.
All areas are reduced by 5ft, to a minimum of 5ft.
They deal -1 die of damage.

</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Mythical Beast Amber</summary>
`f08e8db7553d06cf3aa3b7bca13f8be6.jpg`
A sorcerer activating the technique, Source [59]
The Mythical Beast Amber innate technique allows you to transform your body to make any phenomena that can be manifested from lightning. However, it has a great price to pay for its power due to transcending humanity. 

#### Electric Cursed Energy

You have been born with a special cursed energy, granting you special properties that make you easily stand out against sorcerers. You gain the Unique Cursed Energy feat and have lightning as your Cursed Energy Type. You also gain all three of the Wellspring Of Energy, Dynamic Striker, and Natural Resistance features from the background feat but you must use the Water Weakness example provided. Your Dynamic Striker condition being Shocked.


#### Surpassing the Limits of Mankind

This Innate Technique is unique in the fact that it is rarely useable and comes at great cost. As such, Mythical Beast Amber only provides its Lapse Technique as a feature. After level one, a character should then gain a Sorcerer Path, which they will level in as they progress for each level after the first. For example, a 20th level character would possess one level in Mythical Beast Amber, and 19 levels in the Sorcerer Path. If you reach Epic Levels, then as normal, the Subclasses will not continue to level past 20.

Design Note: When using the Sorcerer Path + Innate Technique rule, the character would still gain a sorcerer path of their own as well as the Mythical Beast Amber technique, and after level one, choosing another Sorcerer Path, which would be one level behind their other regular Path. In addition, DMs should be very careful allowing Positive Energy Master Sorcerer Path, as this will allow the character to subvert many downsides of using Mythical Beast Amber at a very early level.


#### Mythical Beast Amber Unleashed


**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 1st level, your technique reconstructs your flesh in order to manifest phenomena that can be created by electricity, which is converted from your cursed energy. However, since the technique causes your body to surpass the limits of a human, it can only be used once before your body collapses following the technique's conclusion.

As a bonus action for 6 cursed energy, your body will start getting filled with electric cursed energy, making you constantly grow in power for a short period of time. Your technique lasts a number of rounds equal to your original Constitution score (minimum of 10), after which you are killed instantly.

As the technique progresses, you gain certain benefits from lightning. See the table below for reference:

| Time Active | Body Increases Benefits | Lightning Phenomena |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0 rounds | You gain a +4 to your Strength, Dexterity and Constitution scores, including their maximums, immunity to lightning damage, and your speed increases by 60 feet. Also, you gain advantage in Dexterity saving throws. Also, you can make one additional attack during the attack action. This attack must be made with an unarmed strike. | Lightning Claw. When you activate your technique, your cursed energy surrounds your hands, forming claws. Your unarmed strikes now deal lightning damage. Also, their damage dice is increased by one (if they have no damage die, they are treated as if they were a d4 for the purposes of this feature). Finally, if you do not use Cursed Strike on an attack with these claws they deal an additional amount of your Cursed Strike damage dice equal to half your proficiency bonus. |
Loud Blast. Your jaw starts to get covered with cursed energy, allowing you to emit loud energy blasts from your mouth. You can spend 1 cursed energy as an action, making a cursed energy attack roll against a creature within 30 feet of you. On a hit, they take Xd10 (with X being your Charisma modifier) lightning damage. Even if the attack misses, all creatures within a 15 foot radius of the target must make a Constitution saving throw. On a failure, they take xd8 (with X being your proficiency bonus) thunder damage and are deafened until the beginning of your next turn. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not deafened. If they fail by 5 or more, they are deafened for 1 minute instead. In that case, they can remake the Constitution saving throw at the beginning of their turns, ending the effect early on a success.

| 5 rounds | Your physical scores and their maximums increase by +2 once more (to a total of +6), and your speed increases by another 30 feet. Also, you can make two additional attacks instead of one during the attack action. These attacks must be made with an unarmed strike. | Lightning Armor. Your torso and legs start to get filled with cursed energy. You have a damage reduction equal to your Charisma score (minimum of 10). |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 minute | Your physical scores and their maximums increase by +2 once more (to a total of +8), and your speed increases by another 30 feet. Additionally, you gain an additional bonus action. | X-Ray Vision. Your head gets completely filled with cursed energy, granting you new eyes with improved sight. You gain 120 feet of true sight, and you're also able to see all Body feats the creature has, and the creature's Strength, Dexterity, and Constitution scores with no action required. Additionally, you have advantage on Wisdom (Perception) checks related to sight, and may add your Charisma modifier to those checks and your passive perception. |
| 1 minute and 5 rounds | Your physical scores and their maximums increase by +2 once more (to a total of +10), and your speed increases by another 30 feet. Additionally, you gain an additional action. | Cursed Beam. Your form now allows you to discharge a powerful beam of light. You can spend 2 cursed energy to launch a powerful beam of light as an action (S-2), forcing all creatures in a 60 feet long 5 feet wide line to make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take Xd12 (with X being your Charisma modifier added twice) lightning damage and are blinded for 1 minute. On a success, they take half as much damage and are blinded, but only until the beginning of your next turn. They can make a Constitution saving throw at the beginning of their turns, ending the blinded early on a success. If a creature's Dexterity score is lower than your cursed energy DC, they do this save with disadvantage. When using this technique, you can spend additional cursed energy up to your Charisma modifier (minimum of 1), adding one damage die to the total and increasing the DC by 1 per cursed energy spent. |
Technique Deactivation. If a feature were to deactivate your Innate Technique, such as being hit by the Inverted Spear of Heaven, you go back to normal, however, you must make a DC 10 + 5 per 5 rounds passed on the form Constitution saving throw. The effects determined by a success or a failure are as follows:

| Time Active | Detriment on a Success | Detriment on a Failure |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 0 rounds | You gain two levels of exhaustion and your jaw and arms become broken, meaning any ability checks or attack rolls you make with your arms automatically fail, you always fail Charisma checks and you cannot breathe without assistance. | You lose your arms and jaw and you gain three levels of exhaustion. |
| 5 rounds | You gain one level of exhaustion and your torso and legs become broken, meaning you will have disadvantadge on all Dexterity and Constitution saving throws, your hit point maximum is halved, and you cannot jump. | You die. |
| 1 minute | You gain one level of exhaustion and your skull and eyes become broken, meaning you will fall unconscious until the end of your next turn, you will recieve a -7 on all Wisdom (Perception) checks involving sight, and any saving throws you make to avoid the blinded condition you do with disadvantage. | You die. |
| 1 minute and 5 rounds | You gain one level of exhaustion and you are reduced to ¼ of your maximum hit points. | You die. |
All successive rounds passed incur each of the previous detriments before it, with the results still varying if you succeeded or failed. Any broken limbs heal once you take a long rest, meaning you lose any detriments aside from exhaustion, which must be removed as normal.


#### Mythical Beast Amber Suppressed


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

Your mastery of the Reverse Cursed Technique has granted you the reversal of your technique, the ability to turn your body back to flesh after using your lapse. As a bonus action, while your lapse technique is active, you can spend 4 cursed energy to start reversing the effects, paying again at the start of each of your turns the reversal is happening. The amount of time that you were using your lapse is now the amount of time it will take to fully reverse the effects. When you hit each threshold in reverse, you lose the benefits it gave and the save becomes easier. If you wait for the same amount of time that you had your lapse active then your lapse technique will be deactivated and you do not have to make a saving throw to leave the form, gaining only one exhaustion level. You must concentrate on this reversal while your transformation is being reversed, and if you lose concentration during this process the reversal ends early, forcibly deactivating your technique at the stage you had reversed to and incurring the corresponding saving throw.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Drill Hair</summary>
`drill-hair.png`
The drill hair technique, Source [60]
This technique allows you to create and shoot drills from your hair. 

#### Drill Hair


**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 1st level, your technique allows you to use cursed energy to make your hair turn into small drills. These drills can then be fired off. When you take the attack action, you can spend 1 cursed energy to replace one attack with a ranged cursed energy attack roll with a range of 30ft. This attack deals 1d6 + your Charisma modifier piercing damage.

This technique’s range and damage increase to 45ft and 2d6 + Charisma modifier at 5th level, to 60ft and 3d6 + Charisma modifier at 11th level, to 75ft and 4d6 + Charisma modifier at 17th level, and 90ft and 5d6 + Charisma modifier at 20th level.


#### Explosive Drill


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, you have learned how to make your drills explode upon impact. When attacking with a drill, you may spend an additional 3 cursed energy. This makes the drill explode in a 5ft radius upon the point of impact causing creatures within the radius to make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take an additional 3d6 fire damage including the base damage from the drill on a success, they take half as much damage from the drill.

This technique’s damage increases to 5d6 at 5th level, to 7d6 at 11th level, 9d6 at 17th level, and to 11d6 at 20th level.


#### Charged Drill


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, you have now learned how to add more power to your drills, before releasing them. You may now replace multiple attacks as part of the attack action when you use your drill to combine them into one attack. You make a single attack with your drill and spend an additional 1 cursed energy for each attack replaced this way, and the damage of your drill is the damage the number of attacks replaced added together.


#### Maximum Drill


**Extension Technique**

At 10th level, you have learned a new way to use your drills. You are now able to use more of your cursed energy to imbue it into one large drill. As an action for 8 cursed energy, you can shoot this new drill. The drill is fired in a 15ft wide and 100ft long line. Anyone in this line must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, it deals 10d10 piercing damage. On a success, they take half the damage. This technique deals quadruple damage to objects and structures and ignores cover. The damage of this technique increases to 14d10 at 17th level, and to 18d10 at 20th level.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Hand Drill

Extension Technique (S-1)
At 14th level, you have learned a new way to use your drill from your time at the Tokyo school. You are now able to create a drill from your hair that is useable as a melee weapon. As a bonus action for 4 cursed energy, you can summon this drill. When holding this drill you can use it as a normal melee weapon with a range of 5ft and dealing 4d8 + your Charisma modifier piercing damage. This weapon lasts for 1 minute or until dismissed.


#### Stronger Drills


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, your drills have reached their maximum potential. The piercing damage they deal now ignores resistances.


#### Unscrew


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

Due to your mastery over Reverse Cursed Technique, you have learned your cursed technique reversal. You are now able to unscrew the drills you have shot. As a part of any nonexplosive drill feature, you can spend its cost an additional time to have the drill come back through the target dealing the appropriate damage again by making a ranged cursed energy attack roll for Drill Hair, a melee weapon attack for Hand Drill and creatures must make the Dexterity saving throw for Maximum Drill. For example, if you use a drill hair attack, you can spend an additional 1 cursed energy to have it come back through dealing another 1d6 + your Charisma modifier piercing damage.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Teleportation</summary>
`Ui_Ui_and_Kirara_retrieve_Higuruma.png`
A user of the teleportation technique being ready to teleport himself out of danger, Source [61]
The teleportation technique allows you to teleport yourself and creatures near you by concealing yourself with a sheet. 

#### Teleportation

Lapse Technique (S-1)
Starting at 1st level, your technique allows you to teleport to destinations you have marked. You will start out with a white sheet, which you use to cover yourself to teleport. As a bonus action for 1 cursed energy, you may mark a person, location, or object which you can touch. You can have up to twice your Charisma modifier people, locations, or objects marked at once and they will remain marked for up to 12 hours. You can unmark something for no action required.

As an action for 2 cursed energy, you can cover yourself and any willing creature within 5ft. of you that is also covered to teleport up to 500 feet to a mark.

At 10th level, you may now teleport as a bonus action.


#### Improved Teleport


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, you have improved your technique. The range of your teleport now increases to (your Charisma modifier + your proficiency bonus + your Jujutsu Sorcerer level) x 100 feet, and you can now use the teleport as a reaction to an attack or saving throw as long as you're holding the sheet.


#### Mastered Teleport


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, with great training, you have reached the pinnacle of your teleport. As an action for 10 cursed energy you use teleport. You can only go to marks you have already made with this teleport and you always go to the exact mark you want to go to, regardless of how far away from you they are.

Additionally, your markings now last for a week instead of 12 hours.


#### Improved Marking


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, you have learned to mark things more efficiently. You can now mark for a free action instead of a bonus action and the amount of markings you can have is increased to 3 times your Charisma modifier.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Stay Behind


**Technique Improvement**

At 14th level, you are now able to teleport others while you do not leave. You can now choose to only teleport other creatures when using any teleport feature. Additionally, if not used on a willing creature, they are forced to make a Charisma saving throw. If they fail, the creature is teleported. On a success, they are not teleported.


#### Perfected Teleport


**Technique Improvement**

Finally at 20th level, you're grown more efficient with your teleports and have perfected their usage. The action cost for your teleports is reduced by one(full turn action->action->bonus action->free action).

Additionally, your markings now last for 1 month.


#### Grounding


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat (S-1)**

You have mastered Reverse Cursed Technique and unlocked your cursed technique reversal. You have now learned how to force people to stay still by reversing your technique. As a bonus action for 2 cursed energy, you can cover an enemy and force them to make a Charisma saving throw. This enemy must be in melee range so you can cover them with your sheet. On a failure, their speed is reduced to 0 for 1 minute, on a success they are not affected. A creature who fails can repeat the saving throw at the end of their turns, ending the effect on a success.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Heart Catch</summary>
`0c358-17122255604483-1920.jpg`
A user of the Heart Catch technique, Source [62]
This technique allows you to summon a large hand and grab people or their attention. 

#### Heart Catch

Lapse Technique (S-1)
Starting at 1st level, you can create a virtual hand that floats in the air near you. As an action for up to half your Charisma modifier cursed energy, you may create this hand. The hand lasts for 1 minute or until you dismiss it. This hand is small-sized.

This hand can pick up and manipulate small or smaller-sized objects or creatures. You may control the hand as a bonus action. You can use the hand to manipulate an object, open an unlocked door or container, grab items, and attack with a weapon. When attacking with the hand, make a melee cursed energy attack roll against the target's AC, dealing the held weapons damage on a hit.

This hand has an AC of 10 and hit points equal to 10 times the cursed energy spent. When controlling the hand you can move it up to 30 feet. The maximum amount the hand can carry is equal to ¼ your Charisma score times 15. When your hand takes damage, you take 1/10th of it rounded down.


#### Improved Hand Creation


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, your technique has improved. This allows you to increase the size of your hand. You may summon your hand as an action for up to your Charisma modifier cursed energy. It still lasts for 1 minute or until you dismiss it, but it is now medium-sized. It still has the same actions as before and is controlled on a bonus action, but now it can grapple large or smaller creatures.

When attempting to grapple an enemy, they must make an Charisma (Athletics) or Charisma (Acrobatics) check against your hand’s athletics check using Charisma instead of Strength. Once a creature is grappled the hand can freely move them around. A creature can try to break free from the grapple at the end of its turns.

You may also attack without a weapon using this bigger hand. The same rules apply as an attack with a weapon, but now you deal your unarmed strike damage and do not need to be holding a weapon.

In addition, the hand has an AC of 10 plus the energy spent and an HP of 10 times the energy spent. It also has a movement of 60 feet now. The carry weight of the hand also increases to ½ your Charisma score times 15.


#### Hand Fling

Extension Technique (S-1)
At 6th level, you have learned how to manipulate creatures better with the hand. You may now grab and fling creatures who are being held by your hand. As a bonus action while your hand is summoned you may spend 3 cursed energy to fling a creature. A grappled creature can be flung up to 30 feet. The creature takes 5d8 bludgeoning upon landing after being thrown. You may also throw willing creatures to up to half the range without taking damage. If you want to throw a willing creature further they will take damage.

This technique’s range and damage increase to 60ft and 7d8 at 11th level, to 90ft and 9d8 at 17th level, and 120ft and 11d8 at 20th level.


#### Expert Hand Improvement


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, your mastery of your technique has grown. Your hand is now large-sized. This allows you to grapple huge or smaller creatures. You can now spend up to twice your Charisma modifier cursed energy when summoning your hand. Its movement is also increased to 90 feet. If you punch using the hand it also now deals two rolls of your unarmed strike damage.

You also gain access to a new attack with your hand. You may now push back enemies and slam them into a surface within range. When your hand is summoned you may use an action for 8 cursed energy to slam a creature. The targeted creature must make a Strength saving throw, or it is pushed taking 12d12 bludgeoning damage. On a success, they can resist the slam and take half as much damage.

This technique's damage increases to 16d12 at 17th level, and to 20d12 at 20th level.

You can also fling an ally or yourself out of the way of danger. When you or an ally is targeted by an attack you can use reaction and 4 cursed energy to fling them or yourself out of harm’s way. You may fling the creature up to the safe range of your hand fling.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Cutie Honey

Extension Technique (S-1)
At 14th level, you have developed an extension technique that allows you to grab enemies differently. You are now able to try and grab the attention of your target. This target has to have been grabbed by your hand before in order to be subject to this effect. As an action for 10 cursed energy, you can force a creature within 90ft to make a Charisma saving throw. On a failure, the creature's eyes turn into hearts and they are forced to look at you leaving them unable to react to anyone's actions besides yours. They are left this way until the beginning of their next turn. On a success, they are not effected.


#### Perfect Hand Improvement


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, you have finally mastered your technique. Your hand will now gain the following benefits.

The hand is now huge-sized. You can now grapple gargantuan-sized creatures.
It has resistance to bludgeoning, slashing, and piercing damage.
A range of 120 feet.
It can deal force damage instead of bludgeoning on its attacks. You choose which damage type.
When attacking it deals 3 rolls of your unarmed strike damage.
The health of your hand is now equal to 20 times the cursed energy spent.
The carry weight of the hand is equal to your Charisma score times 15.
You may control the hand as a free action.

#### Attention Loss


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

After mastering Reverse Cursed Technique, you have unlocked your cursed technique reversal. You are now able to summon small feet which disrupt the focus of creatures. As an action for 4 cursed energy, you may shoot out a bunch of little feet in a 30 foot cone. Creatures in this cone must make a dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they take 5d6 bludgeoning damage and are Confused for 1 minute. On a success, they take half damage and are not Confused. Confused creatures can repeat the save at the end of their turns.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Hakuna Laana</summary>
`GJ2-HHt2-XYAA9-UKI.jpg`
A sorcerer using Hakuna Laana to dodge attacks, Source [63]
Your cursed technique allows you to dance magnificently, the beat etched by your body expelling curses and boosting your physical capabilities. 

#### Hakuna Laana


**Lapse Technique**

At first level you’ve learned to dance to the rhythm etched in your body. As an action for 3 cursed energy you may enter Hakuna Laana for 1 minute, during which you gain advantage on the Strength (Athletics) and Dexterity (Acrobatics) checks, and saving throws using Strength, Dexterity, and Constitution. Additionally while Hakuna Laana is active you gain advantage on any saving throw you make against a cursed technique or a feature which costs cursed energy, ranged cursed energy attack rolls, and melee cursed energy attack rolls against you have disadvantage.

You must maintain concentration to keep Hakuna Laana active, and any effects or conditions that stun, grapple, or otherwise incapacitate or render you incapable of moving, therefore of maintaining the dance, automatically end your concentration on this technique.

At 5th level, you gain proficiency in Charisma (Performance) checks.


#### Curse Expelling Improvement


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level you’ve become more skilled at your usage of Hakuna Laana. Now while within Hakuna Laana you reduce melee cursed energy attack rolls and ranged cursed energy attack rolls against you and reduce DCs of saves you make due to cursed techniques or features which cost cursed energy by half of your Charisma modifier rounded up. Lastly, while Hakuna is active, your Strength, Dexterity, and Constitution scores and their maximums increase by 2.


#### Physical Improvement


**Technique Improvement**

Now at 6th level the increase to your physical capabilities has improved too, making you truly a monster in hand to hand. Now while in Hakuna Laana add half of your Charisma modifier (rounded up) to any ability check, saving throw, attack roll, or damage roll made using Strength, Dexterity, or Constitution.


#### Dancing Improvement


**Technique Improvement**

Now at 10th level you've mastered entering the dance etched into your body. Hakuna Laana now only takes a Bonus Action to activate.


#### Tokyo School Feature: In The Rhythm


**Technique Improvement**

Now at 14th level you’ve expanded upon your technique greatly, deciding to boast more, which you have done to the point that not just you are trapped in your rhythm but your enemies too. When a hostile creature misses an attack against you or you pass a save caused by a hostile creature because of the effects of Hakuna Laana you gain one rhythm counter. You may only have an amount of rhythm counters at once up to your Charisma modifier and all rhythm counters disappear at the end of your turn. Once per turn while in Hakuna Laana before making a melee weapon attack roll, you may gain a bonus to the roll equal to the amount of rythm counters you possess.


#### There Are No Curses


**Technique Improvement**

At level 20, you’ve reached the apex of your technique, a dance which practically erases the cursed. When you activate Hakuna Laana, you may spend an extra 3 Cursed Energy to gain a damage threshold equal to your Jujutsu Sorcerer level. This damage threshold only works against cursed techniques or features which cost cursed energy. If an attack’s damage is ignored because of the threshold it is treated as having missed you, and if a saving throw’s damage is ignored because of the threshold you are treated as having passed the saving throw. Lastly, You now use your full Charisma modifier instead of just half for your Curse Expelling Improvement and Physical Improvement features.


#### The Curse of Moping Around


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

You have mastered Reverse Cursed Technique and therefore gained your cursed technique reversal. Dancing is your whole thing, so your reversal forces you to not be able to dance. However, this makes you incredibly sad. As a bonus action, you can stop using your technique and spend 6 cursed energy to enter this new sad form for 1 minute. During this you have disadvantage on all saving throws, attack rolls are made with disadvantage, and attacks against you have advantage. However, this increased sadness boosts your negative emotions and generates more cursed energy. You regain cursed energy equal to your Charisma modifier at the beginning of your turns. This regen only works if you are in combat where your life is in danger. You can deactivate your reversal early for a free action and you will not gain the cursed energy for that turn.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Cursed Illusions</summary>
`illusions.png`
A illusion made by a cursed illusions user, Source [64]
Your cursed technique allows you to curse people and create illusions. 

#### Cursed Illusions


**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 1st level, you have unlocked your lapse technique. Your technique allows you to curse creatures and make them see other people's faces in a distorted way. You are also able to track those you have cursed. As an action for 3 cursed energy, you can curse a creature. You must select 1 target within 60 feet of yourself. The creature must make a Charisma saving throw. On a failure, the next person they see has a distorted face. Seeing this distortion forces the creature to make an Intelligence saving throw. On a failure, they become confused until the end of their next turn. On a success, they are not affected. After failing, a creature may repeat the saves at the end of their turns if they have 1 level of jujutsu sorcerer or after 1 minute if they do not have any levels, ending the curse on a success. If they pass the first roll, they do not become cursed.

After a creature is cursed, your technique range increases to 400 feet. While a creature is cursed, you know its general location. The cursed creature also keeps seeing the distorted face until they are no longer cursed, but they only need to make the save the first time they see the said creature. A creature may only stop being distorted by either leaving your range or passing the save.

The range increase becomes 500 feet at 5th level, 600 feet at 11th level, 700 feet at 17th level, and to 800 at 20th level.

You may only have a number of people cursed by your lapse at a time equal to your proficiency bonus.


#### Leg Distortion


**Technique Improvement**

At 3rd level, you’ve learned how to distort more of one’s body. Once you have cursed a creature, you can extend the amount of distortion they see. Now, not only is the creature’s face distorted, but also its legs. As a free action for 1 cursed energy, you can make a cursed creature’s legs become distorted. For the remaining duration of your lapse technique, the distorted person does not provoke opportunity attacks from the cursed target.


#### Curse of Tracking


**Extension Technique**

At 6th level, you have advanced your usage of the tracking abilities granted by your technique. As a bonus action for 4 cursed energy, you are able to enhance the tracking abilities of your technique. For the next minute, the range at which you can track someone increases by your proficiency bonus times 100 feet. At the end of this minute, you can spend 1 more cursed energy to extend the duration by another minute.

In addition, you gain more knowledge of your target. When you use this, you now know the general activity the cursed person is performing (determined by DM).


#### Arm Distortion


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, you’ve now learned to distort the arms of creatures. As a bonus action for 3 cursed energy, you can distort the arms of the person. For the remaining duration of your lapse technique, the distorted person makes melee weapon attacks at advantage when attacking the cursed target.


#### Tokyo Feature: Shared Distortions


**Extension Technique**

At 14th level, you have developed a unique way to force other creatures to see the same distortion that your targeted creature sees. As an action for 8 cursed energy, you may force all creatures within 45 feet of the person you cursed with your lapse technique to make Charisma saving throws. On a failure, they see the same illusions caused by your lapse, leg distortion, and arm distortion. On a success, they are unaffected. This lasts for the remaining time your lapse has, and creatures can repeat the saves to end the effect early at the end of their turns.


#### Perfect Illusions


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, your technique has reached its peak. You can now have a number of creatures cursed by your technique equal to 2 times your proficiency bonus. In addition, you know the exact location of anyone cursed by your technique.


#### Illusion Materialization


**Must have the Reverse Cursed Technique cursed feat**

Due to your mastery of Reverse Cursed Technique, you have learned your cursed technique reversal. You are now able to make the distorted faces real. As an action for 8 cursed energy, you can distort a creature's face that is distorted for a cursed creature. The creature must make a Charisma saving throw. On a failure, their face is distorted and they become confused. After failing, a creature may repeat the saves at the end of their turns. On a success, they are not confused.


#### Modulo Techniques


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Chaos and Harmony</summary>
`673d328b98e3bb4ed71eaee7320215c9.jpg`
Maru using his technique for large scale telekinesis,, source [65]
This technique allows the user manipulate the natural laws of the world, using the forces of Chaos and Harmony 
Design Note: This innate technique has what may be the most broad target concept in all of Jujutsu Kaisen, and as such, its capabilities are vast and varied. It is recommended that when using the Extension Techniques Rule, DMs allow far more applications of this technique to be used, even on the fly or in the heat of the moment, to represent the sheer versatility of chaos and harmony. This should not however, be prioritized over game balance.


#### Harmony


**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 1st level, you are able to utilize your technique to help bring people and things related to each other into Harmony, allowing you to find them.

Creatures. You can bring two creatures into Harmony by spending a moment with one, in order to find their equal match by bringing them and the world into Harmony. For 2 cursed energy, you can spend one minute touching a willing creature in order to learn the location of someone they are connected to, such as a biologically related sibling or child. This could also be someone the creature is deeply connected to, such as a lover or very close friend, as determined by the DM. After one minute, you learn the current location of the chosen creature.

Objects. You can use your technique to locate objects related to each other. In order to do this, you must have one object that is part of a greater whole, or strongly related to another object, such as half of a sword or a book. If you have one object with a strong relation to another, you can spend 2 cursed energy while being in contact with the object for one minute. Afterwards, you learn the current location of the related object.


#### Chaos


**Lapse Technique**

At 3rd level, you can use your technique to alter the world around you, allowing you to pull off the most versatile techniques imaginable. You learn to use your technique in the following ways:

Trajectory Change. You can manipulate a projectile to change course and swerve to hit its target. As a bonus action for 2 cursed energy, immediately after making a ranged attack roll, you can cause that attack roll to gain a bonus to hit equal to your proficiency bonus, as the projectile swerves at the last minute while maintaining its velocity.

Water Boil. You can heat water and bring it to a boil by inverting the natural laws of the world. As an action for 6 cursed energy, you can heat a body of water within 60 feet of you that is no more than a 30 foot cube. The water is heated up for one minute, and any creatures or objects within its area must make a Constitution saving throw, taking 6d8 fire damage on a failure and half as much on a success. While the water is boiling, you can use a bonus action for 2 cursed energy to have it burst into water vapor, repeating the saving throw for any creatures within it, dealing half the damage and expelling the creature outward in the direction the water bursts. You can spend additional cursed energy up to your Charisma modifier to increase the amount of water you can boil at once, boiling an additional 5 foot cube for each cursed energy spent.

The damage of this feature increases to 8d8 fire damage at 5th level, 10d8 at 11th level, 14d8 at 17th level, and 16d8 at 20th level.

Water Freeze. You can freeze a body of water by inverting the natural laws of the world. As an action for 6 cursed energy, you can freeze a body of water within 60 feet of you that is no more than a 30 foot cube. The water is frozen, and creatures within the area of the water must make a Constitution saving throw, taking cold damage equal to half of your Charisma score and being restrained for one minute on a failure. On a success, they take half as much damage and are not restrained.

At the end of their turns, the creatures may make a Strength saving throw to escape the condition early on a success.

You can spend additional cursed energy up to your Charisma modifier to increase the amount of water you can freeze at once, freezing an additional 5 foot cube for each cursed energy spent.

Inflated Ball Burst. You can cause inflated and pressurized objects to burst. As a bonus action for 3 cursed energy, you can cause an inflated or nonmagical pressurized object of tiny or small size to burst, forcing all creatures in a 5 foot radius of it to make a Dexterity saving throw, taking 3d10 force damage on a failure, and half as much on a success. You can spend additional cursed energy up to your Charisma modifier, to burst an additional item per cursed energy spent as part of the same action. Creatures within the radius of multiple saving throws instead take an additional die of damage for each item after the first.

You can spend cursed energy up to your Charisma modifier plus half of your Charisma modifier at 5th level, this becomes twice your Charisma modifier at 11th level, twice your Charisma modifier plus half of your Charisma modifier at 17th level, and three times your Charisma modifier at 20th level.

Rampant Roots. You can cause plant life to spiral out of control and manipulate it. As an action for 6 cursed energy, roots sprout from the ground and can attack a creature within 30 feet of you. Make 3 ranged cursed energy attack rolls at creatures in range. On a hit, they deal 2d10 piercing damage each.

The damage increases to 3d10 at 11th level, 4d10 at 17th level, and 5d10 at 20th level.

Pathway of Roots. You can cause plant life to spiral out of control and follow your direction. As a bonus action for 3 cursed energy, you can create roots that spread out in a large area around yourself. You create a 100 foot radius area of roots around you. These roots can handle up to your Charisma modifier (minimum of 1) times 100 pounds. They last for a number of minutes equal to your charisma modifier (minimum of 1) or until you dispel it at will (no action required) and have a number of hit points equal to your Charisma modifier times 5.

At 11th level, the radius increases to 200 feet, at 17th level the radius increases to 300 feet, and at 20th level the radius increases to 400 feet.


#### Telekinesis


**Technique Improvement**

At 6th level, you have gained a greater aspect of your control over gravity and motion. You can now manipulate objects and adversaries.

You have gained the basic application of your technique on gravity. As a free action for 2 cursed energy, you can give yourself a flying speed equal to your walking speed for one minute.

Telekinesis. As an action for an amount of cursed energy depending on the size, you can choose an object or creature within 60 ft., moving it a number of feet equal to your proficiency bonus times 10. If it collides with an object, they both take 1d12 bludgeoning damage. If it collides with a creature or structure, they both take Collision Damage based on the object's size, making a Dexterity saving throw, taking half as much damage on a success. Any adjustments used to enhance Telekinesis, besides effects that increase your damage dice, also apply to this (including range increases and Cursed Energy cost reductions).

| Object Size | Cursed Energy Cost | Collision Damage |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Tiny | 1 Cursed Energy | 1d4 + your Charisma modifier bludgeoning damage. |
| Small | 2 Cursed Energy | 2d6 + your Charisma modifier bludgeoning damage. |
| Medium | 3 Cursed Energy | 2d8 + your Charisma modifier added twice bludgeoning damage. |
| Large | 5 Cursed Energy | 2d10 + Charisma modifier added twice bludgeoning damage. |
| Huge | 8 Cursed Energy | 2d12 + Charisma modifier added three times bludgeoning damage. |
| Gargantuan | 10 Cursed Energy | 4d12 + Charisma modifier added three times bludgeoning damage. |
Depending on the nature of the object or structure you are moving, your DM may allow you to rip things planted or rooted to something, such as pulling a specific portion of a bridge.

Moving a Creature. You can pull a creature using the chart above for its size. Make a contested check against the creature, with you using your Cursed Energy Modifier, and the creature using Strength. If you succeed, the creature is pulled up to the normal range. On a failure, they are not moved. A creature can choose to fail this check.

A creature counts as their size for the purposes of Collision Damage.

You can use this feature out of combat to freely manipulate tiny objects at no cursed energy cost, as well as an aura around you. As a free action for 1 cursed energy, you can begin controlling gravity in a radius in feet around you equal to your proficiency bonus times 10, for one minute. Within this radius, all nonmagical objects that aren’t being worn or carried begin harmlessly floating upward, up to ten feet in the air and oscillating there for the duration.

World Inversion. At 11th level, you can invert the world for a creature and yourself, causing extreme disorientation. As an action for 7 cursed energy, choose one creature within 60 feet, forcing it to make a Charisma saving throw, on a success, they are unaffected. On a failure, you and the creature have the direction of gravity shifted in a direction of your choice (such as in one direction sideways or directly inverted upside down) for one minute. For the duration, both you and the creature have your gravity changed to flow in that direction, as well as any nonmagical objects you or the creature interact with or wear. The creature may repeat its saving throw at the end of its turn, ending the effect on a success. This feature ends if you are ever more than 100 feet away from the creature.

Multi-Telekinesis. At 17th level, when using your Telekinesis, you may instead use a Full Turn Action to cast Telekinesis on multiple objects at once, sending a volley of attacks at your enemy. You may use Telekinesis on an additional number of objects equal to your level, spending 2 cursed energy for each additional item. If you attack the same target with more than 1 object, each additional object after the first adds one die of damage based on its size, to the initial saving throw.


#### Cognitive Chaos


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, you can use your control of Chaos in the most unique way possible, trying to trigger a state of mental chaos and disorientation against an adversary. As a bonus action for 8 cursed energy, you can force a creature within 60 feet to make a Wisdom saving throw. On a failure, they become Confused until the end of their next turn. If they fail by 5 or more, they become incapacitated instead. On a success, they are unaffected. Taking damage while under the conditions from this feature ends the condition.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Advanced Harmony


**Technique Improvement**

At 14th level, you’ve uncovered some extremely handy ways to utilize your technique, allowing you to use harmony to speed up difficult tasks:

Linguistic Harmony. You can speed up the amount of time needed to learn to speak a language. As a free action for 5 cursed energy, you can harmonize the ability to speak to someone as you begin to learn their language. You learn any immediate words that are needed to communicate in your current situation. You also have advantage in any checks to learn more of the language and learn in half the normal amount of time. This effect also spreads to those within a 100 foot radius of you and lasts for 8 hours.

Literary Harmony. You can instantly bring yourself into harmony with information in a written form that you have contact with. When holding a piece of written information, like a book or scroll, you may use an action and 5 cursed energy to instantly learn the contents of the written information.


#### New World Order


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, your power and connection to Harmony can bring you to new heights. Your technique can lead you to Harmony with your goal:

During a short or long rest, you can spend 20 cursed energy to have your technique try to bring you guidance on how to achieve a goal. Choose a meaningful goal that is important to you, such as attempting to achieve peace between peoples or harmony in a region.

The way you are directed to achieve the goal is determined by the DM. It can be anything from you learning of a person who can assist you to gaining vague information on how to achieve the goal. Once this feature is used, you cannot use it again for 30 days.

This feature can be heavily enhanced with the use of Mul. It can bolster its power, granting you wide-reaching effects and facilitating your desired harmony, such as by allowing you to traverse the realms that souls walk, bolster the presence of fading souls, and even transport willing creatures across great distances.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Costume Séance</summary>
`Osada-smelling-blood.png`
A man using this technique to become a shark, source [66]
This technique transforms you into whatever costume you wear. 

#### Multifaceted

This Innate Technique is quite limiting in its capabilities. As such, Costume Séance only provides its Lapse Technique as a feature. After level one, a character should then gain a Sorcerer Path, which they will level in as they progress for each level after the first. For example, a 20th level character would possess one level in Costume Séance, and 19 levels in the Sorcerer Path. If you reach Epic Levels, then as normal, the Subclasses will not continue to level past 20.


#### Costume Séance


**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 1st level, you are able to use costumes to become the creature you are wearing. As a bonus action, you are able to become the creature of the costume you are wearing. The cursed energy cost of this feature is equal to the CR of the costume. You may only become a creature that has a CR equal to or lower than your level and the creature must be a non-humanoid creature. This transformation lasts for 1 minute or until you hit 0 hit points. For the duration, your Strength, Dexterity, and Constitution scores are replaced by the scores of the costume. You maintain your alignment and personality. You assume the hit points of your new form. When you revert to your normal form, you return to the number of hit points it had before it transformed. If it reverts as a result of dropping to 0 hit points, any excess damage carries over to your normal form. As long as the excess damage doesn't reduce your normal form to 0 hit points, you are not knocked unconscious. You gain the actions of the creature you've become; however, you can still do all of your normal actions as long as the body allows it. You also gain the features of the creature.

Additionally, your transformation slightly alters how some features from the costume would be used. For example, if you transform into a shark and are not in water, you might be able to swim in the air. The ways that these features would be used are determined by your DM.


</details>
<details markdown="1"><summary>Marmalade Boy</summary>
`img-2026-03-29-03-15-37.png`

#### A curse user and his guardian spirit

This technique allows the user's guardian spirit to create thread out of human skin. 

#### Guardian Spirit

An integral part of your technique is your guardian spirit. You gain the Cursed Partner Background Feat, with your spirit possessing no innate technique. Your spirit may replace attacks within the multiattack action with the needle created by your technique, with which it is proficient. You may also choose for your spirit to possess Cursed Weapon Enhancement rather than Cursed Strikes. This choice is permanent.


#### Marmalade Boy


**Lapse Technique**

Starting at 1st level, you have awakened your technique. It allows your guardian spirit to sew new appearances over creatures using human skin. You are able to command your guardian spirit to use this feature. When you do so, the spirit must use its action to make a new appearance over a creature within its reach. For it to do so, the spirit must be in possession of enough human skin to make the new appearance. The amount of skin needed is determined by your DM. Creatures must make a Wisdom (Perception) check against your cursed energy DC to know that it is a disguise.

This new appearance lasts until the creature takes an amount of damage equal to your level or until they use a bonus action to tear it off.


#### Sewing Needle


**Lapse Technique**

At 3rd level, you are able to summon large sewing needles to use as weapons. As a bonus action for 2 cursed energy, you create a large sewing needle cursed tool. It has the finesse property, and you may use your Charisma modifier for its attack and damage rolls. On a hit, it deals 1d4 + your Charisma modifier piercing damage. Additionally, it counts as a grade 4 cursed tool. This needle lasts for one minute, or until combat ends if in initiative. You may have a number of these needles active at once equal to your Charisma modifier.

Additionally, your guardian spirit may use its cursed energy to summon a needle instead of you doing it. Any needles they summon also count towards the limit.

At 5th level, the needle now uses your martial arts die for damage rolls and counts as a Grade 3 cursed tool. At 11th level, it counts as a Grade 2 cursed tool. At 17th level, it counts as a Grade 1 cursed tool. At 20th level, it counts as a Special Grade cursed tool.


#### Some Strings Attached


**Extension Technique**

At 6th level, you have learned how to use your needles alongside some string in order to perform various moves during combat. When you summon a sewing needle, you can choose for it to have a string attached. Additionally, you can use a free action while within reach of your spirit to attach a string to a needle. While a string is attached, you gain the following maneuvers:

String Pull. Once per round in place of an attack in the attack action, you may make a Dexterity (Sleight of Hand) check against a target within reach. The target must make an Dexterity (Acrobatics) check. If you roll higher than your target, you can trip them, and they fall prone.

String Trap. As a bonus action, you can set a point for the trap to begin. You may then move to a point within 30 feet and set another point to finish the trap. A string now runs across the 2 points, making a trap. Whenever a creature with a passive perception lower than your cursed energy DC moves across the area where your string lies, they must make a Dexterity saving throw. On a failure, they trip and fall prone. On a success, they are unaffected.

String Reel. Instead of tripping a creature, you may instead pull them towards your spirit. Once per round in place of an attack in the attack action, you may make a Dexterity (Sleight of Hand0 check against a target within reach. The target must make an Strength (Athletics) check. If you roll higher than your target, you can pull them, and they are moved a number of feet closer to your spirit equal to 5 times your Strength modifier.

Additionally, all of these maneuvers may also be used by your guardian spirit.


#### Skin Suit Stuffing


**Technique Improvement**

At 10th level, you have learned a new, unique way for your spirit to interact with the new appearances it creates. You are now able to have it fill the suit with part of its body, so that it can move and act. When your spirit makes a disguise, it can instead fill the disguise with itself instead of putting it over that creature. When done in this way, the suit moves with your spirit as if it were attached to it and acts as if it were a normal person, but it cannot speak. Additionally, if targeted, it has the same armor class as your spirit and the same hit points as a normal appearance. A creature can make a Wisdom (Perception) check against your cursed energy DC to determine that it is not a real person.

Additionally, you are able to hide under your spirit. As a bonus action while next to your spirit, you may move under it. If no one saw you move, then you are hidden, and creatures must make a perception check against your cursed energy DC to know that you are there. You may exit from your spirit as a bonus action.


#### Tokyo School Feature: Sewing Pins


**Extension Technique**

At 14th level, you have learned how to use another aspect of your technique. You can summon a new cursed tool that resembles a sewing pin. You can throw these pins at creatures. As a bonus action for 4 cursed energy, you create 4 of these pins. These pins have a range of 30/60, the thrown property, and deal your martial arts die in damage. They also count as Grade 2 cursed tools. You may use your Charisma modifier for its attack and damage rolls

At 17th level, it counts as a Grade 1 cursed tool. At 20th level, it counts as a Special Grade cursed tool.


#### Perfected Disguise


**Technique Improvement**

At 20th level, the new appearances your technique creates are indistinguishable from the real thing. Whenever a creature makes a Wisdom (Perception) check to try to see if you are disguised or to see if your spirit is inhabiting a disguise, they make that check at disadvantage.

■■■ and Light
`jujutsu-kaisen-jjk.gif`
Dabura using his technique, source [67]
