You are still able to utilize Idle Transfiguration.


#### Self Alternated Tendrils

Prerequisites: Idle Transfiguration, Maximized Destruction Vow

Due to your vow preventing an alteration of the body, you’ve imposed a separate vow on your tendrils to still be able to effectively transfigure them. A separate vow on the tendrils has been made that shortens the metamorphosis cycle, allowing them to count as a limb slot for the purpose of self-alteration’s weapon and shape alterations. Alterations made to the tendrils in this manner only last until the beginning of your next turn.

106,000 IQ Play
Prerequisites: Boogie Woogie innate technique, must be attuned to a Prosthetic Vibraslap

You’ve made a binding vow turning something you thought would kill your technique, the loss of a hand, into a blessing in disguise. When you use Boogie Woogie as a reaction you may spend an amount of additional reactions to empower the swap. For each additional reaction your swapping range increases by 30 feet and for every 5 additional reactions you may choose one additional target for the swap. When swapping more than two targets you choose which are sent to which positions.


#### Preheated Furnace

Prerequisites: Shrine innate technique, Furnace: Open feature

You’ve crafted a vow to overcome your furnace’s greatest weaknesses. Outside of your domain, Furnace: Open is now an action and a ranged cursed energy attack roll against one creature within 20 times the amount spent feet. Inside your domain it now targets all creatures within your domain except yourself, and deals its damage in bludgeoning damage as a second instance of damage to any creature it hit as long as the domain its used within has a barrier that stops at least non-living things from leaving. This bludgeoning damage ignores resistance and kills a creature outright if they’re reduced to 0 hit points by it.


#### Limited Copy

Prerequisites: Vengeful Cursed Spirit innate technique, Copy feature

When you would copy a creature who is higher level or CR than yourself, you can make them count as lower by giving up the number of times you can use the technique. When you use this vow and copy, you can treat all creatures as equal to your level or CR, but you may only use the copied technique a number of times equal to your Charisma modifier. You are still subject to all the normal rules of copy when using this vow such as healing making your copied technique not work and not being able to copy certain features. You can choose not to use this vow when attempting to copy a cursed technique.


#### Soul Dismantle

Prerequisites: Shrine innate technique, 81 or more Soul Knowledge

When you make a dismantle, its range is reduced to touch and you must make a melee weapon attack roll while using it. If you do this the dismantle now makes every attack you hit force the trapped soul to make the Charisma saving throw for Soul Separation. You no longer have to roll 5 or more over the vessel’s AC.

Design Note: This vow can be customized to work with other cursed techniques. Work with your GM to make a variation of it that allows other techniques to hit the soul.


#### Resonance Conduit

Prerequisites: Straw Doll Technique innate technique, Resonance feature

You are now able to transfer all the Soul Harming damage of Resonance from a Cursed Object to the incarnated creature that the cursed object belongs to. In order to do this you lose the ability to harm the Cursed Object in any manner while using Resonance.


#### Final Gimmick

Prerequisites: Contruction Technique, must be at least 11th level

Your technique is able to create almost any grade of tool, allowing for quite a lot of interesting applications. What it cannot create however is special cursed tools thus you've made a vow where you give your essence to have them created.

When you use Cursed Construction to make Grade 1 Cursed Tools, you may choose to spend your vitality to imbue it with a special effect. For every cursed energy spent on the cursed tool, after reductions, you take 2d12 necrotic damage that bypasses, immunities, reductions, thresholds and temporary hit points. If a cost is increased by Cursed Energy Deficiency, the damage gets increased accordingly. In exchange, you may imbue a special effect/ability in the Cursed Tool that you must determine with your DM. You may not imbue the effects of Special Grade Cursed Tools into lower graded cursed tools in any way, shape or form.

You may also use Cursed Construction to make Special Grade Cursed Tools, however for every cursed energy spent this way BEFORE cost reductions, you take 6d12 necrotic damage that bypasses resistances, immunities, reductions, thresholds and temporary hit points. When you take this damage, you also reduce your maximum hit points by an equal amount. Any cost increased by the cursed energy deficiency feature counts towards the damage for this vow. You cannot make the Ring of Yuta Okkotsu.

You regain your lost maximum hit points over long rests. For every long rest you take, you regain your Constitution modifier (minimum of 1) amount of maximum hit points. You're unable to create another special grade cursed tool as long as you're still recovering any maximum hit points you've lost.


#### Multiple Sorcerer Binding Vows

By forging a vow with others, sorcerers can share risks and rewards, creating powerful bonds that are above individual strength. These vows might involve shared techniques, conditional alliances, or mutual restrictions, and the consequences for breaking them are often severe for breaker. 

#### Master and Servant

Requires two or more different Jujutsu Sorcerers.
You have a deep admiration and respect for someone, so much so that you can restrain yourself just to obey them. One of the members of the vow will be the "master" of the vow, while another will be the "servant". The servant will receive one of the following restrictions:

Their Cursed Energy' maximum will be reduced by a number decided by its master.
Their technique's range is reduced by a number of ft. that the master decides.(Cannot go lower than 5ft.)
Their technique's damage is reduced by how many damage dice the master decides.(Cannot go lower than one dice.)
They cannot use any barrier techniques.
The servant will have this restraint until the master allows the servant to unleash its cursed energy as an action. The servant gains one the following benefits depending on his restraint:

Their cursed energy maximum is reset to the normal maximum and increases by the amount that was lost (counting the original value without the reduction), and they regain cursed energy equal to the cursed energy they lost.
Their technique's range is reset to the normal range and increases by the amount that was lost (counting the original value without the reduction).
Their technique's damage dice is reset to the normal amount and increases by the amount that was lost (counting the original value without the reduction).
This lasts for a number of rounds equal to the master's proficiency bonus, after which this vow can only take place after a long rest.


#### Enchain

Must be made between a Living Vessel and their Cursed Object.
Your cursed object has something you want, but they won't give it to you without a price, so they instead propose a vow of mutual interest. When you accept this vow, the cursed object will do something you want that they're capable of, such as reviving you from certain death, however you will forget all about this vow and ever making it. Additionally, the next time the cursed object chants "Enchain", you freely relinquish control of your body to them for 1 minute. During this minute, the cursed object cannot harm or kill any creatures, or cause any actions that would directly lead to harm (such as shoving another creature off of a cliff or ledge). Once this minute is over, you regain control of your body and this vow is lifted for both you and the cursed object with no ill effects.

</details>
